# Native Unreal Tools

!!! note "Official - Unreal Engine 5.8 and later"
    The actions on this page wrap Unreal's native AI Toolset Registry (the plugin behind Unreal's own MCP server). Each one is a declared ue-mcp action inside the matching category: it states what it does to the editor, declares its own parameters, and goes through the same guards, locks and dispatch as every other action here. Pass a tool's arguments as ordinary top-level parameters, or as `input` when you prefer. Requires UE 5.8+ with the `ToolsetRegistry` plugin (and the toolset plugins you want) enabled. The `epic` category discovers the live registry (`status` / `list_toolsets` / `describe_toolset` / `call_tool`).

ue-mcp currently wraps **2171 official tools** across **137 toolsets**, grouped below by the ue-mcp category they surface in. Domains ue-mcp has no native handlers for still get their own category (`dataflow`, `conversation`); only the registry's own meta-tooling lives under `epic`.

## Engine availability

The list is the union of every recorded engine's registry. A tool that is not on every engine is marked with the engines it is on; calling it on a recorded engine without it returns `unsupported_engine_version` naming the engines that have it.

| Engine | Toolsets | Tools | Only on this engine |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UE 5.8 | 52 | 830 | 549 |
| UE 5.9 | 109 | 1914 | 1633 |

## project

Wraps 31 official tools.

- `project(epic_compile_live_coding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LiveCodingToolset.LiveCodingToolset] Triggers a Live Coding compile and returns a pending result that resolves once the compile finishes. Live Coding must be enabled in Editor Preferences. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `project(epic_create_directory)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Creates a directory and any missing parents. A directory that already exists is a success. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path
- `project(epic_delete_file)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Deletes one file. Directories are rejected. Recursive deletion is unavailable. Source-controlled files are marked for deletion when source control is available. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path
- `project(epic_discover_tests)` - [Epic AutomationTestToolset.AutomationTestToolset] Initialize automation worker discovery and load the test list. Must be called once before ListTests or RunTests. Takes several seconds as it discovers the local automation worker and enumerates all registered tests. Returns an async result that completes with true when tests are available, or an error if discovery fails. Params: bForceRediscover?
- `project(epic_download_url)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Downloads an HTTPS URL to a file. Redirects are not followed. Responses above 1 GiB are rejected. Local, private, and selected special-use addresses are rejected. Only HTTP 200 responses succeed. Host resolution and the HTTP request each time out after 30 seconds. Existing read-only files are checked out through source control before replacement. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: url, destinationPath, bOverwrite
- `project(epic_edit_text_by_anchor)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Edits the unique occurrence of a piece of text. Source files and results above 1 MiB are rejected. Big-endian UTF-16 files are rejected. Existing read-only files are checked out through source control before replacement. AnchorText is matched exactly first. If that fails, LF and CRLF variants are tried. NewText uses the file's existing line ending. Replace changes the anchor text itself. InsertBefore and InsertAfter insert whole lines and preserve the anchor. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, anchorText, newText, editMode
- `project(epic_edit_text_by_line_range)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Edits an inclusive range of one-based lines. Source files and results above 1 MiB are rejected. Big-endian UTF-16 files are rejected. Existing read-only files are checked out through source control before replacement. Lines outside the range remain unchanged, including their line endings. NewText uses the file's existing line ending. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, firstLine, lastLine, newText, editMode
- `project(epic_enable_live_coding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LiveCodingToolset.LiveCodingToolset] Call before CompileLiveCoding on a session launched with Live Coding off; starts the Broker/Agent. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `project(epic_get_section_property_values)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Returns the current values of the specified properties as a JSON object. Params: containerName, categoryName, sectionName, propertyNames
- `project(epic_get_section_schema)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Returns a JSON Schema describing the user-visible properties of a settings section. The schema maps each property name to its type, description, and constraints. A section that uses a custom widget has no settings object and so no schema. Params: containerName, categoryName, sectionName
- `project(epic_get_supported_platforms)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Returns the platforms the project declares support for in its .uproject file. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `project(epic_get_test_results)` - [Epic AutomationTestToolset.AutomationTestToolset] Get detailed results for the current or most recent test run. Requires DiscoverTests() to have completed. Params: none
- `project(epic_get_test_status)` - [Epic AutomationTestToolset.AutomationTestToolset] Get a lightweight status snapshot of the automation controller. Requires DiscoverTests() to have completed. Params: none
- `project(epic_is_live_coding_ready)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LiveCodingToolset.LiveCodingToolset] Poll before calling CompileLiveCoding to avoid blocking on the Broker's initial module load. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `project(epic_list_available_platforms)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Lists the platforms a project can declare support for, the choices in the Project Settings Supported Platforms section. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `project(epic_list_categories)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Lists the names of all categories within a settings container, sorted alphabetically. Params: containerName
- `project(epic_list_containers)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Lists the names of all known settings containers, sorted alphabetically. Common containers are "Editor" and "Project". Params: none
- `project(epic_list_directory)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Lists a range from the immediate contents of a directory. Directories with more than 100000 immediate entries are rejected before filtering or range selection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, firstEntry?, maximumEntries?
- `project(epic_list_sections)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Lists the names of all sections within a settings category, sorted alphabetically. Params: containerName, categoryName
- `project(epic_list_tests)` - [Epic AutomationTestToolset.AutomationTestToolset] List available automation tests. Requires DiscoverTests() to have completed. Params: nameFilter?, tagFilter?, limit?
- `project(epic_read_text_file)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Reads lines or fragments without line numbers, budgeting the complete JSON response for EDA. Decode Contents once as JSON when ContentsEncoding is "json-string" to recover NUL and U+FFFE/U+FFFF without transport substitution. Oversized lines are returned in character-aligned fragments. Pass NextCursor to continue without losing text. Supports UTF-8 and byte-order-marked UTF-16. Legacy single-byte text is not supported on all platforms. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, firstLine?, maximumLines?, continuationCursor?, maximumResponseCharacters?
- `project(epic_reset_section_to_defaults)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Resets the settings in a section to their default values. Project sections saved to a Default*.ini cannot be reset, matching the Settings editor. Params: containerName, categoryName, sectionName
- `project(epic_resolve_path)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Resolves a native path, Unreal virtual-content path, or plugin path that the plugin manager finds to an absolute native path. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path
- `project(epic_run_tests)` - [Epic AutomationTestToolset.AutomationTestToolset] Run a set of automation tests by name. Requires DiscoverTests() to have completed. Starts executing the specified tests and returns an async result that completes with a summary when all tests finish. Params: testNames
- `project(epic_run_tests_by_filter)` - [Epic AutomationTestToolset.AutomationTestToolset] Run automation tests selected by a filter expression. Requires DiscoverTests() to have completed. Much faster than RunTests when targeting a large batch because the engine narrows the report tree in a single pass instead of running a per-leaf membership check against the requested name list. Filter syntax (multiple expressions joined by '+'): "StartsWith:System.Engine" prefix match against the full test path "^Foo" prefix anchor (equivalent to StartsWith:) "Bar$" suffix anchor "Substring" bare token matches anywhere in the path "Group:Smoke" expand a named group from AutomationControllerSettings ini Groups Returns an async result that completes with the same summary shape as RunTests. Params: filterExpression
- `project(epic_save_section)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Saves the settings in a section. The default config file is checked out first when source control is enabled. Params: containerName, categoryName, sectionName
- `project(epic_search_settings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Finds settings by the label shown in the Settings editor, the property name, the tooltip, or the console variable the property is bound to, across every container. Every word in the query must match, ignoring case. A section whose own name matches is returned with an empty PropertyName. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: query
- `project(epic_set_section_properties)` - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Sets one or more properties on a settings section from a JSON object and saves. PropertiesJson must be a JSON object mapping property names to new values, in the same format returned by GetSectionPropertyValues. If any value is rejected, none are applied and nothing is saved. The default config file is checked out first when source control is enabled. Params: containerName, categoryName, sectionName, propertiesJson
- `project(epic_set_supported_platforms)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConfigSettingsToolset.ConfigSettingsToolset] Replaces the platforms the project declares support for and saves the .uproject file, checking it out first when source control is enabled. Packaging, running or cooking for a platform not in the list warns. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: platforms
- `project(epic_stop_tests)` - [Epic AutomationTestToolset.AutomationTestToolset] Stop all currently running tests. Requires DiscoverTests() to have completed. If a RunTests async result is pending, it will be completed with an error. Params: none
- `project(epic_write_text_file)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic FileSystemToolset.FileSystemToolset] Writes a complete UTF-8 text file and creates missing parent directories. Existing read-only files are checked out through source control before replacement. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, contents, bOverwrite

## asset

Wraps 165 official tools.

- `asset(epic_add_enumerator)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Appends a new enumerator to an enum. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: enum, displayName
- `asset(epic_add_gradient)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Appends a gradient to an atlas as a new final row. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: atlas, gradient
- `asset(epic_add_input_param)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Adds an input-parameter node (a graph-level parameter such as Scalar, Color, or Vector) and returns it. The parameter is exposed on the graph automatically. If ParamName is set it becomes the parameter's name, uniquified against the graph's existing parameters. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, expressionClass, paramName, posX?, posY?
- `asset(epic_add_key)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Adds a key to a row. Params: curve_table, row_name, key. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.AddKey (Params: curveTable, rowName, key).
- `asset(epic_add_node)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Adds a node of the given expression type to the graph and returns the new node. Input-parameter and Output expression types are not accepted here; they are created with the dedicated input-parameter and output tools. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, expressionClass, posX?, posY?
- `asset(epic_add_object_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Adds a UObject-reference variable, applying all fields of Spec atomically. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, objectClass, spec
- `asset(epic_add_output)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Adds an Output node and returns it. The output is exposed on the graph automatically. If OutputName is set it becomes the output's name (and its export filename), uniquified against the graph's existing outputs. Configure the output further with the output-settings tool. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, outputName, posX?, posY?
- `asset(epic_add_row)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Adds a new row to the curve table with an optional default value. Params: curve_table, row_name, default_value?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.AddRow (Params: curveTable, rowName, defaultValue?).
- `asset(epic_add_rows)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Adds new rows with default values to the data table. Params: data_table, row_names. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.AddRows (Params: dataTable, rowNames).
- `asset(epic_add_struct_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Adds a variable of an arbitrary script-struct type, applying all fields of Spec atomically. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, structType, spec
- `asset(epic_add_user_asset_tags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Adds the given User Asset Tags to an asset. Pass tag names without the UAT. prefix; the prefix is added internally. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, tags
- `asset(epic_add_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Adds a variable of a primitive or basic struct type, applying all fields of Spec atomically. Supported TypeName values (case-insensitive): bool, int, int64, byte, float, double, name, string, text, Vector, Rotator, Transform, Vector2D, LinearColor. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, typeName, spec
- `asset(epic_append_box)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic geometry_toolset.geometry_ops.GeometryTools] Appends a box of the given dimensions to the component's mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: component, transform, dimensions
- `asset(epic_append_cone)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic geometry_toolset.geometry_ops.GeometryTools] Appends a cone (or truncated cone) to the component's mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: component, transform, base_radius, top_radius, height
- `asset(epic_append_cylinder)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic geometry_toolset.geometry_ops.GeometryTools] Appends a cylinder of the given radius and height to the component's mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: component, transform, radius, height
- `asset(epic_append_disc)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic geometry_toolset.geometry_ops.GeometryTools] Appends a flat disc to the component's mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: component, transform, radius
- `asset(epic_append_sphere)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic geometry_toolset.geometry_ops.GeometryTools] Appends a sphere of the given radius to the component's mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: component, transform, radius
- `asset(epic_append_torus)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic geometry_toolset.geometry_ops.GeometryTools] Appends a torus to the component's mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: component, transform, major_radius, minor_radius
- `asset(epic_assign_clothing_to_section)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ChaosClothAssetToolset.ChaosClothAssetToolset] Binds a clothing asset to a specific LOD and section on the skeletal mesh. Mirrors the SkeletalMesh Editor's "Apply Clothing Data" action. Any clothing already bound to the section is unbound first. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: skeletalMeshPath, clothingAssetName, lodIndex, sectionIndex, clothingLodIndex
- `asset(epic_can_edit_asset)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Checks whether an asset can be edited. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: asset_path
- `asset(epic_connect_pins)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Connects an output pin on FromNode to an input pin on ToNode. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, fromNode, fromPin, toNode, toPin
- `asset(epic_convert_clothing_asset_common_to_chaos_cloth_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ChaosClothAssetToolset.ChaosClothAssetToolset] Converts a legacy UClothingAssetCommon (the cloth asset created via UChaosClothingSimulationFactory) into a new UChaosClothAsset whose embedded Dataflow graph mirrors the legacy UChaosClothConfig and UChaosClothSharedSimConfig values, with one WeightMap node per legacy weight map. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: skeletalMeshPath, clothingAssetName, outputPackagePath, assetName
- `asset(epic_create)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Creates a new CurveTable asset. Params: folder_path, asset_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.Create (Params: folderPath, assetName).
- `asset(epic_create__data_asset_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_asset.DataAssetTools] Creates a new DataAsset asset in the project. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: folder_path, asset_name, asset_type
- `asset(epic_create__data_asset_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.DataAssetToolset] Creates a new DataAsset asset in the project. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName, assetType
- `asset(epic_create__data_table_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Creates a new DataTable asset with the specified column schema. Params: folder_path, asset_name, schema. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.Create (Params: folderPath, assetName, schema).
- `asset(epic_create__string_table_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Creates a new StringTable asset. Params: folder_path, asset_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.Create (Params: folderPath, assetName).
- `asset(epic_create__user_defined_enum_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Creates a new user-defined enum asset with no enumerators. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName
- `asset(epic_create__user_defined_struct_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Creates a new user-defined struct asset. The new struct starts with one default bool variable (engine convention). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName
- `asset(epic_create_atlas)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Creates a new curve atlas asset containing no gradients. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName, textureSize?
- `asset(epic_create_clothing_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ChaosClothAssetToolset.ChaosClothAssetToolset] Creates a UChaosClothAssetSKMClothingAsset from a Chaos Cloth/Outfit Asset and adds it to the skeletal mesh's clothing asset array, making it available to assign to sections. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: skeletalMeshPath, chaosClothAssetPath
- `asset(epic_create_folder)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Creates a folder at the specified path. Params: path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.CreateFolder (Params: path).
- `asset(epic_create_gradient)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Creates a new linear color gradient asset, seeded with an opaque black-to-white ramp. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName
- `asset(epic_create_texture_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Creates a new empty TextureGraph asset. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName
- `asset(epic_delete)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Deletes an asset or folder. Params: path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.Delete (Params: path).
- `asset(epic_diff)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two CurveTable assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `asset(epic_diff__data_asset_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.DataAssetToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two DataAsset assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `asset(epic_diff__data_table_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.DataTableToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two DataTable assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `asset(epic_diff__string_table_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StringTableToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two StringTable assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `asset(epic_disconnect_pins)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Removes the edge between the specified pins. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, fromNode, fromPin, toNode, toPin
- `asset(epic_duplicate)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Makes a copy of a folder or asset. Params: path, new_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.Duplicate (Params: path, newPath).
- `asset(epic_exists)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Determines if a folder or asset exists. Params: path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.Exists (Params: path).
- `asset(epic_export_png)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.TextureToolset] Exports a Texture to a PNG file on disk. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: texture, filePath
- `asset(epic_export_texture_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Exports every texture output node to a Texture2D asset in the given content folder. Triggers a full graph evaluation internally before writing assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, outputFolder
- `asset(epic_find_assets)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Searches the project for assets that match specific criteria. Params: folder_path, name, asset_type?, recursive?, tags?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.FindAssets (Params: filter?).
- `asset(epic_find_similar)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic SemanticSearchToolset.SemanticSearchToolset] Find assets whose embeddings are semantically similar to the given asset's embedding. Vector-only (no BM25). The source asset must already be indexed by the SemanticSearch plugin. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: assetPath, classFilter, pathRegexes, k?
- `asset(epic_fix_up_redirectors)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Fixes up object redirectors at or under a path, rewiring the assets that reference them to point at each redirector's target. A folder also fixes up every other redirector under it, including ones a rename did not create - pass the specific asset path instead when only cleaning up after a move or rename. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, bDeleteRedirectors?
- `asset(epic_generate_convex_collisions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Generates convex hull collision shapes for a static mesh. Convex hulls provide accurate collision for physics simulation. More hulls improve accuracy but increase runtime cost. Replaces any existing collision. Params: mesh, hull_count?, max_hull_verts?, hull_precision?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GenerateConvexCollisions (Params: mesh, hullCount?, maxHullVerts?, hullPrecision?).
- `asset(epic_generate_lods)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Auto-generates LODs for a static mesh using triangle reduction. Each entry in triangle_percents creates one additional LOD. The value is the fraction of triangles to keep relative to LOD 0, from just above 0.0 (nearly empty) to 1.0 (full detail). For example, [0.5, 0.25] creates LOD1 with 50% of the original triangles and LOD2 with 25%. Params: mesh, triangle_percents. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GenerateLods (Params: mesh, trianglePercents).
- `asset(epic_get_alpha_stops)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Returns the alpha stops of a gradient in time order. Only keys with derived tangents form stops; GetChannelKeys reads any other gradient. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: gradient
- `asset(epic_get_asset_class)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Gets the class of an asset. Params: asset_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.GetAssetClass (Params: assetPath).
- `asset(epic_get_asset_content_roots)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Returns the known content-browser roots in the current project. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: contains?, bWritable?
- `asset(epic_get_asset_tags)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Gets the asset registry tags for an asset. Params: asset_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.GetAssetTags (Params: assetPath).
- `asset(epic_get_bounds)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns the local-space bounding box of a static mesh. Params: mesh. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GetBounds (Params: mesh).
- `asset(epic_get_channel_keys)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Returns the raw keys of one channel of a gradient, including tangents. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: gradient, channel
- `asset(epic_get_collision_complexity)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset] Returns the collision trace behavior of a static mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mesh
- `asset(epic_get_color_stops)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Returns the color stops of a gradient in time order. Only gradients whose red, green and blue curves share key times, interpolation modes and derived tangents form stops; GetChannelKeys reads any other gradient. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: gradient
- `asset(epic_get_dependencies)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Lists assets that the specified asset depends on. Params: asset_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.GetDependencies (Params: assetPath).
- `asset(epic_get_entry)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Returns the source string for a specific key. Params: string_table, key. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.GetEntry (Params: stringTable, key).
- `asset(epic_get_enumerators)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Returns the enumerators of an enum in order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: enum
- `asset(epic_get_gradients)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Returns the gradients of an atlas in row order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: atlas
- `asset(epic_get_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Returns every node in the graph, each with its input and output pins and, for a connected input pin, the node feeding it. Input parameters and outputs are ordinary nodes here, identified by their ExpressionClass. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph
- `asset(epic_get_graph_issues)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Validates the graph and returns every issue it reports: the errors and warnings the engine's validation surfaces (for example missing required inputs, unsupported types, material or subgraph errors, and node warnings). Use it to check a graph's health after editing. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph
- `asset(epic_get_items)` - [Epic DataRegistryToolset.DataRegistryTools] Returns cached item data. Items must be loaded in the registry cache to be returned. Params: registryName, itemNames
- `asset(epic_get_keys)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Returns all keys for a row. Params: curve_table, row_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.GetKeys (Params: curveTable, rowName).
- `asset(epic_get_live_cloth_simulation_stats)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ChaosClothAssetToolset.ChaosClothAssetToolset] Returns the live solver counts for every clothing simulation running in PIE - the figures the cloth debug UI shows, without opening it. A simulation only reports counts once it is actually simulating. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `asset(epic_get_lod_count)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns the number of LODs in a static mesh asset. Params: mesh. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GetLodCount (Params: mesh).
- `asset(epic_get_lod_thresholds)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns the screen-size thresholds at which each LOD becomes active. Screen size is a ratio of the mesh's screen height to the viewport height. A value of 1.0 means the mesh fills the full viewport height; values above 1.0 are valid and mean the mesh must appear larger than the viewport before the next LOD activates. Each LOD activates when the mesh appears smaller than its threshold. Params: mesh. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GetLodThresholds (Params: mesh).
- `asset(epic_get_material)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns the material assigned to a named slot on a static mesh. Params: mesh, slot_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GetMaterial (Params: mesh, slotName).
- `asset(epic_get_material_slots)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns the names of all material slots in a static mesh. Material slot names are used when assigning materials to specific parts of the mesh. Use these names with get_material and set_material. Params: mesh. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GetMaterialSlots (Params: mesh).
- `asset(epic_get_metadata_tags)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Gets the metadata tags for an asset. Params: asset_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.GetMetadataTags (Params: assetPath).
- `asset(epic_get_namespace)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Returns the namespace of a StringTable asset. Params: string_table. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.GetNamespace (Params: stringTable).
- `asset(epic_get_plugin_content_paths)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Returns the root content paths for plugins that have content. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: include_engine?
- `asset(epic_get_quality_level_min_lod)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset] Returns the per-quality-level Min LOD overrides for a static mesh. Min LOD is the smallest LOD index the mesh may render, forcing lower hardware quality levels to skip its higher-detail, more expensive LODs. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mesh
- `asset(epic_get_quality_level_min_lod_default)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset] Returns the fallback Min LOD for quality levels with no explicit override. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mesh
- `asset(epic_get_referencers)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Lists assets that reference the specified asset. Params: asset_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.GetReferencers (Params: assetPath).
- `asset(epic_get_registry_info)` - [Epic DataRegistryToolset.DataRegistryTools] Returns detailed information about a specific registry. Params: registryName
- `asset(epic_get_rows)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Returns the column values for one or more rows as a JSON string. Params: data_table, row_names. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.GetRows (Params: dataTable, rowNames).
- `asset(epic_get_schema)` - [Epic DataRegistryToolset.DataRegistryTools] Returns the item struct schema as JSON. Params: registryName
- `asset(epic_get_schema__data_table_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Returns the column schema of the data table as a JSON string. Params: data_table. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.GetSchema (Params: dataTable).
- `asset(epic_get_section_clothing)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ChaosClothAssetToolset.ChaosClothAssetToolset] Returns the name of clothing bound for a specific LOD section (if any). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: skeletalMeshPath, lodIndex, sectionIndex
- `asset(epic_get_size)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.texture.TextureTools] Returns the dimensions of a Texture2D in pixels. Params: texture. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.TextureToolset.GetSize (Params: texture).
- `asset(epic_get_table_id)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Returns the table ID for a StringTable asset. The table ID is derived from the asset's package path and is used to reference the string table in text properties and localisation. Params: string_table. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.GetTableId (Params: stringTable).
- `asset(epic_get_tooltip)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Returns the struct-level developer tooltip. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct
- `asset(epic_get_triangle_count)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns the number of triangles in a specific LOD of a static mesh. Params: mesh, lod_index?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GetTriangleCount (Params: mesh, lodIndex?).
- `asset(epic_get_user_asset_tags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Gets the User Asset Tags on an asset. User Asset Tags are user-authored labels stored in the asset's package metadata. They are surfaced by the Manage Tags editor and the Tagged Asset Browser, and are distinct from asset registry tags and metadata tags. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `asset(epic_get_vertex_count)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns the number of vertices in a specific LOD of a static mesh. Params: mesh, lod_index?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.GetVertexCount (Params: mesh, lodIndex?).
- `asset(epic_get_virtual_paths)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Converts internal asset/folder paths to their Content Browser virtual path. In most projects this is the same as the input. In UEFN/Valkyrie projects it can differ (e.g. an engine plugin's content grouped under a friendly display path) -- use this only to present a path to the user in conversation. The result may not be a valid input to other tools; pass the original path (e.g. from FindAssets) to other tools instead. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: paths
- `asset(epic_import_file)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Imports a file from disk as a CurveTable asset. The file's first column is the row name; subsequent columns are sample times and values. interp_mode controls how the imported keys are interpolated between samples. Params: folder_path, asset_name, source_file, interp_mode. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.ImportFile (Params: folderPath, assetName, sourceFile, interpMode, bReplaceExisting?, bSave?).
- `asset(epic_import_file__data_table_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Imports a file from disk as a DataTable asset. The file's columns must match the property names in schema. Use search_row_structs to discover usable schema structs. Params: folder_path, asset_name, source_file, schema. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.ImportFile (Params: folderPath, assetName, sourceFile, schema, bReplaceExisting?, bSave?).
- `asset(epic_import_file__static_mesh_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Imports a mesh file from disk as a StaticMesh asset. Params: folder_path, asset_name, source_file, import_materials?, import_textures?, combine_meshes?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.ImportFile (Params: folderPath, assetName, sourceFile, bImportMaterials?, bImportTextures?, bCombineMeshes?, bReplaceExisting?, bSave?).
- `asset(epic_import_file__string_table_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Imports a file from disk as a StringTable asset. The file must have a header row with at least 'Key' and 'SourceString' columns. Additional meta-data columns are imported but the namespace is not - the StringTable's namespace is derived from its asset path. Params: folder_path, asset_name, source_file. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.ImportFile (Params: folderPath, assetName, sourceFile, bReplaceExisting?, bSave?).
- `asset(epic_import_file__texture_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.texture.TextureTools] Imports an image file from disk as a Texture2D asset. Params: folder_path, asset_name, source_file. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.TextureToolset.ImportFile (Params: folderPath, assetName, sourceFile, bReplaceExisting?, bSave?).
- `asset(epic_is_bitflags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Returns whether the enum is marked as a bitflags type. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: enum
- `asset(epic_is_checked_out)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Checks whether an asset is checked out by the current user. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: asset_path
- `asset(epic_is_dirty)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Checks whether an asset has unsaved changes. Params: asset_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.IsDirty (Params: assetPath).
- `asset(epic_is_nanite_enabled)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Returns whether Nanite is enabled for a static mesh. Nanite is Unreal's virtualized geometry system that renders highly detailed meshes efficiently. It is most beneficial for meshes with many triangles. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: mesh
- `asset(epic_is_nanite_mesh)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset] Returns whether this is a Nanite mesh. This reflects the mesh's current state, so it may briefly lag behind a just-changed Nanite setting while a background rebuild finishes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mesh
- `asset(epic_list_clothing_assets)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ChaosClothAssetToolset.ChaosClothAssetToolset] Returns information about all Clothing Assets on a skeletal mesh. Use AssetName with AssignClothingToSection. Check bRequiresMatchingLodIndex to determine how to supply ClothingLodIndex: - true (ChaosClothAsset): pass the same value as LodIndex - false (UClothingAssetCommon): pass any value in [0, NumClothingLods-1] Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: skeletalMeshPath
- `asset(epic_list_data_sources)` - [Epic DataRegistryToolset.DataRegistryTools] Returns the editor-defined sources configured on a Data Registry. These are the sources as authored on the registry asset, before any runtime expansion of meta sources. Params: registryName
- `asset(epic_list_folders)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Lists the folders contained within a folder. Params: root_path, recursive?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.ListFolders (Params: rootPath, bRecursive?).
- `asset(epic_list_input_params)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Returns the graph's input-parameter nodes: the graph-level parameters such as Scalar, Color, or Vector. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph
- `asset(epic_list_items)` - [Epic DataRegistryToolset.DataRegistryTools] Returns all item names in a Data Registry. Params: registryName
- `asset(epic_list_keys)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Lists all keys in the string table. Params: string_table. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.ListKeys (Params: stringTable).
- `asset(epic_list_node_types)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Returns every available TextureGraph expression node type, sorted. A returned class identifies the node type to add. Input-parameter and Output types are included but are rejected by the generic add; create those through the dedicated input-parameter and output tools. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `asset(epic_list_outputs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Returns the graph's Output nodes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph
- `asset(epic_list_registries)` - [Epic DataRegistryToolset.DataRegistryTools] Returns the names of all registered Data Registries. Params: structFilter?
- `asset(epic_list_rows)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Lists the names of all rows in the curve table. Params: curve_table. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.ListRows (Params: curveTable).
- `asset(epic_list_rows__data_table_tools)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Lists the names of all rows in the data table. Params: data_table. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.ListRows (Params: dataTable).
- `asset(epic_list_runtime_sources)` - [Epic DataRegistryToolset.DataRegistryTools] Returns the runtime sources for a Data Registry. This is the expanded list including transient child sources generated from meta sources. Will equal ListDataSources when the registry has no meta sources. Params: registryName
- `asset(epic_list_variables)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Returns the variables of a struct in authored order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct
- `asset(epic_load_asset)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Loads an asset from the project. Params: asset_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.LoadAsset (Params: assetPath).
- `asset(epic_move)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Moves or renames an asset or folder. Params: path, new_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.Move (Params: path, newPath).
- `asset(epic_move_enumerator)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Moves an enumerator to a new position, shifting the others to accommodate it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: enum, fromIndex, toIndex
- `asset(epic_move_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Moves a variable above or below another variable on the struct. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, variableName, relativeToName, bAbove
- `asset(epic_read_file)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Reads a text file from disk and returns its contents. Only files under /Game/, an enabled plugin's Content/ directory, or the project Saved/ directory may be read. Only plain text formats are supported. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: file_path
- `asset(epic_read_texture)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.TextureToolset] Reads a Texture and returns it as an image. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: texture
- `asset(epic_reload_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Reloads an asset from disk, discarding any unsaved in-memory changes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `asset(epic_remove_clothing_from_section)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ChaosClothAssetToolset.ChaosClothAssetToolset] Removes the clothing binding from a specific LOD section on the skeletal mesh. Mirrors the SkeletalMesh Editor's "Remove Clothing Data" action. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: skeletalMeshPath, lodIndex, sectionIndex
- `asset(epic_remove_collisions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Removes all collision shapes from a static mesh. Params: mesh. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.RemoveCollisions (Params: mesh).
- `asset(epic_remove_entry)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Removes an entry from the string table. Params: string_table, key. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.RemoveEntry (Params: stringTable, key).
- `asset(epic_remove_enumerator)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Removes the enumerator at the given index, shifting later enumerators down to fill the gap. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: enum, index
- `asset(epic_remove_gradient)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Removes a gradient from an atlas, shifting later rows up to fill the gap. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: atlas, gradient
- `asset(epic_remove_input_param)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Removes the input-parameter node exposing the named parameter. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, paramName
- `asset(epic_remove_lods)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Removes all auto-generated LODs from a static mesh, keeping only LOD 0. Params: mesh. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.RemoveLods (Params: mesh).
- `asset(epic_remove_node)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Removes a node and all its connected edges from the graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, node
- `asset(epic_remove_output)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Removes the Output node exposing the named output. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, outputName
- `asset(epic_remove_row)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Removes a row from the curve table. Params: curve_table, row_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.RemoveRow (Params: curveTable, rowName).
- `asset(epic_remove_rows)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Removes rows from the data table. Params: data_table, row_names. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.RemoveRows (Params: dataTable, rowNames).
- `asset(epic_remove_user_asset_tags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Removes the given User Asset Tags from an asset. Pass tag names without the UAT. prefix. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, tags
- `asset(epic_remove_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Removes a variable from a struct. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, variableName
- `asset(epic_rename_row)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Renames a row in the curve table. Params: curve_table, row_name, new_row_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.RenameRow (Params: curveTable, rowName, newRowName).
- `asset(epic_rename_rows)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Renames one or more rows in the data table. Params: data_table, renames. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.RenameRows (Params: dataTable, renames).
- `asset(epic_rename_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Renames a variable. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, oldName, newName
- `asset(epic_sample_gradient)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Evaluates a gradient with the asset's hue, saturation, brightness, vibrance and alpha-range adjustments applied, so the result can differ from the keyed values. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: gradient, time
- `asset(epic_save_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Saves an asset to disk. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `asset(epic_save_assets)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Saves assets to disk. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: asset_paths
- `asset(epic_search)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic SemanticSearchToolset.SemanticSearchToolset] Run a semantic search over the Content Browser assets indexed by the SemanticSearch plugin. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: query, classFilter, pathRegexes, k?
- `asset(epic_search_row_structs)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Finds structs that can be used as a DataTable schema. Params: struct_name?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.SearchRowStructs (Params: structName?).
- `asset(epic_set_alpha_stops)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Replaces the alpha stops of a gradient, leaving its color keys untouched. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: gradient, stops
- `asset(epic_set_bitflags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Sets whether the enum is treated as a bitflags type. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: enum, bEnabled
- `asset(epic_set_channel_keys)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Replaces the raw keys of one channel of a gradient, leaving the other channels untouched. Tangents of keys set to an automatic tangent mode are recomputed; authored tangents are kept. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: gradient, channel, keys
- `asset(epic_set_collision_complexity)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset] Sets the collision trace behavior of a static mesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mesh, complexity
- `asset(epic_set_color_stops)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Replaces the color stops of a gradient, leaving its alpha keys untouched. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: gradient, stops
- `asset(epic_set_display_name)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedEnumToolset] Sets the display name of the enumerator at the given index. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: enum, index, displayName
- `asset(epic_set_entry)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.string_table.StringTableTools] Adds or updates an entry in the string table. If the key already exists its value is replaced; otherwise a new entry is created. Params: string_table, key, value. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StringTableToolset.SetEntry (Params: stringTable, key, value).
- `asset(epic_set_gradients)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.CurveAtlasToolset] Replaces the gradients of an atlas, rebuilding its texture once. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: atlas, gradients
- `asset(epic_set_keys)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.curve_table.CurveTableTools] Replaces all keys in a row with the provided list. Params: curve_table, row_name, keys. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.CurveTableToolset.SetKeys (Params: curveTable, rowName, keys).
- `asset(epic_set_lod_thresholds)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Sets the screen-size thresholds at which each LOD becomes active. Screen size is a ratio of the mesh's screen height to the viewport height. A value of 1.0 means the mesh fills the full viewport height; values above 1.0 are valid and mean the mesh must appear larger than the viewport before the next LOD activates. Thresholds must be in strictly descending order (LOD 0 has the largest threshold), and there must be exactly one threshold per LOD. Params: mesh, thresholds. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.SetLodThresholds (Params: mesh, thresholds).
- `asset(epic_set_material)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Assigns a material to a named slot on a static mesh asset. This affects all instances of the mesh that do not override the slot material. Use set_component_material_override to change materials on a single instance. Params: mesh, slot_name, material. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.SetMaterial (Params: mesh, slotName, material).
- `asset(epic_set_nanite_enabled)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.static_mesh.StaticMeshTools] Enables or disables Nanite for a static mesh. Changing this setting triggers a mesh rebuild. Nanite is most beneficial for high-polygon meshes. Low-polygon meshes may not benefit from Nanite. Params: mesh, enabled. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset.SetNaniteEnabled (Params: mesh, bEnabled).
- `asset(epic_set_node_alias)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Renames a node by setting its primary pin's alias name. For Output nodes this additionally syncs OutputSettings.OutputName so the bake's export filename matches the new node name. For other node types, sets the alias on the node's first output pin (or first input pin for input-only nodes like Scalar/Color/Texture). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, newAlias
- `asset(epic_set_output_settings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Typed shortcut for the most common Output-node configuration. Sets BaseName, FolderPath, and TexturePresetType (cascading preset defaults for Compression/LODGroup/sRGB), then syncs the node's display name and export name to BaseName. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: outputNode, baseName, folderPath, presetType
- `asset(epic_set_pin_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TextureGraphToolset.TextureGraphTools] Sets one input pin's value on a node, resolving the pin by argument name or alias. For a polymorphic Variant pin (which takes its value from a connected node, not a string) this creates an inline constant node matching the value (a scalar, color, or vector inferred from the value string) and connects it to the pin, replacing any existing source. Re-setting an ordinary pin to the value it already holds is an idempotent success. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, pinName, value
- `asset(epic_set_quality_level_min_lod)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset] Merges per-quality-level Min LOD overrides into a static mesh. Quality levels not listed in Overrides keep their current value; pass -1 to clear a level's override. Leaves the fallback Min LOD unchanged. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mesh, overrides
- `asset(epic_set_quality_level_min_lod_default)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.StaticMeshToolset] Sets the fallback Min LOD for quality levels with no explicit override. Leaves existing per-quality-level overrides unchanged. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mesh, default
- `asset(epic_set_rows)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.data_table.DataTableTools] Sets column values for one or more rows. Params: data_table, values. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.DataTableToolset.SetRows (Params: dataTable, values).
- `asset(epic_set_tooltip)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Sets the struct-level developer tooltip. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, tooltip
- `asset(epic_set_variable_default_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Sets a variable's default value. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, variableName, defaultValue
- `asset(epic_set_variable_editable_on_instance)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Sets whether a variable is editable on instances of the struct. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, variableName, bEditableOnInstance
- `asset(epic_set_variable_save_game)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Sets whether a variable participates in SaveGame archives. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, variableName, bSaveGame
- `asset(epic_set_variable_tooltip)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UserDefinedStructToolset] Sets a variable's developer tooltip. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: struct, variableName, tooltip
- `asset(epic_update_metadata_tags)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Sets or removes metadata tags on an asset. Params: asset_path, set_tags?, remove_tags?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.AssetToolset.UpdateMetadataTags (Params: assetPath, setTags?, removeTags?).
- `asset(epic_validate_assets)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.AssetToolset] Validates the given assets and returns the errors and warnings found. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPaths
- `asset(epic_write_file)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.asset.AssetTools] Writes text content to a file on disk. Only files under /Game/, an enabled plugin's Content/ directory, or the project Saved/ directory may be written. Only plain text formats are supported. Overwrites the file if it already exists. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: file_path, content

## blueprint

Wraps 92 official tools.

- `blueprint(epic_add_comment_box)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Adds a comment box (the resizable labeled rectangle) to a Blueprint graph. Pass Nodes to wrap them in a box sized to their bounds, or pass Rect to place a standalone box. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, commentText, nodes?, rect?, padding?
- `blueprint(epic_add_component_bound_event)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Creates a component bound event node in the event graph. Params: component, event_name, graph. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddComponentBoundEvent (Params: component, eventName, graph).
- `blueprint(epic_add_event)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds an event node to the Blueprint's event graph. If event_name matches an inherited overridable event, the new node is an override of that event. Otherwise a new custom event with the given name is created. Idempotent - if an event node with that name already exists, that node is returned. Params: blueprint, event_name, position?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddEvent (Params: blueprint, eventName, position?).
- `blueprint(epic_add_event_dispatcher)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds an event dispatcher to a Blueprint. Params: blueprint, name. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddEventDispatcher (Params: blueprint, name).
- `blueprint(epic_add_function_graph)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds a function graph to the Blueprint. If graph_name matches an inherited overridable function, the new graph is a function-graph override of that function. Idempotent - if a graph with that name already exists, that graph is returned. Params: blueprint, graph_name. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddFunctionGraph (Params: blueprint, graphName, flags?).
- `blueprint(epic_add_function_param)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds an input or output to a function or event dispatcher Params: graph, param_name, param_type, input_param, container_type?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddFunctionParam (Params: graph, paramName, paramType, bInputParam, options?).
- `blueprint(epic_add_interface)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Adds an interface to a Blueprint's implemented interfaces. The interface's function-shape members become function graphs on the Blueprint, and its event-shape members become overridable events. The Blueprint must be recompiled for the change to take effect. Idempotent - adding an interface the Blueprint already implements is a no-op. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blueprint, interfaceClass
- `blueprint(epic_add_matching_delegate_event)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Adds a custom event matching a Create Event node's delegate signature and binds it. The generated event's params match the delegate exactly. Signatures with output params or a return value cannot be implemented by an event - use AddMatchingDelegateFunction for those. The node's OutputDelegate pin must be connected first; the signature is read from it. Idempotent - if an event of the requested name already exists and matches the delegate it is bound and returned. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, eventName?
- `blueprint(epic_add_matching_delegate_function)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Adds a function graph matching a Create Event node's delegate signature and binds it. The generated function's params match the delegate exactly, including the passing mode of each param. The node's OutputDelegate pin must be connected first; the signature is read from it. Idempotent - if a graph of the requested name already exists and matches the delegate it is bound and returned. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, functionName?
- `blueprint(epic_add_node_pin)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds a pin to a node that supports dynamic pin addition. Works for Switch nodes (adds one case pin), Sequence nodes (adds one Then output), commutative binary operators like Add/Multiply (adds one input), Make Array nodes, etc. Params: node. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddNodePin (Params: node).
- `blueprint(epic_add_object_function_param)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds an object reference input or output to a function or event dispatcher. Params: graph, param_name, object_class, input_param, container_type?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddObjectFunctionParam (Params: graph, paramName, objectClass, bInputParam, options?).
- `blueprint(epic_add_object_variable)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds a member or local variable that holds an object reference to a Blueprint. Params: blueprint, name, object_class, graph?, container_type?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddObjectVariable (Params: blueprint, name, objectClass, options?).
- `blueprint(epic_add_struct_function_param)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds a struct input or output to a function or event dispatcher. Params: graph, param_name, struct_type, input_param, container_type?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddStructFunctionParam (Params: graph, paramName, structType, bInputParam, options?).
- `blueprint(epic_add_struct_variable)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds a member or local variable of a struct type to a Blueprint. Use this to add variables of any UStruct type, including custom structs and engine structs not in the basic list supported by add_variable (e.g. HitResult, GameplayTag). Params: blueprint, name, struct_type, graph?, container_type?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddStructVariable (Params: blueprint, name, structType, options?).
- `blueprint(epic_add_variable)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds a member or local variable to a Blueprint. Supported type names: Primitives: 'bool', 'int', 'float', 'byte', 'string', 'name', 'text' Structs: 'Vector', 'Rotator', 'Transform', 'Vector2D', 'LinearColor' Params: blueprint, name, type_name, graph?, container_type?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.AddVariable (Params: blueprint, name, typeName, options?).
- `blueprint(epic_arrange_graph_layered)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Arranges a whole graph in a readable left-to-right layout, per connected component: each component becomes a horizontal band, execution chains hold a single straight row, and comment boxes are honored as nested groups and rebuilt around their members. Does not require an open graph editor. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, pinnedNodes?, restrictToIslandOf?
- `blueprint(epic_arrange_nodes)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Arranges a list of nodes in a readable left-to-right layout. Organizes nodes into columns based on data/execution flow, with producer nodes to the left of the nodes they feed into. Call this after building a graph to avoid nodes overlapping. Connections to nodes outside the list are used as anchors so the arranged nodes integrate cleanly with the rest of the graph. Params: nodes. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ArrangeNodes (Params: graph, nodes).
- `blueprint(epic_break_pins)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Breaks the connection between two pins. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: output_pin, input_pin
- `blueprint(epic_compile_blueprint)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Compiles the given Blueprint. Blueprints should be compiled after all graph modifications are complete. Params: blueprint, warnings_as_errors?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.CompileBlueprint (Params: blueprint, bWarningsAsErrors?).
- `blueprint(epic_connect_pins)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Makes a connection between source (output) and dest (input) pins. Params: output_pin, input_pin. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ConnectPins (Params: outputPin, inputPin).
- `blueprint(epic_create)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Creates a new Blueprint asset in the project. Params: folder_path, asset_name, asset_type. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.Create (Params: folderPath, assetName, assetType).
- `blueprint(epic_create_node)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Adds a new node to the graph. Params: graph, type_id, pos, declaring_class?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.CreateNode (Params: graph, typeId, pos, declaringClass?).
- `blueprint(epic_delete_node)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Deletes the node from its graph. Params: node. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.DeleteNode (Params: node).
- `blueprint(epic_diff)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintDslToolset] Returns a line diff between two Blueprints' graphs, as DSL, and their class defaults. Empty when they match. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `blueprint(epic_diff__blueprint_dsl_tools)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintDslTools] Returns a unified text diff between two Blueprint assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: old_asset_path, new_asset_path
- `blueprint(epic_diff_blueprint_html)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint_diff.BlueprintDiffTools] Diffs the workspace version of a Blueprint against a depot revision and writes HTML. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset_path, output_path, revision?, fragment?
- `blueprint(epic_diff_blueprint_revisions_html)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint_diff.BlueprintDiffTools] Diffs two depot revisions of a Blueprint and writes HTML. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset_path, old_revision, new_revision, output_path, fragment?
- `blueprint(epic_diff_changelist_blueprints_html)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint_diff.BlueprintDiffTools] Writes one HTML diff per Blueprint asset that a changelist modifies. Works for submitted and shelved changelists on any branch. The before and after files come from Perforce, so the workspace version is not used. Added, moved, deleted, and non-Blueprint files are skipped, since they have no earlier version at their path. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: changelist, output_dir, fragment?
- `blueprint(epic_diff_graphs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Compares two versions of a graph with the same matching the editor's Diff window uses. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldGraph, newGraph
- `blueprint(epic_disconnect_pins)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Breaks two connected pins. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: outputPin, inputPin
- `blueprint(epic_docs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintDslToolset] Returns the common grammar followed by the Blueprint dialect reference. Read it before the first Write or Edit. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `blueprint(epic_edit)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintDslToolset] Applies ordered exact replacements to the latest session text. Revision must come from the latest Read, Write, or Edit for the same asset and scope; otherwise call Read again. The full batch is staged before mutation. The edit fails without a matching session, with a stale Revision, when an OldString is absent or non-unique unless bReplaceAll is true, or when the resulting DSL does not parse. Any failure leaves the asset unchanged. When bDslOmitted is true, apply the replacements to your existing copy. Otherwise Dsl is the authoritative canonical text for later edits. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, edits, revision, scope?
- `blueprint(epic_find_node_categories)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Retrieves a list of available node categories in a Blueprint graph, optionally filtered by compatible input and/or output pin types Params: graph, category_filter, context_pins. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.FindNodeCategories (Params: graph, categoryFilter?, contextPins?).
- `blueprint(epic_find_node_references)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintDslToolset] Returns Graph DSL node references whose palette name, category or reference contains the query (case and spaces ignored), with each one's palette category and name. The reference, not the palette name, is what goes in a Write or Edit call head. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, query
- `blueprint(epic_find_node_types)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Finds node types that can be created in a particular graph meeting the search criteria. Params: graph, type_id_filter, context_pins. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.FindNodeTypes (Params: graph, typeIdFilter, contextPins?).
- `blueprint(epic_find_nodes)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Finds nodes in a graph by title, class, and/or execution role. All filters are optional and ANDed together. Useful for locating specific event chains in large graphs like EventGraph before reading them with get_connected_subgraph. Params: graph, title, node_class?, entry_points_only?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.FindNodes (Params: graph, title?, nodeClass?, bEntryPointsOnly?).
- `blueprint(epic_get_comment_box_text)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Returns the text of a comment box. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: commentBox
- `blueprint(epic_get_connected_subgraph)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Returns detailed information for all nodes connected to the given node. Use this alongside find_nodes to read a single event chain from a large graph (like Event Graph) without reading the entire graph. Params: node. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetConnectedSubgraph (Params: node).
- `blueprint(epic_get_create_event_function)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Returns the function currently bound to a Create Event node. Params: node. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetCreateEventFunction (Params: node).
- `blueprint(epic_get_default_object)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Returns the Class Default Object (CDO) for a Blueprint's. ObjectTools list/set/get property will get the CDO automatically, so this should primarily be used before calling tools that want to operate on the object inside the blueprint (like ActorTools). Params: blueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetDefaultObject (Params: blueprint).
- `blueprint(epic_get_delegate_signature)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Returns the signature a Create Event node's bound function must implement. Each param is listed on its own line with its type, direction and passing mode, in the terms AddFunctionParam takes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node
- `blueprint(epic_get_function_flags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Returns the detail-panel settings of a Blueprint function. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph
- `blueprint(epic_get_graph)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Retrieves a specific graph from a Blueprint asset by name. Params: blueprint, graph_name. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetGraph (Params: blueprint, graphName).
- `blueprint(epic_get_graph_dsl_docs)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Returns the full syntax reference for write_graph_dsl. Call this before using write_graph_dsl for the first time. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: none
- `blueprint(epic_get_graph_dsl_docs__blueprint_dsl_tools)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintDslTools] Returns the full syntax reference for write_graph_dsl. Call this before using write_graph_dsl for the first time. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `blueprint(epic_get_node_infos)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Retrieves detailed information for a list of Blueprint graph nodes. Params: nodes. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetNodeInfos (Params: nodes).
- `blueprint(epic_get_node_note)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Returns the note text on a Blueprint node (the small comment bubble shown over an individual node). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node
- `blueprint(epic_get_node_type_pins)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Returns the pin names and types for a node type. Params: graph, type_id. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetNodeTypePins (Params: graph, typeId).
- `blueprint(epic_get_nodes_in_comment_box)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Returns the nodes grouped inside a comment box. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: commentBox
- `blueprint(epic_get_parent)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Returns the parent class of a Blueprint. Params: blueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetParent (Params: blueprint).
- `blueprint(epic_get_pin_value)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Gets the value of a Blueprint graph pin. Params: pin. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetPinValue (Params: pin).
- `blueprint(epic_get_variable_category)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Gets the user-defined category of a Blueprint member variable. Categories group variables in the My Blueprint panel. Variables with no explicit category default to the Blueprint's name in the UI. Params: blueprint, variable_name. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetVariableCategory (Params: blueprint, variableName, graph?).
- `blueprint(epic_get_variable_read_only)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Gets whether a member variable is Blueprint Read Only (graphs can read but not write it). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blueprint, variableName
- `blueprint(epic_get_variable_replication)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Gets the replication mode of a Blueprint member variable. Params: blueprint, variable_name. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.GetVariableReplication (Params: blueprint, variableName).
- `blueprint(epic_get_variable_tooltip)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Gets the tooltip shown for a Blueprint variable in the editor. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blueprint, variableName, graph?
- `blueprint(epic_is_node_note_pinned)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Returns whether a node's note bubble is pinned (always visible). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node
- `blueprint(epic_list_comment_boxes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Lists all comment boxes in a Blueprint graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph
- `blueprint(epic_list_compatible_event_functions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Lists functions that can be bound to a Create Event node. Params: node. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ListCompatibleEventFunctions (Params: node).
- `blueprint(epic_list_component_events)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Lists the bindable delegate events available on a component. Params: component. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ListComponentEvents (Params: component).
