- `blueprint(epic_list_event_dispatchers)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Lists all event dispatchers defined on a Blueprint. Params: blueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ListEventDispatchers (Params: blueprint).
- `blueprint(epic_list_events)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Lists all events visible on the Blueprint - locally defined custom events plus inheritable events from the parent class chain and implemented interfaces. Params: blueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ListEvents (Params: blueprint).
- `blueprint(epic_list_functions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Lists all functions visible on the Blueprint - locally defined plus inheritable ones from the parent class chain and implemented interfaces. Params: blueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ListFunctions (Params: blueprint).
- `blueprint(epic_list_graphs)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Lists all graphs in the Blueprint. Params: blueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ListGraphs (Params: blueprint).
- `blueprint(epic_list_interfaces)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Lists the interfaces implemented by a Blueprint. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blueprint, bIncludeInherited?
- `blueprint(epic_list_variables)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Lists member or local variables defined on a Blueprint. Params: blueprint, graph?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.ListVariables (Params: blueprint, scope?, graph?, bIncludeInherited?).
- `blueprint(epic_read)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintDslToolset] Reads a Blueprint scope as canonical DSL and returns the revision required by Edit. Scope is empty for the whole asset, GraphName for one graph, or GraphName/EntryName for one entry. Backtick-quote a segment containing spaces, syntax characters, or `/`; `` `A/B` `` is one segment. Set bIncludePositions only when node positions are needed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, scope?, bIncludePositions?
- `blueprint(epic_read_graph_dsl)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Reads a Blueprint graph and returns a DSL script. The returned code uses the same syntax that write_graph_dsl accepts and can be edited and passed back to write_graph_dsl to modify the graph. Call get_graph_dsl_docs() for the full syntax reference. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: graph
- `blueprint(epic_read_graph_dsl__blueprint_dsl_tools)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintDslTools] Reads a Blueprint graph and returns a DSL script. The returned code uses the same syntax that write_graph_dsl accepts and can be edited and passed back to write_graph_dsl to modify the graph. Call get_graph_dsl_docs() for the full syntax reference. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, entry_name?, allow_unresolved_references?
- `blueprint(epic_remove_comment_box)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Removes a comment box from its graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: commentBox
- `blueprint(epic_remove_function_graph)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Removes a function graph or event dispatcher from the Blueprint. Params: blueprint, graph_name. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.RemoveFunctionGraph (Params: blueprint, graphName).
- `blueprint(epic_remove_function_param)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Removes an input or output from a function or event dispatcher. Params: graph, param_name, input_param. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.RemoveFunctionParam (Params: graph, paramName, bInputParam).
- `blueprint(epic_remove_interface)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Removes an implemented interface from a Blueprint. The Blueprint must be recompiled afterward. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blueprint, interfaceClass, bPreserveFunctions?
- `blueprint(epic_remove_node_pin)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Removes a specific pin from a node that supports dynamic pin removal. Works for Switch nodes (removes one case pin), Sequence nodes (removes one Then output), commutative binary operators like Add/Multiply (removes one input), Make Array nodes, etc. Params: node, pin. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.RemoveNodePin (Params: node, pin).
- `blueprint(epic_remove_variable)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Removes a member or local variable from a Blueprint. Params: blueprint, name, graph?. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.RemoveVariable (Params: blueprint, name, graph?).
- `blueprint(epic_retarget_node_class)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Replaces a node's baked-in class reference from old_class to new_class in place. If the node's current class reference matches old_class it is replaced with new_class and the node is reconstructed so its pins reflect the new type. If the node already references new_class the call is a no-op. When a Blueprint is duplicated, nodes in the copied graph retain class references pointing to the original Blueprint. Calling this on each node with the original and duplicate classes retargets them without manual delete-recreate-rewire cycles. Handles cast, function call, event, and multicast delegate nodes. The Blueprint must be compiled after all retargeting is complete. Params: node, old_class, new_class. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.RetargetNodeClass (Params: node, oldClass, newClass).
- `blueprint(epic_schema)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintDslToolset] Returns one node type's schema as a DSL form. Query unfamiliar node types before writing: (node `Type` (in (`Pin` Type) ...) ; data input pins (out (`Pin` Type) ...) ; data output pins (params (`Prop` Type [:over]) ...) ; writable properties, baked as :`Prop` value (determines `Prop` ...)) ; properties whose value changes the pin set (Enum A B ...) lists writable values. :over marks a property with an override input, supplied as :`Prop` (wire Source). Write an (Instanced /Script/Pkg.Base) property with a concrete class-headed value: (class:/Script/Pkg.Concrete :`Field` value ...). Empty sections are omitted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, nodeType
- `blueprint(epic_set_comment_box_text)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Sets the text on a comment box. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: commentBox, text
- `blueprint(epic_set_create_event_function)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Binds a function to a Create Event node. Use list_compatible_event_functions to find valid function names. Params: node, function_name. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.SetCreateEventFunction (Params: node, functionName).
- `blueprint(epic_set_function_flags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Sets detail-panel settings on a Blueprint function. Only the fields set on Flags are applied; leave a field unset to keep the function's current value for that flag. The Blueprint must be recompiled for the changes to take effect. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, flags
- `blueprint(epic_set_node_note)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Sets the note text on a Blueprint node (its comment bubble). The bubble is made immediately visible in the graph editor when a non-empty note is set. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, text
- `blueprint(epic_set_node_note_pinned)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Sets whether a node's note bubble is pinned (always visible), vs. only shown on hover. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, bPinned
- `blueprint(epic_set_node_position)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Sets a new position for the node. Params: node, pos. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.SetNodePosition (Params: node, pos).
- `blueprint(epic_set_parent)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Reparents a Blueprint to a new parent class. The Blueprint must be recompiled after reparenting. Params: blueprint, parent_class. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.SetParent (Params: blueprint, parentClass).
- `blueprint(epic_set_pin_value)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Sets the value of a Blueprint graph pin. Params: pin, value. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.SetPinValue (Params: pin, value).
- `blueprint(epic_set_variable_category)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Sets the user-defined category on a Blueprint member variable. Categories group variables in the My Blueprint panel. Pass an empty string to reset to the default (the Blueprint's name). Params: blueprint, variable_name, category. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.SetVariableCategory (Params: blueprint, variableName, category, graph?).
- `blueprint(epic_set_variable_instance_editable)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Sets whether a member variable is editable per-instance on actors placed in the level. Params: blueprint, variable_name, instance_editable. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.SetVariableInstanceEditable (Params: blueprint, variableName, bInstanceEditable).
- `blueprint(epic_set_variable_read_only)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Sets whether a member variable is Blueprint Read Only. A read-only variable can still be read by graphs, but a setter can no longer be created for it. Local variables are not supported. The Blueprint must be recompiled for the change to take effect. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blueprint, variableName, bReadOnly
- `blueprint(epic_set_variable_replication)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Sets the replication mode on a Blueprint member variable. RepNotify will automatically create an OnRep_ function on the Blueprint if one does not already exist. Params: blueprint, variable_name, replication. On UE 5.9 it calls BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset.SetVariableReplication (Params: blueprint, variableName, replication).
- `blueprint(epic_set_variable_tooltip)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintToolset] Sets the tooltip shown for a Blueprint variable in the editor. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blueprint, variableName, tooltip, graph?
- `blueprint(epic_write)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic BlueprintToolsets.BlueprintDslToolset] Transactionally applies a DSL document and returns its canonical form and a new revision. A failure leaves the asset unchanged. Use Write for a complete document or a large structural change. Use Edit for a targeted change that can be expressed as exact replacements against text you just read. Top-level entries use upsert semantics: omitted entries remain and produce a warning. Delete an entry with a tombstone such as (event `Foo` :!delete). Deleting a missing entry only warns. Dsl is omitted when bDslOmitted is true because the submitted document is already canonical. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, dsl, scope?
- `blueprint(epic_write_graph_dsl)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintTools] Populates a Blueprint graph with nodes from a DSL script and compiles the Blueprint. Call get_graph_dsl_docs() for the full syntax reference and examples. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: graph, code
- `blueprint(epic_write_graph_dsl__blueprint_dsl_tools)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.blueprint.BlueprintDslTools] Makes a Blueprint graph match a DSL script and compiles the Blueprint. Statements whose DSL is unchanged from the graph's current state keep their existing nodes - positions, wires, and node ids are preserved - and only changed statements are rebuilt. Call get_graph_dsl_docs() for the full syntax reference and examples. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, code, entry_name?, force_rewrite?

## level

Wraps 99 official tools.

- `level(epic_add_actors_to_data_layer)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Adds actors to a data layer, creating its instance in the current world if it has none yet. Actors already in the layer are left unchanged. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actors, assetPath
- `level(epic_add_component)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Adds a component to an actor instance or blueprint. Params: owner, component_type, name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.AddComponent (Params: owner, componentType, name).
- `level(epic_add_cone)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.primitive.PrimitiveTools] Adds a cone-shaped StaticMeshComponent to an actor. Params: actor, name, radius?, height?, local_transform?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.PrimitiveToolset.AddCone (Params: actor, name, radius?, height?, localTransform?).
- `level(epic_add_cube)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.primitive.PrimitiveTools] Adds a cube-shaped StaticMeshComponent to an actor. Params: actor, name, dimensions?, local_transform?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.PrimitiveToolset.AddCube (Params: actor, name, dimensions?, localTransform?).
- `level(epic_add_cylinder)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.primitive.PrimitiveTools] Adds a cylinder-shaped StaticMeshComponent to an actor. Params: actor, name, radius?, height?, local_transform?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.PrimitiveToolset.AddCylinder (Params: actor, name, radius?, height?, localTransform?).
- `level(epic_add_sphere)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.primitive.PrimitiveTools] Adds a sphere-shaped StaticMeshComponent to an actor. Params: actor, name, radius?, local_transform?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.PrimitiveToolset.AddSphere (Params: actor, name, radius?, localTransform?).
- `level(epic_add_tag)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Adds a tag to an actor. Params: actor, tag. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.AddTag (Params: actor, tag).
- `level(epic_add_to_scene_from_asset)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Creates a new actor in the scene from an asset. Params: asset_path, name, xform, parent?, snap_to_ground?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.AddToSceneFromAsset (Params: assetPath, name, xform, parent?, bSnapToGround?, bAllowSingletonDuplicate?).
- `level(epic_add_to_scene_from_class)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Creates a new instance of the specified object at the specified transform. Params: actor_type, name, xform, parent?, snap_to_ground?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.AddToSceneFromClass (Params: actorType, name, xform, parent?, bSnapToGround?, bAllowSingletonDuplicate?).
- `level(epic_annotate_actor)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Annotate a scene actor, so its meshes are marked in the matte. Annotating it again replaces the prompt. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actor, prompt, group?, bShowLabel?
- `level(epic_are_annotated_actors_always_on_top)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Whether annotated scene actors draw through occluders, which also makes the matte ignore occlusion. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_are_labels_visible)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Whether the viewport draws the prompt labels. Each annotation can also hide its own (bShowLabel). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_can_edit)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Checks whether an actor can be edited. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: actor
- `level(epic_commit_level_instance)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Saves or discards edits to a level instance and exits edit mode. Params: level_instance, discard?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.CommitLevelInstance (Params: levelInstance, bDiscard?).
- `level(epic_create_data_layer_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Creates a new Data Layer asset in the content browser. A Data Layer asset is reusable across levels; assign it to actors in the current world by adding them to the data layer. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, bIsRuntime?
- `level(epic_create_level)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Creates a new empty level asset and opens it in the editor. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelPath, bWorldPartition?
- `level(epic_create_level_instance)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Creates a Level Instance actor in the scene referencing an existing level asset. Params: level_path, name, xform, parent?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.CreateLevelInstance (Params: levelPath, name, xform, parent?).
- `level(epic_delete_folder)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Deletes a folder from the outliner. Actors directly in the folder are moved to the parent folder. Sub-folders and their actors are preserved by re-rooting them under the parent. For example, deleting 'Lighting' with a sub-folder 'Lighting/Spotlights' leaves 'Spotlights' intact under the parent. Params: folder_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.DeleteFolder (Params: folderPath).
- `level(epic_diff)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.ActorToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two actors. Compares the actors' classes, top-level properties, and per-component class and top-level properties. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldActor, newActor
- `level(epic_diff__level_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two scene asset packages, each expected to contain a single external actor. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldScenePath, newScenePath
- `level(epic_edit_level_instance)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Opens a level instance for editing. While in edit mode, scene tools such as add_to_scene_from_class and remove_from_scene operate within the level instance's sub-level. Only one level instance can be in edit mode at a time. Call commit_level_instance when done to save or discard changes. Params: level_instance. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.EditLevelInstance (Params: levelInstance).
- `level(epic_find_actors)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Searches the scene for actors that match specific criteria. Params: root?, name, actor_type?, tag, bounds?, collision_channels. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.FindActors (Params: filter?).
- `level(epic_find_annotation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Find an annotation by the id an MCP tool or GetAnnotations reported. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: id
- `level(epic_gather_visible_annotations)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationToolset] Returns the annotations with their on-screen rects, as the JSON the image pipeline consumes. Each entry carries a `visible` flag that is false when the annotation is out of frustum OR its colour group is hidden - so an isolate/show call shows up there, not by dropping entries. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_get_active_group)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] The group, 1-8, that new annotations join when no group is given. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_get_actor_asset_path)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Returns the content path that identifies this actor on disk. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actor
- `level(epic_get_actor_bounds)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the bounding box of an actor. Params: actor. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetActorBounds (Params: actor).
- `level(epic_get_actor_transform)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the position, rotation, and scale of an actor. Params: actor. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetActorTransform (Params: actor).
- `level(epic_get_actors_in_data_layer)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Returns descriptors for the loaded actors assigned to a data layer. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `level(epic_get_actors_in_folder)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Returns the actors in the specified outliner folder. Params: folder_path, recursive?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.GetActorsInFolder (Params: folderPath, bRecursive?, levelInstance?).
- `level(epic_get_annotations)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] List every annotation in the editor level, proxies and annotated actors alike. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_get_canonical_actor_state)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.ActorToolset] Returns a JSON-safe canonical form of an actor (or a Blueprint's CDO) for diffing. Captures the actor's class, its top-level properties, and each component's class and top-level properties. References to the actor itself (path or label) are normalized to "<actor>", and for Blueprint CDOs the generated class path is normalized to "<class>" and the reported class becomes the parent native class, so two CDOs from structurally identical Blueprints produce identical output despite their unique generated-class paths. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actor
- `level(epic_get_capture_stage)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Where in the frame the viewport capture reads colour. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_get_collision_channels)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Returns all available collision channels for use with find_actors. Params: none. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.GetCollisionChannels (Params: none).
- `level(epic_get_component_actor)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the actor that owns the specified component. Params: component. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetComponentActor (Params: component).
- `level(epic_get_component_transform)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.ActorToolset] Returns the position, rotation, and scale of a scene component. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: component
- `level(epic_get_components)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the components that an actor contains. Params: actor, component_type?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetComponents (Params: actor, componentType?).
- `level(epic_get_current_level)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Returns the path to the current level asset. Params: none. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.GetCurrentLevel (Params: none).
- `level(epic_get_data_layers)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Returns the data layer instances in the current world. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_get_folders)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Returns all folder paths currently in use in the outliner. Includes all intermediate parent paths. For example, if an actor is assigned to 'Lighting/Spotlights', both 'Lighting' and 'Lighting/Spotlights' are returned. Params: none. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.GetFolders (Params: name?).
- `level(epic_get_label)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the actor's human friendly name as it appears in the editor. Params: actor. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetLabel (Params: actor).
- `level(epic_get_parent_component)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the parent component that this component is attached to, if any. Params: component. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetParentComponent (Params: component).
- `level(epic_get_parent_level_instance)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Returns the level instance that directly contains an actor, loaded or not. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actor
- `level(epic_get_root_component)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the root component of an actor, if any. Params: actor. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetRootComponent (Params: actor).
- `level(epic_get_tags)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns the list of tags on an actor. Params: actor. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.GetTags (Params: actor).
- `level(epic_get_visible_groups)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] The colour groups shown in the viewport overlay and the matte, in ascending order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_has_tag)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Returns whether an actor has a specific tag. Params: actor, tag. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.HasTag (Params: actor, tag).
- `level(epic_is_actor_hidden)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Returns whether the actor is hidden in the editor viewport and outliner. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actor
- `level(epic_is_checked_out)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Checks whether an actor is checked out by the current user. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: actor
- `level(epic_is_level_instance_loaded)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Returns whether a level instance's level is loaded. Level instances created by other tools, or that came with a loaded level, finish loading some time after those tools return. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelInstance
- `level(epic_is_overlay_visible)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Whether the viewport draws the coloured annotation overlay. The matte and passes do not depend on it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_isolate_annotation_group)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationToolset] Shows a single colour group and hides the rest, so the annotation guide marks one target. - `level(epic_persist_annotations)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Save every scratch annotation with the level from now on. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none

- `level(epic_rename_folder)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Renames a folder in the outliner. Updates the folder path for all actors in the folder and any sub-folders. For example, renaming 'Lighting' to 'Lights' also updates actors in 'Lighting/Spotlights' to 'Lights/Spotlights'. If the new path already exists then the affected actors will be merged into it. Params: old_path, new_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.RenameFolder (Params: oldPath, newPath).
- `level(epic_replace_actors_with_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Replaces actors in the scene with new actors created from an asset (e.g. a static mesh, Blueprint, or particle system), matching the editor's "Replace Selected Actors With..." command. Preserves each actor's world transform, World Partition identity (actor guid and external package), and layers, and reattaches its former parent and children. Does not preserve the outliner folder path, Actor tags, or data layer assignments. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actors, assetPath, bCopySourceProperties?
- `level(epic_replace_actors_with_class)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Replaces actors in the scene with new instances of a native Actor class, matching the editor's "Replace Selected Actors With..." command. Preserves each actor's world transform, World Partition identity (actor guid and external package), and layers, and reattaches its former parent and children. Does not preserve the outliner folder path, Actor tags, or data layer assignments. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actors, actorType

- `level(epic_set_active_group)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Set the group, 1-8, that new annotations join. It must be visible, or they would not show. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: group
- `level(epic_set_actor_folder)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.scene.SceneTools] Assigns an actor to the specified folder in the outliner. Creates the folder implicitly if it does not already exist. Pass an empty string to move the actor to the root of the outliner. Params: actor, folder_path. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.LevelToolset.SetActorFolder (Params: actor, folderPath).
- `level(epic_set_actor_hidden)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LevelToolset] Shows or hides the actor in the editor viewport and outliner. Params: actor, bHidden

- `level(epic_set_capture_stage)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] For this editor session; Project Settings saves it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: stage
- `level(epic_set_component_transform)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.ActorToolset] Updates the position, rotation, and/or scale of a scene component. - `level(epic_set_labels_visible)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Set Labels Visible Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bVisible
- `level(epic_set_overlay_visible)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Show or hide the viewport overlay. It turns on again when a level that had no annotations gets one. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bVisible
- `level(epic_set_parent_component)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.actor.ActorTools] Sets the parent for the specified scene component. For blueprint actors, passing a component as the parent of the root promotes it to the scene root, making the current root a child of it. If the current root is a DefaultSceneRoot, Unreal will automatically remove it. Params: component, parent?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ActorToolset.SetParentComponent (Params: component, parent?).
- `level(epic_set_persist_new_annotations)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] For this editor session; Project Settings > Plugins > Unreal Canvas Spatial Annotations saves it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bPersist
- `level(epic_set_visible_annotation_groups)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationToolset] Shows a specific set of colour groups and hides every other one. If the active group ends up hidden it moves to the lowest group still visible, so a later PlaceAnnotation without an explicit Group stays visible. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. - `level(epic_set_visible_groups)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Show only these colour groups. If the active group ends up hidden it moves to the lowest visible one. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: groups
- `level(epic_should_persist_new_annotations)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Whether new annotations are saved with the level (the default) or are scratch work. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `level(epic_show_all_annotation_groups)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationToolset] Makes every annotation colour group visible again, undoing an isolate or set-visible call. - `level(epic_show_all_groups)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UnrealCanvasAnnotationsEditor.UnrealCanvasSpatialAnnotationEditorSubsystem] Show All Groups Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none

## material

Wraps 39 official tools.

- `material(epic_add_expression)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Adds a new expression node to a Material or MaterialFunction graph. Use list_expression_classes to discover available types. Params: material_or_function, expression_class, x?, y?. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.AddExpression (Params: materialOrFunction, expressionClass, x?, y?).
- `material(epic_clear_parameters)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Clears all parameter overrides on a material instance, reverting to parent defaults. Params: instance. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.ClearParameters (Params: instance).
- `material(epic_connect_expressions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Connects an expression node's output pin to another expression node's input pin. Params: from_expression, from_output_name, to_expression, to_input_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.ConnectExpressions (Params: fromExpression, fromOutputName, toExpression, toInputName).
- `material(epic_connect_to_output)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Connects an expression node's output to one of the material's output properties. Params: expression, output_name, material_property. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.ConnectToOutput (Params: expression, outputName, materialProperty).
- `material(epic_create)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Creates a new MaterialInstanceConstant asset derived from a parent material. Material instances expose the parent's parameters without triggering a full shader recompile when parameter values change. Params: folder_path, asset_name, parent. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.Create (Params: folderPath, assetName, parent).
- `material(epic_create_function)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Creates a new empty MaterialFunction asset. Params: folder_path, asset_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.CreateFunction (Params: folderPath, assetName).
- `material(epic_create_material)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Creates a new empty Material asset. Warning: Each new Material increases shader compile times. Prefer creating a MaterialInstance from an existing Material where possible. Params: folder_path, asset_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.CreateMaterial (Params: folderPath, assetName).
- `material(epic_create_parameter_collection)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Creates a new empty MaterialParameterCollection (MPC) asset. An MPC holds named Scalar and Vector parameters with default values that materials can reference at runtime without recompiling shaders. Params: folder_path, asset_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.CreateParameterCollection (Params: folderPath, assetName).
- `material(epic_delete_expression)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Removes an expression node from a Material or MaterialFunction graph. Params: material_or_function, expression. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.DeleteExpression (Params: materialOrFunction, expression).
- `material(epic_delete_parameter_group)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Removes a parameter group, ungrouping all parameters that belong to it. The parameter expressions themselves are not deleted - only their group assignment is cleared. Params: material_or_function, group_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.DeleteParameterGroup (Params: materialOrFunction, groupName).
- `material(epic_delete_unused_expressions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Deletes all expression nodes not connected to any material output. Useful for cleaning up a material graph after reorganising or after the AI has added experimental nodes that were later abandoned. Params: material. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.DeleteUnusedExpressions (Params: material).
- `material(epic_diff)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two MaterialInstanceConstant assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `material(epic_diff_function)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.MaterialToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two MaterialFunction assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `material(epic_diff_material)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.MaterialToolset] Returns a human-readable text diff between two Material assets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldAssetPath, newAssetPath
- `material(epic_disconnect_expressions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Disconnects the input pin of an expression node, removing whatever is connected to it. Params: to_expression, to_input_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.DisconnectExpressions (Params: toExpression, toInputName).
- `material(epic_disconnect_from_output)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Disconnects the expression currently connected to a material output property. Params: material, material_property. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.DisconnectFromOutput (Params: material, materialProperty).
- `material(epic_get_expression_input_names)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns the names of all input pins on a material expression node. Use these names as to_input_name when calling connect_expressions. Params: expression. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.GetExpressionInputNames (Params: expression).
- `material(epic_get_expression_inputs)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns the current wiring of each input pin on a material expression. Use after building or modifying a graph to verify the wiring matches expectations. Params: material_or_function, expression. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.GetExpressionInputs (Params: materialOrFunction, expression).
- `material(epic_get_expression_output_names)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns the names of all output pins on a material expression node. Use these names as from_output_name when calling connect_expressions or connect_to_output. The empty string represents the default (first) output of nodes that expose only an unnamed output. Params: expression. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.GetExpressionOutputNames (Params: expression).
- `material(epic_get_expressions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns all expression nodes in a Material or MaterialFunction graph. Params: material_or_function. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.GetExpressions (Params: materialOrFunction).
- `material(epic_get_property_input)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns the expression and output pin feeding a material output property. Use to inspect what drives MP_EmissiveColor, MP_Opacity, MP_BaseColor, etc. Params: material, material_property. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.GetPropertyInput (Params: material, materialProperty).
- `material(epic_get_referencing_materials)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns asset data for all Materials that reference this MaterialFunction. Params: material_function. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.GetReferencingMaterials (Params: materialFunction).
- `material(epic_get_scalar_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Gets the current value of a scalar parameter on a material instance. Params: instance, name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.GetScalarParameter (Params: instance, name).
- `material(epic_get_static_switch_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Gets the value of a static switch parameter on a material instance. Params: instance, name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.GetStaticSwitchParameter (Params: instance, name).
- `material(epic_get_statistics)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.MaterialToolset] Returns compiled shader statistics for a Material or MaterialInstance on the current shader platform: instruction counts, texture-sample counts, and sampler usage. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: material
- `material(epic_get_texture_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Gets the texture assigned to a texture parameter on a material instance. Params: instance, name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.GetTextureParameter (Params: instance, name).
- `material(epic_get_vector_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Gets the current value of a vector parameter on a material instance. Params: instance, name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.GetVectorParameter (Params: instance, name).
- `material(epic_layout_expressions)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Automatically arranges all expression nodes in a Material or MaterialFunction graph. Params: material_or_function. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.LayoutExpressions (Params: materialOrFunction).
- `material(epic_list_expression_classes)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns MaterialExpression subclasses valid for the given context. Use the results with add_expression. Pass a search string to filter by name, e.g. 'Multiply' or 'Parameter'. Params: material_or_function, search. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.ListExpressionClasses (Params: materialOrFunction, search?).
- `material(epic_list_parameter_groups)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Returns the unique parameter group names defined in a Material or MaterialFunction. Parameters are organised into groups in the Material Instance editor. This returns the distinct set of group names found across all parameter expressions in the graph. The empty string represents parameters that have not been assigned to a named group. Params: material_or_function. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.ListParameterGroups (Params: materialOrFunction).
- `material(epic_list_parameters)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Returns all parameters exposed by a material or instance, with their names and types. Params: material. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.ListParameters (Params: material).
- `material(epic_recompile)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Recompiles a Material or MaterialFunction after edits. For Materials, raises if the shader fails to compile. For MaterialFunctions, also recompiles any Materials that reference the function. Call this once after a set of graph modifications is complete - after adding or deleting expressions, making connections, or changing expression properties such as parameter names or default values. Params: material_or_function. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.Recompile (Params: materialOrFunction).
- `material(epic_rename_parameter_group)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material.MaterialTools] Renames a parameter group across all parameter expressions in a Material or MaterialFunction. All parameters currently in old_name will be moved to new_name. If new_name already exists, the parameters are merged into it. Params: material_or_function, old_name, new_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialToolset.RenameParameterGroup (Params: materialOrFunction, oldName, newName).
- `material(epic_set_parameter_override)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Enables or disables a parameter override on a material instance. Enabling sets the override to the current effective value. Disabling reverts to the parent. For non-static parameter types, disabling also discards the prior override value; re-enabling later restores the parent value, not the prior override. Static switches and static component masks preserve their value across toggle. Params: instance, name, override. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.SetParameterOverride (Params: instance, name, bOverride).
- `material(epic_set_parent)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Changes the parent of a material instance. Params: instance, parent. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.SetParent (Params: instance, parent).
- `material(epic_set_scalar_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Sets the value of a scalar parameter on a material instance. - `material(epic_set_static_switch_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Sets the value of a static switch parameter on a material instance. - `material(epic_set_texture_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Assigns a texture to a texture parameter on a material instance. Params: instance, name, value. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.SetTextureParameter (Params: instance, name, value).
- `material(epic_set_vector_parameter)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.material_instance.MaterialInstanceTools] Sets the value of a vector parameter on a material instance. Params: instance, name, value. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.MaterialInstanceToolset.SetVectorParameter (Params: instance, name, value).

## animation

Wraps 1004 official tools.

- `animation(epic_add_actors)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Add actors from the level to the currently open sequence. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: actors

- `animation(epic_add_actors_by_name)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Add actors to the sequence by their names in the level. Finds actors by label in the current level and adds them to the currently open sequence as possessable bindings. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: actor_names
- `animation(epic_add_actors_by_name__level_sequence_editor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Add all actors in the Sequencer playback world whose labels match the supplied names. Raises an error when no actors match. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actorNames
- `animation(epic_add_actors_to_binding)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Add actors to an existing binding. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: actors, binding
- `animation(epic_add_actors_to_binding__level_sequence_editor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Assigns the given actors to the binding Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actors, objectBinding
- `animation(epic_add_anim_layer_from_selection)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Add an anim layer containing the controls and objects selected in Sequencer, adding the Base layer first if the sequence has no layers. With nothing selected it adds an empty layer named "Empty Layer <N>". Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_add_auto_bake)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set up auto-bake recorders for the linked anim sequences on the given bindings. Equivalent to the "Add AutoBake" context-menu entry in the Sequencer UI. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inBindings
- `animation(epic_add_auto_bake_with_anim_sequences)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Same as AddAutoBake, but links pre-created anim sequences instead of prompting for new ones. No asset creation or export options dialogs are shown. Everything is validated first: if anything is invalid an error is raised and nothing is changed. That includes a binding that already has a linked anim sequence, a null, incompatible, repeated or already linked anim sequence, or an anim sequence the editor's asset reference filter refuses to link (as in UEFN). The anim sequences are only linked, not baked here: like AddAutoBake the level sequence is dirtied and the auto-bake runs when the linked track is toggled to, overwriting each anim sequence. The current export options are recorded on the link. To undo this for a binding, call DeleteLinkedAnimTrack, which removes the recorder, the link and the linked track. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inBindings, inAnimSequences
- `animation(epic_add_backward_solve_graph)` - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig.ControlRigTools] Create a backward solve graph with InverseExecution event. Params: control_rig, name?

Raises a script error if a handle is null or invalid, the child is the parent, or the parent is constrained to the child, directly or through a chain of constraints. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world, inType, inChild, inParent, bMaintainOffset
- `animation(epic_add_constraint_between)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Add a constraint between two objects, creating their transformable handles; the constraint owns the handles. Each object is a Control Rig with a control name as its attachment, a scene component with an optional socket, or an actor (its root component, optional socket). When a level sequence is open in Sequencer the constraint is keyed active at CreationTime, as the Constraints panel's creation options do; otherwise it is added as a static constraint. Raises a script error if an object is null or not transformable, a control or socket does not exist, a control's type cannot be constrained (Bool, Float, ScaleFloat, Integer), the child is the parent, the parent is constrained to the child, directly or through a chain of constraints, or the constraint cannot be added. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world, type, childObject, childAttachment, parentObject, parentAttachment, bMaintainOffset, creationTime?

- `animation(epic_create_camera_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Create a cine camera in the active Sequencer and return its binding. A spawnable camera is owned by the sequence. Otherwise the camera is a CineCameraActor placed in the level and bound as a plain possessable, so it renders in Movie Render Queue and PIE without a binding override. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bSpawnable
- `animation(epic_create_condition_endpoint)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AnimationAssistantToolset.SequencerDirectorToolset] Create a Director Blueprint Condition function endpoint on the sequence's Director Blueprint and bind it to a director-blueprint condition on the given host (a section, a whole track, or a single track row), so the host only evaluates at runtime when the function returns true. If the host has no condition, a Director Blueprint Condition is created on it; if it already has one, that condition is reused. A host carrying a different condition type (e.g. a platform or group condition) is rejected - clear it first via MovieSceneConditionExtensions.ClearSectionCondition or ClearTrackCondition. Generates a function graph named after FunctionName whose entry node takes an FMovieSceneConditionContext and returns a bool. The body is left empty by design - author it via BlueprintToolset, then call BlueprintToolset.CompileBlueprint on the Director BP so the condition's CompiledFunctionName is populated; without that the condition is skipped at runtime. The returned UK2Node_FunctionEntry is the new function's entry node. Scope, CheckFrequency, bEditorForceTrue and payload variables are EditAnywhere on the condition; set them via set_editor_property on the condition UObject, obtained from the native accessor (e.g. section.get_section_condition()). Retrieve the condition object before editing its properties; a class-path string is not an object reference. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sequence, conditionHost, functionName, rowIndex?
- `animation(epic_create_control_rig_pose_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Create a pose asset from a Control Rig's controls, as the Pose Library's Create Pose does; a unique name is used if the asset exists. Raises a script error if the Control Rig is null, the path is not a valid long package path, the folder is not visible or not writable in this editor (as in UEFN), or bUseAllControls is false and no control is selected. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: controlRig, packagePath, assetName, bUseAllControls?
- `animation(epic_create_deformer)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] Creates a deformer asset with an empty UpdateGraph and the primary component binding. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `animation(epic_create_dynamic_binding_resolver)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AnimationAssistantToolset.SequencerDirectorToolset] Create a dynamic-binding resolver function endpoint on the sequence's Director Blueprint and wire it to the binding's FMovieSceneDynamicBinding so the binding is resolved at runtime by the generated function. Generates a function graph named after FunctionName whose entry node returns a FMovieSceneDynamicBindingResolveResult. The function body is left empty by design - callers author it via BlueprintToolset (e.g. drop a "GetPlayerPawn" node, connect its return to the entry's Object pin via a MakeStruct FMovieSceneDynamicBindingResolveResult). The returned UK2Node_FunctionEntry is the entry-point node of the new function graph; pass it by name lookup to BlueprintToolset calls that take node references. After authoring the body, call BlueprintToolset.CompileBlueprint on the Director BP to populate FMovieSceneDynamicBinding's CompiledFunctionName. Otherwise the resolver will be silently skipped at runtime. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sequence, binding, functionName

- `animation(epic_export_anim_sequence__sequencer_tools_function_library)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScriptingEditor.SequencerToolsFunctionLibrary] Export Passed in Binding as an Anim Sequence. Note use this when you want to export without an open Level Sequence in Sequencer, and know that all spawnables that may be dynamically created and destroyed will be valid. If the spawnable won't be fully created and you have an open Sequencer use UControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary::ExportAnimSequenceFromSequencer instead. The animation asset must be writable, as must ExportOptions when provided. The binding must belong to Sequence. The exported range is the sequence's playback range, which must be non-empty and bounded, unless ExportOptions enables a custom time range; that range must then have a positive display rate and an end frame at or after its start. Creating a link also requires a writable sequence. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world, sequence, animSequence, exportOptions, binding, bCreateLink
- `animation(epic_export_anim_sequence_from_sequencer)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Export the binding's animation to an Anim Sequence, using the level sequence focused in Sequencer. Use this instead of USequencerToolsFunctionLibrary::ExportAnimSequence when you may be exporting an anim sequence from a Spawnable that can't be created dynamically in a closed system. With bCreateLink, raises, changing nothing, when the level sequence is read-only or the editor's asset reference filter refuses a link between the level sequence and the AnimSequence (as in UEFN). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: animSequence, exportOption, binding, bCreateLink

Raises when trails are hidden, the rig has no track in the sequence, the control is missing or has no trail, or the trail could not be pinned, as when MaxNumberPinned trails are already pinned. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlName

- `animation(epic_reorder_anim_layers__control_rig_sequencer_editor_library)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Move Anim Layer to new index, this will also change evaluation order Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: oldIndex, newIndex
- `animation(epic_reorder_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Place nodes among their siblings in the order given, as the outliner's Sort by Selection Order does, storing the result in their sorting orders. A node in Nodes with a descendant also in Nodes keeps its place. Raises for a node that cannot be sorted, such as a channel row, and for a node under a binding, whose children keep their type and label order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: nodes
- `animation(epic_replace_binding_with_actors)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Replace all bound actors on a binding with new ones. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: actors, binding
- `animation(epic_replace_binding_with_actors__level_sequence_editor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Replaces the binding with the given actors Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actors, objectBinding
- `animation(epic_replace_control_rig)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Replace the Control Rig on the track with a new Control Rig. Note it will now be owned by the track. When the track's sequence is focused in Sequencer, a rig with no bound object is bound to the track's skeletal mesh and initialized, and the new rig replaces the old one in Sequencer and Animation Mode, so setting its values keys it. The new rig is constructed with the track's settings and rotation order overrides, and the controls selected on the old rig are selected on it. One undoable step. Raises a script error if the track is not a writable Control Rig track, the rig is already on it or on another track, one is layered and the other is not, or the editor's asset reference filter refuses a reference from the track's sequence to the rig's asset (as in UEFN). ReplaceControlRigFromReference creates the new rig from a Control Rig class or asset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inControlRigTrack, newControlRig
- `animation(epic_replace_control_rig_from_reference)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Replace the Control Rig on a Control Rig track with a new rig created from a Control Rig class or asset, as FindOrCreateControlRigTrackFromReference creates one. Keys on controls the new rig also has are kept, a layered track stays layered, an FK rig keeps the old FK rig's apply mode, and the track is renamed after the new rig when it comes from a different class or asset. The new rig is constructed with the track's settings and rotation order overrides, and the controls selected on the old rig are selected on it. When the track's sequence is focused in Sequencer, the new rig is bound to the track's skeletal mesh and replaces the old one in Sequencer and Animation Mode, so it can be read and keyed at once. One undoable step. Raises a script error if the track is not a writable Control Rig track, the reference is not a Control Rig class or asset, the track is layered and the new rig is not an FK rig and has no backwards solve event, or the editor's asset reference filter refuses a reference from the track's sequence to the rig (as in UEFN). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inTrack, inRef
- `animation(epic_reset_control_rig_controls_to_default)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set controls to their initial values at the current Sequencer time, as Anim Details' Reset To Default does; this covers every control type, where ZeroTransforms covers transforms. Raises a script error if the sequence is not the one focused in Sequencer or a control does not exist. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlNames, bSetKey?
- `animation(epic_reset_playback_context)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Let the open Level Sequence editor choose its world automatically again, from the auto-bind to PIE and Simulate settings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_restore_pre_animated_state_async)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Restore captured pre-animated state and wait for the editor world to tick. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_save_control_rig_pose_to_clipboard)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Save the pose of the selected controls to Animation Mode's pose clipboard, as Save Pose does; with bWholePose, every control of the rig with a selected control, or else of the selected bound actor's rig, as Save Whole Pose does. Raises a script error if Animation Mode is not active, or without bWholePose if no control is selected. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bWholePose?
- `animation(epic_save_default_spawnable_state)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.custom_bindings.SequencerCustomBindingTools] Save the current state of a spawnable as its default. Params: binding. On UE 5.9 it calls LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem.SaveDefaultSpawnableState (Params: objectBinding).
- `animation(epic_select_all_control_rig_controls)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Select every control on the Control Rigs in Animation Mode. Raises a script error if Animation Mode is not active or has no Control Rigs, or if bAlreadySelected is true and no Control Rig in it has a selected control. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bAlreadySelected?

Raises a script error if Index is out of range or the layer has no controls or objects. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: index, bClearSelection?
- `animation(epic_select_anim_layer_weight_channel)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Select an anim layer's weight channels in Sequencer, as the panel's weight button does. Selection drives the selection-based functions such as TweenControlRig, BlendValuesOnSelected, MirrorSelectedControls, ZeroTransforms, and AddAnimLayerFromSelection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: controlRig, controlNames, bSelect?, bClearSelection?
- `animation(epic_select_control_rig_pose_asset_controls)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Select the controls stored in a pose asset, as the Pose Library's Select Controls does. - `animation(epic_select_curve_editor_keys)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Add the specified key indices to the active Curve Editor selection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: channel, indices

- `animation(epic_select_sections)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set the section selection in the Sequencer editor. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sections

- `animation(epic_select_tracks)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set the track selection in the Sequencer editor. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: tracks

- `animation(epic_set_actor_world_transform)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Key an actor's world transform at a frame in the level sequence focused in Sequencer. The key is expressed in the actor's attach parent and, where the actor is the child of an active constraint, in that constraint's space, so the actor reaches the transform. Raises a script error for the cases SetActorWorldTransforms raises for. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, actor, frame, worldTransform, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_set_actor_world_transforms)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Key an actor's world transforms at frames in the level sequence focused in Sequencer, adding a Transform track and section if needed. Each key is expressed in the actor's attach parent and, where the actor is the child of an active constraint, in that constraint's space, so the actor reaches the transform. Location, rotation and scale are keyed. Raises a script error if the sequence is not the one focused in Sequencer, the actor is null or not bound in it, or the counts differ. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, actor, frames, worldTransforms, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_set_actor_world_transforms_async)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] SetActorWorldTransforms as an async operation. See SetActorWorldTransforms for the parameters. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, actor, frames, worldTransforms, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_set_anim_layer_key)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Key an anim layer's current values at the current Sequencer time, on the layer's selected controls and objects or, if none are selected, on all of them. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: index
- `animation(epic_set_anim_layer_passthrough_key)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set a passthrough key on an anim layer at the current Sequencer time, on the layer's selected controls and objects or, if none are selected, on all of them. A passthrough key makes the layer contribute nothing at that time: additive layers key zero and override layers key the value of the layers below. On the Base layer this keys the inverted input pose instead. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: index
- `animation(epic_set_anim_layer_properties)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set an anim layer's name, mute, lock, weight, type and keyed state; only the properties that differ from the layer's are applied, in one undo step. Raises a script error, changing nothing, if no level sequence is open in Sequencer, Index is out of range, the name is empty, the type is not Base for the Base layer or is Base for another layer, or bKeyed is false on the keyed layer. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: index, properties
- `animation(epic_set_anim_mode_gizmo_scale)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Set the editor's transform gizmo size. Writes UTransformGizmoEditorSettings::TransformGizmoSize. The CR-specific gizmo scale was removed in UE 5.8 in favor of this editor-wide setting. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: scale

- `animation(epic_set_anim_mode_local_spaces)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Toggle multi-select transforms acting in each control's own space. When True, transforming multiple selected controls respects each control's own local space. When False, all use a shared reference. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: enabled
- `animation(epic_set_anim_mode_nulls)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Toggle the Animation Mode nulls display. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: enabled

- `animation(epic_set_animation_mode_settings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set every Animation Mode preference and save them to the user's config. Open viewports update immediately. To change some preferences only, read them with GetAnimationModeSettings, edit those fields, and pass the result back. Raises a script error, changing nothing, if TransformGizmoSize is outside -10 to 150, AxisScale or AxisThickness is negative, or a tween setting is not finite. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: settings
- `animation(epic_set_animation_mode_tool_open)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Open or close one of Animation Mode's tools, as its toolbar button or command does. - `animation(epic_set_blender_system)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneTrackExtensions] Set the system that blends the track's overlapping sections. Must be one of GetSupportedBlenderSystems. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: track, blenderSystem
- `animation(epic_set_bool)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Set a bool control value at a specific frame. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sequence, control_rig_asset_path, control_name, frame, value, set_key?
- `animation(epic_set_bound_actor_class)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic TemplateSequenceEditor.TemplateSequenceEditorSubsystem] Rebind the sequence to ActorClass by replacing its root spawnable, moving the tracks and component bindings under the old root to the new one. Component bindings resolve by component name, so those ActorClass lacks no longer bind. Camera animation sequences only accept ACameraActor and its subclasses, and a sequence with more than one spawnable is rejected since its root is ambiguous. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: templateSequence, actorClass
- `animation(epic_set_byte_track_enum)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Configure a byte track to use a specific enum type. - `animation(epic_set_byte_track_enum__movie_scene_property_track_extensions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieScenePropertyTrackExtensions] Set this byte track's enum Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: track, inEnum
- `animation(epic_set_camera_cut_auto_arrange_sections)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneTrackExtensions] Set whether camera cut sections auto-arrange. - `animation(epic_set_camera_cut_binding)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set which camera a camera cut section uses. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: section, camera_binding_id

- `animation(epic_set_camera_cut_can_blend)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneTrackExtensions] Set whether overlapping camera cut sections blend. Disabling it removes section overlaps and easing, as the editor does. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: track, bCanBlend
- `animation(epic_set_camera_lock)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Lock or unlock the camera cut to the viewport. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: lock

- `animation(epic_set_component_binding_source)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] Changes the kind of component a binding reads from. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: deformer, bindingName, componentSource
- `animation(epic_set_constraint_active_key)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set the constraint active key in the current open Sequencer Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inConstraint, bActive, inFrame, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_apply_mode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set the FK Control Rig to apply mode Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inControlRig, inApplyMode
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_available_spaces_order)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Reorder a control's space picker list, as moving spaces up and down in the level editor does. Params: controlRig, controlName, spaces
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_controls_visible)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Show or hide a Control Rig's control shapes in the viewport, as Toggle Controls Visibility does for the selected rigs and Toggle Module Controls Visibility for modules; hiding all shapes at once is SetAnimationModeSettings' bHideControlShapes. Raises a script error for the cases GetControlRigControlsVisible raises for. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: controlRig, bVisible, moduleName?
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_layered_mode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Convert the control rig track into absolute or layered rig. This is destructive: non-FK rigs reset their control values and every section clears its animation and is recreated, and the track is renamed to the rig's class name. Converting to layered requires a backwards solve event unless the rig is an FK rig. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inTrack, bSetIsLayered
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_override)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Override a control setting on a Control Rig instance, as Anim Details' override view does: with an empty Value the setting's current value is captured (Add Override), otherwise the setting takes Value (Edit Override Value). The override goes in the rig's last linked override asset, creating one in the developer folder when it has none, and the rig is reconstructed so it applies. PropertyPath is a control setting path such as TopologyControlSettings->DisplayName, TopologyControlSettings->bShapeVisible, ShapeName, ShapeColor or ShapeTransform; Anim Details' names DisplayName, Shape->bVisible, Shape->Name, Shape->Color and Shape->Transform are accepted too. Raises a script error if overrides are disabled (ControlRig.Overrides), the Control Rig is null, the control does not exist, the path is not a setting of the control, Value cannot be read as that setting, the override asset to edit is read-only, or an override asset must be created and the rig has no asset or the developer folder is not visible or not writable in this editor (as in UEFN). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: controlRig, controlName, propertyPath, value?
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_priority_order)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set the Control Rig track's priority order; tracks with a higher priority evaluate first. Raises a script error if PriorityOrder is negative. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inTrack, priorityOrder
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_rotation_order_override)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Override a control's rotation order, as the Anim Details rotation order dropdown does: its keys are re-baked into the new order so the rotations stay the same, and the rig evaluates in that order. Layered (additive) Control Rigs do not support overrides. Raises a script error for the cases GetControlRigRotationOrder raises for, or when the Control Rig is layered. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlName, rotationOrder
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_section_transform_mask)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set which transform and weight channels a Control Rig section animates (its Active Channels). Disabled channels stop animating and are not keyed; their keys are kept. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inSection, mask
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_space)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Key the Control Rig Space for the Control at the specified time, compensating so the control keeps its world transform. If the control has no space channel and the space is its current parent, nothing is keyed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inSequence, inControlRig, inControlName, inSpaceKey, inTime, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_world_transform)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set ControlRig Control's World Transform at a specific time Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlName, frame, worldTransform, timeUnit?, bSetKey?
- `animation(epic_set_control_rig_world_transforms)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set ControlRig Control's World Transforms at a specific times.
