- `animation(epic_set_controls_mask)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Set the visibility mask for the specified controls on a section. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: section, control_names, visible
- `animation(epic_set_controls_mask__control_rig_sequencer_editor_library)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set whether the section animates the given controls (bVisible true) or masks them out (false). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inSection, controlNames, bVisible
- `animation(epic_set_curve_editor_view_mode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Set how the active Sequencer's Curve Editor lays out its curves. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewMode
- `animation(epic_set_custom_clock_source)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSequenceExtensions] Drive the sequence's playback from ClockSource, as the Custom clock source picker does: an asset or actor that implements MovieSceneCustomClockSource, or an actor with a component that does. Sets the clock source to Custom. Raises if the source is hidden in this editor or may not be referenced from this sequence. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sequence, clockSource
- `animation(epic_set_custom_time_warp)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneTimeWarpExtensions] Replace a section's time warp with a new custom time warp of TimeWarpClass, initialized the way the editor does. Its curve then appears among the section's channels ("PlayRate" for a play rate curve, "TimeWarp" for a time warp curve). PlayRate values are play rate multipliers. TimeWarp values are warped times in tick resolution frames, keyed against unwarped time measured from the section start, so the initial keys map 0 to 0 and the section length to itself. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: section, timeWarpClass
- `animation(epic_set_data_interface_setting)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] Sets a setting of a data interface node, as listed in FDeformerGraphNodeInfo::Settings. - `animation(epic_set_display_name)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneBindingExtensions] Set this binding's name Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inBinding, inDisplayName

- `animation(epic_set_display_rate)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set the display frame rate of a sequence. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sequence, numerator, denominator?
- `animation(epic_set_display_rate__movie_scene_sequence_extensions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSequenceExtensions] Sets this sequence's display rate Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sequence, displayRate
- `animation(epic_set_double_default)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Set the double channel default value. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: channel, inDefaultValue
- `animation(epic_set_double_key_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Set a live double key's value at double precision. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: channel, key, newValue
- `animation(epic_set_ease_in_duration)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionEasingExtensions] Set manual ease-in duration in tick-resolution frames (not display-rate frames), enabling the manual override Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: section, inDuration
- `animation(epic_set_ease_out_duration)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionEasingExtensions] Set manual ease-out duration in tick-resolution frames (not display-rate frames), enabling the manual override Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: section, inDuration
- `animation(epic_set_edit_pivot_tool_active)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Turn the Edit Pivot tool on or off in the level editor, as the Animation Mode "Temporary Pivot" button does. It works on the selected actors and Control Rig controls, so select them first; it turns itself off when the selection changes. It shares the level editor's tool slot with motion trails: turning it on stops motion trails, and SetMotionTrailsVisible(true) turns it off. Raises when no level sequence is open in Sequencer, or the tool does not start. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bActive
- `animation(epic_set_emulated_network_mask)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Set the network mode the active Sequencer's root sequence emulates during playback and recompile to match. Raises when no Sequencer is open or the mask is None. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: networkMask
- `animation(epic_set_emulated_network_mask_async)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Set the network mode the active Sequencer's root sequence emulates during playback and wait for evaluation. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: networkMask
- `animation(epic_set_end_frame_bounded)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Set end frame bounded Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: section, bIsBounded
- `animation(epic_set_enum_track_enum)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieScenePropertyTrackExtensions] Set the enum whose values an enum track animates, including on its existing sections. Keys are entry values, set through the sections' byte channels. A byte track with SetByteTrackEnum animates an enum property the same way. Raises if the enum is hidden in this editor or may not be referenced from this sequence. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: track, inEnum
- `animation(epic_set_euler_transform)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Set an EulerTransform control value at a specific frame. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sequence, control_rig_asset_path, control_name, frame, location_x?, location_y?, location_z?, rotation_pitch?, rotation_yaw?, rotation_roll?, scale_x?, scale_y?, scale_z?, set_key?
- `animation(epic_set_evaluation_type)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set the evaluation type of a sequence. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sequence, eval_type
- `animation(epic_set_evaluation_type__movie_scene_sequence_extensions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSequenceExtensions] Set the evaluation type for this sequence Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inSequence, inEvaluationType
- `animation(epic_set_film_overlay)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Show one of GetFilmOverlayNames in a Cinematic Viewport, or None for no composition overlay. Raises when the viewport is not open or the name is unknown. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewport, overlayName
- `animation(epic_set_film_overlay_frame_enabled)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Enable or disable a frame overlay, which every Cinematic Viewport shows while it is enabled. Raises when the name is unknown. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: frameName, bEnabled
- `animation(epic_set_film_overlay_frame_tint)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Set the color a frame overlay draws with. Raises when the name is unknown. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: frameName, tint
- `animation(epic_set_film_overlay_tint)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Set the tint a Cinematic Viewport applies to the composition overlay it shows, like the Film Overlay menu's tint control. Raises when the viewport is not open. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewport, tint
- `animation(epic_set_fkcontrol_rig_controls_active)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set whether an FK Control Rig animates controls, as its Select Bones To Animate dialog does: inactive controls stop driving their bones and are masked out of the rig's sections in the focused level sequence. Raises a script error if the sequence is not the one focused in Sequencer, the Control Rig is not an FK Control Rig, or a control does not exist. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlNames, bActive
- `animation(epic_set_float)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Set a float control value at a specific frame. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sequence, control_rig_asset_path, control_name, frame, value, set_key?
- `animation(epic_set_float_default)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Set the float channel default value. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: channel, inDefaultValue
- `animation(epic_set_float_key_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Set a live float key's value. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: channel, key, newValue
- `animation(epic_set_folder_color)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneFolderExtensions] Set the display color of the given folder Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folder, inFolderColor

- `animation(epic_set_kernel_additional_sources)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] Sets the shader source assets the kernel includes before its own source, e.g. to share HLSL functions between kernels. - `animation(epic_set_kernel_execution_domain)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] SetKernelExecutionDomain Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: deformer, nodePath, executionDomain
- `animation(epic_set_kernel_group_size)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] SetKernelGroupSize Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: deformer, nodePath, groupSize
- `animation(epic_set_kernel_name)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] SetKernelName Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: deformer, nodePath, kernelName
- `animation(epic_set_kernel_source)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic OptimusScripting.DeformerGraphScriptingSubsystem] Sets the HLSL source. Errors in it are only reported by Compile. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: deformer, nodePath, source
- `animation(epic_set_key_time)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Move a live key to NewFrameNumber. - `animation(epic_set_layered_mode)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Set a Control Rig track to layered or absolute mode. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sequence, control_rig_asset_path, is_layered
- `animation(epic_set_letterbox_aspect_ratio)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Set the width and height terms of the ratio the LetterBox frame overlay masks the viewport to. Raises when either term is outside the range the Film Overlay menu accepts. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: width, height
- `animation(epic_set_linked_anim_sequence_settings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Set a linked Anim Sequence's export settings, as Edit in Sequencer's Modify Level Sequence Link Settings does; the link's binding and Anim Sequence path are kept. Turning bAutoBake on adds the linked animation track that bakes when the level sequence is saved, as Add Auto Bake does; turning it off removes it. Raises a script error if the binding has no level sequence, the Anim Sequence is null or not linked to the binding, bAutoBake changes while the level sequence is not open in Sequencer, or bAutoBake turns on and the editor's asset reference filter refuses a reference from the level sequence to the Anim Sequence (as in UEFN). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: binding, animSequence, settings

On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.SkeletalMeshToolset.SetMaterial (Params: mesh, slotName, material).
- `animation(epic_set_motion_trail_offset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Draw a pinned trail moved by an offset, in the space of the object at each frame, without changing any keys. Raises when the trail is not pinned or trails are hidden. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: trailId, offset
- `animation(epic_set_motion_trail_settings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Set the motion trail display settings and save them to the editor preferences (KeyColor lasts the session only). The struct holds every field, so read it with GetMotionTrailSettings, change what you need and pass it back. Raises for a negative frame count or size, MaxNumberPinned outside 5 to 100, or the TimePosing style while Time Posing is off; nothing is changed then. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inSettings
- `animation(epic_set_motion_trail_space)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Draw a pinned trail relative to another actor, or to one of its components, sockets or bones, instead of world space. Raises when the trail is not pinned or SpaceActor is null. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: trailId, spaceActor, componentOrSocketName

The filter is editor state and is not saved with the sequence. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: group, bEnabled

Name is stored without surrounding whitespace and must not be blank. Renaming to the current name changes nothing. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: group, name
- `animation(epic_set_node_hidden)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Hide nodes from the outliner, or show nodes hidden this way again. Hiding a node hides its descendants with it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: nodes, bHidden
- `animation(epic_set_node_isolated)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Isolate nodes so the outliner shows only isolated nodes with their ancestors and descendants, or stop isolating them Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: nodes, bIsolated

- `animation(epic_set_nodes_expanded_by_path)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Expand or collapse paths atomically. Missing or ambiguous paths raise an error. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: paths, bExpanded

- `animation(epic_set_property_name_and_path__movie_scene_property_track_extensions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieScenePropertyTrackExtensions] Set this track's property name and path Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: track, inPropertyName, inPropertyPath

- `animation(epic_set_section_completion_mode)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set the completion mode of a section. Valid values: 'KeepState', 'RestoreState', 'ProjectDefault'. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: section, completion_mode
- `animation(epic_set_section_condition)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.conditions.SequencerConditionTools] Set a condition on a section. - `animation(epic_set_section_ease_in)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set the ease-in duration of a section in frames. Enables manual ease override if not already active. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: section, duration

- `animation(epic_set_section_play_rate)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneTimeWarpExtensions] Give a section a fixed play rate, replacing any custom time warp. A rate of 0 holds the section on its first frame; GetTimeWarpType then returns FixedTime. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: section, playRate
- `animation(epic_set_section_post_roll_frames)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set the number of frames to post-roll this section after it ends. - `animation(epic_set_section_row_index)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Set the zero-based track row. A negative row is invalid, and so is any row but 0 on a track that does not support multiple rows. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: section, rowIndex
- `animation(epic_set_section_start_bounded)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Set whether the section start frame is bounded or infinite. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: section, bounded

- `animation(epic_set_selection_set_visibility)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Show, hide or isolate a selection set's controls on the actors selected in the viewport, as the set's Hide, Show, Isolate and Show All menu entries do. - `animation(epic_set_sequence_flag)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSequenceExtensions] Set or clear one evaluation flag. Set Volatile on any sequence that will be changed procedurally at runtime. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sequence, flag, bEnabled

- `animation(epic_set_skeletal_animation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSectionExtensions] Assign an animation asset by content path to a skeletal animation section. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: section, animationAssetPath

- `animation(epic_set_time_posing_active)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Turn Time Posing on or off for a sequence. - `animation(epic_set_time_posing_settings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Set the Time Posing preferences and save them to the editor preferences. - `animation(epic_show_all_controls__control_rig_sequencer_editor_library)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Unmask every control on the section so all of them are animated. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inSection
- `animation(epic_show_all_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Show every hidden and isolated node again, as the filter bar's Show All Tracks does Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_show_all_selection_set_actor_controls)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Show every control on an actor's Control Rigs, as the actor's Show All in the Selection Sets panel does. - `animation(epic_snap_control_rig__control_rig_sequencer_editor_library)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Peform a Snap operation to snap the children to the parent. - `animation(epic_sort_all_curve_editor_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Sort the items of the active Sequencer's open Curve Editor tree. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_sort_all_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Sort every node by type and then by label, discarding stored sorting orders, as Sequencer's Sort All Nodes command does Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_sort_nodes_by_start_time)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Sort nodes among their siblings by the start of their earliest section, as the outliner's Sort by Start Time does, storing the result in their sorting orders. A folder in Nodes has its children sorted too. A node in Nodes with a descendant also in Nodes keeps its place, an unbounded section starts at its first key, and a node with no start, such as one with no sections or keys, counts as frame 0. Raises for a node that cannot be sorted, such as a channel row, and for a node under a binding, whose children keep their type and label order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: nodes, bDescending?
- `animation(epic_space_compensate)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Perform compensation for any spaces at the specified time for the specified control rig Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inControlRig, inTime, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_space_compensate_all)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Perform compensation for all spaces for the specified control rig Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inControlRig
- `animation(epic_tag_binding)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Attach a tag to a binding. If the tag has not been seen in the sequence before, it is automatically registered. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: binding, tag_name
- `animation(epic_tag_binding__movie_scene_binding_tag_extensions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneBindingTagExtensions] Attach a tag to the given binding. If the tag name has not been seen before, it is registered in the MovieScene's tag list automatically. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: binding, tagName
- `animation(epic_time_pose_actor_world_transform)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Key an actor's world transform at a frame and spread the change across the Time Posing window by weight, as moving the actor with Time Posing on does. One undoable step. Raises when Time Posing is off, the frame is not strictly inside the far anchors, or SetActorWorldTransform raises. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, actor, frame, worldTransform, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_time_pose_control_rig_world_transform)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Key a Control Rig control's world transform at a frame and spread the change across the Time Posing window by weight, as moving the control with Time Posing on does; the keys go to the control's section to key, so Anim Layers apply. One undoable step. Raises when Time Posing is off, the frame is not strictly inside the far anchors, or SetControlRigWorldTransform raises. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlName, frame, worldTransform, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_transform_actors_about_pivot)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Key actors transformed about a pivot at each frame, as the Edit Pivot tool does in Pose mode: the new world transform is World * Pivot^-1 * Delta * Pivot. With bKeepOrientation the actors orbit the pivot and keep their rotation and scale, as Shift does in the tool. Keys the actors in one undoable step. An actor that cannot be keyed (not bound in the sequence, or its Transform track has no Absolute section) is skipped and the reason is logged as an error. Raises when the sequence is not focused or is read-only, Actors or Frames is empty, an actor is null, or no actor can be keyed; nothing is keyed then. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, actors, frames, delta, pivotTransform, bKeepOrientation?, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_transform_controls_about_pivot)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Move, rotate or scale Control Rig controls about a pivot, keying them at each frame. At every frame a control's new world transform is World * Pivot^-1 * Delta * Pivot: Delta is applied in the pivot's space, so a 90 degree yaw turns the controls about the pivot's up axis. Controls are keyed with SetControlRigWorldTransforms, so constraints and spaces are compensated. Raises a script error if the sequence is not focused, a control does not exist, Frames is empty, or PivotObject names more than one object. Every control is checked for being driven by the rig at the first frame before any is keyed; if keying still fails partway, the controls keyed before the failure stay keyed, in the call's single transaction, so one undo reverts the whole call. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlNames, frames, delta, pivotObject, pivotTransform, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_transform_controls_about_pivot_async)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] TransformControlsAboutPivot as an async operation. See TransformControlsAboutPivot for the parameters. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, controlNames, frames, delta, pivotObject, pivotTransform, timeUnit?
- `animation(epic_tween_control_rig)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Perform a tween operation on a Control Rig at the current Sequencer time. The tween blends between the previous and next keyframe values. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: sequence, control_rig_asset_path, tween_value
- `animation(epic_tween_control_rig__control_rig_sequencer_editor_library)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Perform a Tween operation on the Control Rig's selected controls at the current Sequencer time. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: levelSequence, controlRig, tweenValue
- `animation(epic_ungroup_section)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneSequenceExtensions] Remove a section from its group. Raises when the section is not grouped. A group left with one section is dissolved. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sequence, section
- `animation(epic_unlink_anim_sequence)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Remove the link between a binding and one linked Anim Sequence, on both assets. Raises a script error if the binding has no level sequence or the Anim Sequence is null. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: binding, animSequence
- `animation(epic_unpin_all_motion_trails)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Unpin every actor, control and socket trail. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_unpin_motion_trail)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Unpin a motion trail; it stays drawn while its actor or control is selected. Raises when the trail is not pinned. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: trailId
- `animation(epic_unpin_selected_motion_trails)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerAnimTools.SequencerAnimToolsEditorLibrary] Unpin the pinned trails of the selected actors and controls, as Alt-clicking Motion Paths does. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `animation(epic_untag_binding)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.sequencer.SequencerTools] Remove a tag from a binding. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: binding, tag_name
- `animation(epic_untag_binding__movie_scene_binding_tag_extensions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequencerScripting.MovieSceneBindingTagExtensions] Remove a tag from the given binding. Removing its last association also unregisters the tag. No-op if the binding lacks the tag. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: binding, tagName
- `animation(epic_update_control_rig_pose_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Replace a pose asset's pose with a Control Rig's current values, as the Pose Library's Update Pose does. Raises a script error if the pose asset or Control Rig is null, or bUseAllControls is false and no control is selected. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: poseAsset, controlRig, bUseAllControls?
- `animation(epic_validate_sequence)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SequenceValidator.SequenceValidatorSubsystem] Run validation rules on Sequence and its subsequences, as the Sequence Validator window does. The sequence is not modified. Findings in, or reached through, subsequences hidden in this editor are left out. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sequence, ruleNames
- `animation(epic_wait_for_evaluation_async)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic LevelSequenceEditor.LevelSequenceEditorSubsystem] Wait for a complete editor world tick after this call. TimeoutSeconds must be positive. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: timeoutSeconds?
- `animation(epic_zero_transforms)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic animation_toolset.toolsets.controlrig_sequencer.SequencerControlRigTools] Reset Control Rig transforms to their default (usually zero) values. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: selection_only?, include_channels?
- `animation(epic_zero_transforms__control_rig_sequencer_editor_library)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ControlRigEditor.ControlRigSequencerEditorLibrary] Reset Transforms to default values, usually zero values but which may not be zero if initial values are present. Applies at the current time only. Requires Animation Mode to be active. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bSelectionOnly, bIncludeChannels

## pcg

Wraps 75 official tools.

- `pcg(epic_add_comment_box)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Adds a comment box around the given nodes. Params: graph, nodes, comment?, color?
- `pcg(epic_add_comment_box__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Adds a comment box around the given nodes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, nodes, comment, color?
- `pcg(epic_add_node)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Adds a native node to the graph. Params: graph, nativeNodeType, nodeName, jsonParams?, nodeTitle?, nodeComment?, xPositionIdx?, yPositionIdx?
- `pcg(epic_add_node__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Adds a native node to the graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, nativeNodeType, nodeName, jsonParams?, nodeTitle?, nodeComment?, xPositionIdx?, yPositionIdx?
- `pcg(epic_add_subgraph_node)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Adds a subgraph node to the graph. Params: graph, subGraphForNode, nodeName, jsonParams?, nodeTitle?, nodeComment?, xPositionIdx?, yPositionIdx?
- `pcg(epic_add_subgraph_node__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Adds a subgraph node to the graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, subGraphForNode, nodeName, jsonParams?, nodeTitle?, nodeComment?, xPositionIdx?, yPositionIdx?
- `pcg(epic_collapse_nodes_to_subgraph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Collapses at least two nodes into a subgraph. Empty AssetPath creates an embedded graph; otherwise creates a new unsaved asset named Name in the folder specified by AssetPath. Nodes must belong directly to Graph; input/output nodes are rejected. Comment boxes containing selected node positions and no unselected node positions move to the subgraph too. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, nodes, name, assetPath?
- `pcg(epic_connect_node_pins)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Add an edge between two nodes connected to the specified pins. Params: fromNode, fromPinLabel, toNode, toPinLabel
- `pcg(epic_connect_node_pins__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Add an edge between two nodes connected to the specified pins. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: fromNode, fromPinLabel, toNode, toPinLabel
- `pcg(epic_create_graph)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Creates a new saved PCG graph asset. Params: name, path?
- `pcg(epic_create_graph__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Creates a new unsaved PCG graph asset with a unique name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: name, path?
- `pcg(epic_delete_embedded_subgraph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Deletes an embedded graph. Referenced graphs raise a script error unless bAllowReferenced is true, which clears references and leaves missing-subgraph nodes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: embeddedGraph, bAllowReferenced?
- `pcg(epic_disconnect_node_pins)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Removes the edge between two nodes connected to the specified pins. Params: fromNode, fromPinLabel, toNode, toPinLabel
- `pcg(epic_disconnect_node_pins__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Removes the edge between two nodes connected to the specified pins. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: fromNode, fromPinLabel, toNode, toPinLabel
- `pcg(epic_draw_spline)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Triggers the user to draw a spline in the viewport to be used later in the world building. Waits for the user to be done. Params: actorLabel, actorTag, bRedraw, bClosedSpline
- `pcg(epic_draw_spline__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Triggers the user to draw a spline in the viewport to be used later in the world building. Waits for the user to be done. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actorLabel, actorTag, bRedraw, bClosedSpline
- `pcg(epic_execute_graph_instance)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Schedules generation. Allow editor ticks until the returned result is complete, then inspect Error and Value (captured node diagnostics). Returns an error if the source is already generating. Params: pCGVolume?, executionSource?
- `pcg(epic_execute_graph_instance__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Schedules generation and returns immediately. Returns an error if the source is already generating. Allow editor ticks for generation to progress. Completion includes generation post-processing. Error reports invalid input, scheduling failure, cancellation or abandonment. Value contains captured node messages, which can include errors even when Error is empty. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: executionSource
- `pcg(epic_execute_transient_graph_async)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Executes a transient instance of the PCG graph asynchronously with the specified parameters. Await the returned result in Python to receive execution messages. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, params
- `pcg(epic_get_execution_sources)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Returns registered execution sources as JSON entries with executionSource, gridSize and gridCoordinates. Pass a PCG component or execution source to restrict the list to its world and graph instance; omit it to list all registered sources. Does not generate sources or load cells. Pass a returned source to GetNodeDataView. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: executionSource?
- `pcg(epic_get_execution_sources__pcgtoolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Returns registered execution sources as JSON entries with executionSource, gridSize and gridCoordinates. Pass a PCG component or execution source to restrict the list to its world and graph instance; omit it to list all registered sources. Does not generate sources or load cells. Pass a returned source to GetNodeDataView. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: executionSource?

- `pcg(epic_get_node_data_view)` - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Returns a JSON Data View of a specific node's output data from the supplied execution source. Enables source and node inspection and requests generation when inspection is first enabled. Runtime sources refresh through the runtime scheduler. Allow editor ticks and query again while pending; polling does not repeatedly regenerate. GPU output is read back before serialization. Params: pCGVolume?, node, pinLabel?, attributeName?, startIndex?, endIndex?, executionSource?, executionStack?
- `pcg(epic_get_node_data_view__pcgeditor_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Returns a JSON Data View of a specific node's output data from the supplied execution source. - `pcg(epic_save_data_as_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Exports a data collection to a PCG Data Asset using the attribute inspector's save operation. Game thread only. Headless, commandlet and unattended runs suppress the save dialog; provide Parameters.AssetPath and Parameters.AssetName. Otherwise, Parameters.bOpenSaveDialog controls whether the destination is chosen interactively. Parameters.bSaveOnExportEnded controls saving to disk, including unchanged assets. Returns the asset, or null for empty data, cancellation, export failure or a failed requested save. Save diagnostics are reported separately. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: data, parameters
- `pcg(epic_set_graph_automatic_regeneration_paused)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Controls automatic graph regeneration without requiring an open graph editor. Shares the toolbar's transient pause state. Ordinary change notifications are deferred until resume; forced notifications and explicit execution can bypass the pause. Restore the previous state after edits, including on failure. Closing the graph editor also resumes regeneration. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: graph, bPaused

- `pcg(epic_wait_for_graph_instance)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGEditor.PCGEditorSubsystem] Returns an async result for the source's current generation, without starting or restarting it. Returns an error if no generation is active. Await the result in Python, or allow editor ticks until it completes, then inspect Error and Value as with ExecuteGraphInstance. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: executionSource
- `pcg(epic_wait_for_graph_instance__pcgtoolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PCGToolset.PCGToolset] Returns an async result for the source's current generation, without starting or restarting it. Returns an error if no generation is active. Inspect Error and Value as with ExecuteGraphInstance once the result completes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: executionSource

## niagara

Wraps 57 official tools.

- `niagara(epic_add_emitter)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Adds an emitter to a Niagara System. The new emitter will be based on the template emitter, inheriting its configuration and modules. Returns the full emitter topology (no input values - call GetEmitterInputValues for values). Params: system, templateEmitter, emitterName
- `niagara(epic_add_module)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Adds a module to a script stack. The module will be inserted into the specified script's execution stack. Returns the module topology with all inputs walked (no input values - call GetModuleInputValues for values). Params: moduleLocationRef, moduleAsset
- `niagara(epic_add_renderer)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Adds a renderer to an emitter. Creates a new renderer of the specified type and adds it to the emitter's renderer list. Returns an FNiagaraExt_RendererRef with the new renderer's Index and RendererClass, usable directly with SetRendererData / GetRendererData without a follow-up topology call. Params: newRendererLocation, rendererClass
- `niagara(epic_add_set_parameter_entry)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Adds a single parameter to an existing SetParameters module. The module referenced by ModuleRef must be a SetParameters (UNiagaraNodeAssignment) module. Use bIsSetParametersModule in the module topology to confirm before calling. Params: moduleRef, entry
- `niagara(epic_add_set_parameters_module)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Adds a SetParameters module to a script stack. Unlike AddModule which requires a script asset, a SetParameters module dynamically assigns values to named parameters and generates its own internal script. Use this when you need to set one or more particle/emitter/system parameters directly in the stack. Params: moduleLocationRef, parameters
- `niagara(epic_add_user_variables)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Adds or updates user variables on a system. If a variable with the same name already exists, it will be replaced with the new definition. Params: system, variablesToAdd
- `niagara(epic_apply_stack_issue_fix)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Applies a Fix-style stack issue fix identified by IssueId and FixId. Link-style fixes are rejected. The fix is undoable via the editor undo stack. Applying a fix may trigger a recompile; the result waits for that compile to complete so post-fix state is valid. Params: system, issueId, fixId
- `niagara(epic_construct_niagara_bpwrapper_from_component)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Blueprint] Creates a Blueprint actor wrapper from a Niagara Component. This generates a new Blueprint actor and preserves all component property values and user variable overrides. Params: newAssetPath, component, parentClass
- `niagara(epic_construct_niagara_bpwrapper_from_system)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Blueprint] Creates a Blueprint actor wrapper around a Niagara System. This generates a new Blueprint actor with a Niagara component configured to use the specified system. Params: newAssetPath, system, parentClass
- `niagara(epic_create_niagara_system)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Creates a new Niagara System asset. The new system will be based on the template system, inheriting its configuration and emitters. Params: assetName, assetPath, templateSystem
- `niagara(epic_find_niagara_scripts)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Assets] Searches for UNiagaraScript assets matching the given filters. Params: folderPath, name, usages, visibilities, moduleUsageBitmask?, bRecursive, bIncludeDeprecated, supportedUsages?
- `niagara(epic_get_asset_discovery_info)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Assets] Returns the project's configured asset discovery groups. Params: none
- `niagara(epic_get_available_dynamic_inputs)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns all available Dynamic Input Module assets compatible with the given type. Dynamic inputs provide procedural value generation for module parameters. Params: type
- `niagara(epic_get_data_interface_schema)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns property schema for a specific Data Interface class. Describes all available properties and their types for the given data interface type. Params: dataInterfaceClass
- `niagara(epic_get_dynamic_input_chain)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns the full recursive chain for a dynamic input: topology metadata and resolved values at every level. The starting input must have value mode Dynamic; an error is surfaced otherwise. The schema expands one full level and emits a typed recursion stub at deeper levels; the wire format recurses to arbitrary depth matching the underlying chain. Params: stackInputRef
- `niagara(epic_get_dynamic_input_schema)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns schema for a dynamic input module in the stack. Describes the inputs and configuration for a procedural value generator. Params: dynamicInputReference?, stackInputRef?
- `niagara(epic_get_dynamic_input_schema_from_asset)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns schema for a dynamic input asset. Standalone function that doesn't require a system context - useful for browsing available dynamic inputs. Params: dynamicInputAsset
- `niagara(epic_get_emitter_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns emitter property values as a single JSON-string blob in PropertyValues. The blob contains the full FVersionedNiagaraEmitterData (SimTarget, bLocalSpace, RandomSeed, FixedBounds, etc.) - fields use C++ PascalCase, must be parsed to read individual values. For typed access to common metadata (SimTarget, EmitterName, bEnabled, RendererClasses) prefer GetEmitterSummary - it returns those as named fields directly and avoids a JSON parse step. Use this endpoint when you need the full property set or a less-common field. Params: emitterRef
- `niagara(epic_get_emitter_input_values)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns all resolved input values for every module across all four emitter script stacks. One FNiagaraExt_ModuleInputValues entry per module, each carrying all its resolved input values. Call this in parallel with GetEmitterTopology to get both structure and values in two passes. Params: emitterRef
- `niagara(epic_get_emitter_schema)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns property schema for Niagara Emitter. Describes all available properties and their types that can be set on a Niagara Emitter. Params: none
- `niagara(epic_get_emitter_summary)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns lightweight emitter metadata: name, enabled state, sim target, renderer classes. Use this when you only need to check metadata without walking the full emitter structure. Params: emitterRef
- `niagara(epic_get_emitter_topology)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns full emitter topology: four script stacks with all modules and inputs, renderer references. All fields always populated. The returned topology carries no input values; call GetEmitterInputValues in parallel. Params: emitterRef
- `niagara(epic_get_module_input_values)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns resolved input values for a single module. Use when you need values for one specific module without walking the whole emitter. Params: moduleRef
- `niagara(epic_get_module_schema)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns schema for a module and all its inputs. Call this after seeing a module in topology to understand what inputs it exposes. Params: moduleReference?, moduleRef?
- `niagara(epic_get_module_schema_from_asset)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns schema for a module asset. Standalone function that doesn't require a system context - useful for browsing available modules. Params: moduleAsset
- `niagara(epic_get_module_topology)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns module topology: metadata and all inputs (name/type/visibility only, no values). All fields always populated. Params: moduleRef
- `niagara(epic_get_niagara_script_digest)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Assets] Returns the decoded asset-registry tag metadata for a Niagara script asset. Params: objectPath
- `niagara(epic_get_renderer_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns renderer property values. Retrieves the current values of all configurable renderer properties. Params: rendererRef
- `niagara(epic_get_renderer_schema)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns property schema for a specific Renderer class. Describes all available properties and their types for the given renderer type. Params: rendererClass
- `niagara(epic_get_script_stack_input_values)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns all resolved input values for every module in the given script stack. One FNiagaraExt_ModuleInputValues entry per module. Params: scriptRef
- `niagara(epic_get_script_stack_topology)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns script stack topology: all modules and their inputs in execution order. All fields always populated. Params: scriptRef
- `niagara(epic_get_stack_input_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns the value of a stack module input. Retrieves the current value and configuration for a specific module input parameter. Params: stackInputRef
- `niagara(epic_get_stack_input_schema)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns schema for a single module input in the stack. Describes the type, metadata, and configuration options for a specific input parameter. Params: inputReference?, stackInputRef?
- `niagara(epic_get_stack_input_topology)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns stack input topology: name, type, visibility, editability. No value payload. For the resolved value call GetStackInputData. For a dynamic-input chain call GetDynamicInputChain. Params: stackInputRef
- `niagara(epic_get_stack_issues)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns all stack issues (errors, warnings, info) from the Niagara module stack, including dismissed ones. Waits for any in-flight compile to complete before collecting. Params: system
- `niagara(epic_get_system_compile_state)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns the current compile state of a Niagara System: aggregate status, per-script compile events, and summary flags. Waits for any in-flight compile to complete before collecting. Params: system
- `niagara(epic_get_system_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns system property values. Retrieves the current values of all configurable system-level properties. Params: system
- `niagara(epic_get_system_dependencies)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns the four Used* sets (renderers, data interfaces, modules, dynamic inputs) gathered across all emitters and system scripts. These sets are not included in topology structs; call this endpoint separately when needed. Params: system
- `niagara(epic_get_system_schema)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns property schema for Niagara System. Describes all available properties and their types that can be set on a Niagara System. Params: none
- `niagara(epic_get_system_summary)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns lightweight system metadata: name, user variables, and one summary entry per emitter. Use this for first contact with an unfamiliar system. For full structural detail call GetEmitterTopology per emitter. Params: system
- `niagara(epic_get_user_variables)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Component] Returns all user variable values currently set on the component. This retrieves the current values of all user-exposed parameters that can be overridden at the component level. Params: component
- `niagara(epic_get_user_variables__niagara_toolset_system)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Returns all user variables defined on the system. User variables are parameters exposed for external control and can be overridden per component instance. Params: system
- `niagara(epic_get_variable)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Component] Gets the current value of a specific user variable on the component. This retrieves the current value of a user-exposed parameter, including any component-level overrides. Params: component, var
- `niagara(epic_remove_emitter)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Removes an emitter from a system. Deletes the specified emitter and all its associated scripts, modules, and renderers. Params: emitterToRemove?, emitterRef?
- `niagara(epic_remove_module)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Removes a module from a script stack. Deletes the specified module and all its inputs from the script's execution stack. Params: moduleToRemove?, moduleRef?
- `niagara(epic_remove_renderer)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Removes a renderer from an emitter. Deletes the specified renderer from the emitter's renderer list. Params: rendererToRemove?, rendererRef?
- `niagara(epic_remove_set_parameter_entry)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Removes a parameter from an existing SetParameters module by name. The module referenced by ModuleRef must be a SetParameters (UNiagaraNodeAssignment) module. Use bIsSetParametersModule in the module topology to confirm before calling. Params: moduleRef, parameterName
- `niagara(epic_remove_user_variables)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Removes user variables from a system. Deletes the specified user variables from the system's user parameter collection. Params: system, variablesToRemove
- `niagara(epic_set_emitter_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Sets property values on a Niagara Emitter. Applies new values to emitter-level properties based on the provided data structure. Params: emitter?, emitterData, emitterRef?
- `niagara(epic_set_module_enabled)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Sets whether a module is enabled. Disabled modules remain in the stack but don't execute. Current state is visible in module topology. Params: moduleRef, bEnabled
- `niagara(epic_set_niagara_script_description)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Assets] Sets the Description field on a Niagara script's FVersionedNiagaraScriptData. Does not checkout or save the asset but will update the asset registry tags. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: objectPath, newDescription, versionTarget
- `niagara(epic_set_renderer_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Sets property values on a Niagara Renderer. Applies new values to renderer properties based on the provided data structure. Payload shape varies with the concrete renderer class; call GetRendererSchema for the renderer's class to inspect valid properties before writing. Params: renderer?, rendererData, rendererRef?
- `niagara(epic_set_stack_input_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Sets the value of a stack module input and returns the resulting stored value. Updates the value and configuration for a specific module input parameter. Params: stackInputRef, inputData
- `niagara(epic_set_system)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Component] Sets the Niagara System for a component. Use this instead of setting the Asset property directly to ensure proper initialization. Params: niagaraComponent, system, bResetExistingOverrideParameters
- `niagara(epic_set_system_data)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System] Sets property values on a Niagara System. Applies new values to system-level properties based on the provided data structure. Params: system, systemData
- `niagara(epic_set_variable)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Component] Sets the value of a user variable on the component. This overrides the default value of a user-exposed parameter on a specific component instance. Params: component, variable
- `niagara(epic_uenum_info)` - [Epic NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Info] Returns information about a UEnum and all its values. ALWAYS call this when working with a UEnum type to see valid values. Params: enum

## audio

Wraps 44 official tools.

- `audio(epic_add_graph_inputs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Declares graph inputs. Each becomes a node whose ref is the input's own name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, inputs, page?
- `audio(epic_add_graph_outputs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Declares graph outputs. Each becomes a node whose ref is the output's own name. A Source's audio output is already an interface member. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, outputs, page?
- `audio(epic_add_graph_page)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Adds a paged graph. Page must be declared (FindRegisteredPages) and not already carried (the record's Pages). Duplicating copies topology but not graph-input defaults. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page, bDuplicateLastGraph?, bSetAsBuildPage?
- `audio(epic_add_graph_variables)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Declares graph variables, which carry state across blocks. Referenced by name thereafter. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, variables, page?
- `audio(epic_add_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Adds nodes by registered class. Returns each new node's ref in the report's Nodes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, nodes, page?
- `audio(epic_add_variable_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Adds Set / Get / Delayed-Get nodes for already-declared graph variables. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, nodes, page?
- `audio(epic_attach_builder_to_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Attaches an editable builder to an existing MetaSound under BuilderName, targeting Page. The builder is the engine's singleton for that asset, so anything else editing it in this editor shares the state. BuilderName is independent of the asset; any unused name works. Page is stated, not inherited: the page a builder arrives on differs by whether the asset is open, was opened earlier, or never has been, and those are indistinguishable from here. Pass "Default" unless the asset is paged. Fails without attaching on an unknown page, or one the asset does not carry -- AddGraphPage adopts it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, builderName, page
- `audio(epic_build_to_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Writes the builder to a NEW asset on disk. Returns the new asset's object path. On an attached builder this forks a duplicate rather than saving it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, assetName, packagePath, author
- `audio(epic_clear_pins)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Removes every edge touching the named pins, without knowing what is wired to them. An input takes at most one edge; clearing an output severs every consumer it fans out to. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, pins, page?
- `audio(epic_commit_builder_to_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Saves an attached builder's edits back to its source asset and re-registers its graph, so the next play of the asset hears them. Returns the asset's path. Only valid for an attached builder. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName
- `audio(epic_connect)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Wires outputs to inputs. Every edge is resolved before any is made. Direction is not checked beyond type: same-typed pins wired backwards connect cleanly and produce wrong audio. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, connections, page?
- `audio(epic_create_patch_builder)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Creates a Patch builder registered under BuilderName. Persists for the session. No built-in interface; its graph I/O must be declared explicitly. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName
- `audio(epic_create_patch_preset_builder)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Same contract as CreateSourcePresetBuilder, for MetaSoundPatch parents. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: presetName, referencedPatchPath
- `audio(epic_create_source_builder)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Creates a Source builder registered under BuilderName. Persists for the session. The returned record already holds the interface members: an audio output per channel of OutputFormat, OnPlay, and -- when bIsOneShot -- OnFinished and the UE.Source.OneShot interface. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, outputFormat?, bIsOneShot?
- `audio(epic_create_source_preset_builder)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Creates a Source preset over an existing MetaSoundSource. The returned record's inputs are the whole overridable set; topology tools reject this builder. A restricted editor refuses a parent FindMetaSoundAssets would not list. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: presetName, referencedSourcePath
- `audio(epic_describe_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] The whole document in one call: interfaces, graph I/O with defaults, variables, nodes with their pins and authored values, positions, and edges. The detailed read -- creating or attaching a builder reports the same document with its nodes named but not detailed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page?
- `audio(epic_describe_node)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] One node with its pins and the values authored onto them -- the record a document read reports. Errors when the ref names no node; a node that resolves but declares no pins reads back empty. A pin's ValueType is None when the node authored nothing, so the class default applies; DescribeNodeClass reports it. A connected pin uses neither -- the edge feeding it wins. Re-read refs per page: a duplicated page keeps the ids it was copied from. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, node, page?
- `audio(epic_describe_node_class)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundDiscoveryToolset] A registered class's default pins, without adding a node. Resolves the highest version. Errors when no class has that name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: namespace, name, variant
- `audio(epic_detach_builder_from_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Unregisters an attached builder and drops its attachment record. Returns BuilderName. Uncommitted edits are lost. Safe to call when the builder was already unregistered externally. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName
- `audio(epic_diff_pages)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Compares two pages across topology and paged input defaults. Every array empty means they match. Matches by class, not id, so independently built pages compare usefully. Counts are significant: ten wave players against four reports six extras -- how many differ, not which. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, pageA, pageB
- `audio(epic_disconnect)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Removes specific edges. Takes the same records a read reports. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, connections, page?
- `audio(epic_export_wav)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.SoundWaveToolset] Exports a SoundWave to a .wav file on disk. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: soundWave, filePath
- `audio(epic_find_all_connections)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Every edge on Page, addressed by node ref. Edges whose pin names do not resolve against the document are dropped, not reported. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page?
- `audio(epic_find_all_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Every node on Page, interface and graph-I/O nodes included. Read DisplayName and AssetPath to tell what a node is: a nested patch or source registers its class name off the asset's GUID, so ClassName alone does not name it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page?
- `audio(epic_find_builder_interfaces)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Interfaces declared on this builder. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName
- `audio(epic_find_data_types)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundDiscoveryToolset] Registered MetaSound data types matching Pattern, exact name first. TypeName is the spelling every data-type argument on this surface expects. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pattern
- `audio(epic_find_graph_input_defaults)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Graph-input defaults on Page. Inputs with no default there are omitted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page?
- `audio(epic_find_graph_inputs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Graph inputs as Name / DataType, with the default set on Page. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page?
- `audio(epic_find_graph_outputs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Graph outputs as Name / DataType. Outputs carry no default, so Default is empty. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName
- `audio(epic_find_graph_pages)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Pages this asset carries, naming which one mutations target. For the wider set a page can be added from, call FindRegisteredPages. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName
- `audio(epic_find_graph_variables)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Graph variables on Page as Name / DataType / Default. Their Set and Get nodes are in the node listing, named "Variable.Get.<Name>_<id>". Declaring a variable also adds a "Variable.<Name>_<id>" node holding its value, which performs no access. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page?
- `audio(epic_find_interface_input_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Ids of the input nodes an interface contributes on the build page; empty when undeclared. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, interfaceName
- `audio(epic_find_interface_output_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundInspectionToolset] Ids of the output nodes an interface contributes on the build page; empty when undeclared. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, interfaceName
- `audio(epic_find_interfaces)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundDiscoveryToolset] Every registered MetaSound interface, at its highest registered version. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `audio(epic_find_meta_sound_assets)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] MetaSound assets in content, ordered by path. Where preset authoring starts: each can parent a preset, and a preset that is not read-only can be attached and retuned. Lists only what this editor lets the user see. A restricted editor also drops any asset a preset would fail validation from: a preset's chain must end at a built-in MetaSound, not a project's own. The preset creators and AttachBuilderToAsset refuse the same assets there. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pattern, maxResults?, firstResult?
- `audio(epic_find_node_classes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundDiscoveryToolset] Registered node classes matching Pattern, best first, one group per node. Highest versions only; no deprecated classes. No pins: describe the chosen class before wiring it. The session's first call waits on the asset scan, so an empty result means nothing matched. Every term must match the class triple, display name, description, keywords or category. Ranked by match strength, an exact name first; ties rank UE before Experimental. Asset MetaSounds are listed loaded or not, and match by asset name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pattern, inputs, outputs, bIncludeNonAddable?, maxResults?, firstResult?, maxPerGroup?
- `audio(epic_find_registered_pages)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundDiscoveryToolset] Page names the project declares, Default first -- the set AddGraphPage draws from. A name means the same page on every MetaSound. For the pages one asset carries, read Pages off a graph record. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `audio(epic_import_file)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.SoundWaveToolset] Imports an audio file from disk as a SoundWave asset. Accepts the extensions SoundFactory declares: wav, aif, aiff, ogg, flac, opus, mp3. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName, sourceFile, bReplaceExisting?, bSave?
- `audio(epic_layout_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Arranges the targeted page left to right by connection depth, discarding manual arrangement. Applied counts the nodes positioned, which is every node on the page, so zero with bOk set means the page held no nodes rather than that layout declined to run. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page?
- `audio(epic_remove_graph_page)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Removes a paged graph. Removing the targeted page moves the builder to an adjacent one unreported -- read the next result's BuildPage. Default cannot be removed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page
- `audio(epic_remove_graph_variables)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Removes graph variables, every Set and Get node bound to them, and the edges those carried. Refs to those nodes are stale afterwards. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, variables, page?
- `audio(epic_remove_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Removes nodes and every edge touching them. Refs are stale afterwards. Removing an interface-provided node leaves the interface inconsistent. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, nodes, bRemoveUnusedDependencies?, page?
- `audio(epic_set_build_page)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Moves the builder to another page the asset carries. Every later edit lands there. Shared with the MetaSound editor, so an open editor follows. Transacted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, page
- `audio(epic_set_defaults)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MetaSoundToolset.MetaSoundAuthoringToolset] Sets default values on node pins and graph inputs. A node pin on a preset aborts the whole call rather than failing that one item. A value carries no type; each is parsed against the data type of the pin it lands on. Scalars go bare -- "true", "440", "0.5", an object path -- and arrays as JSON. An empty value clears the default rather than setting one. Array values on a node pin do not render in the MetaSound editor. A graph-input default is page-scoped. Each entry's Page names where it lands, empty meaning the targeted page. Unlike the mutators a named page here writes there rather than failing -- the write is addressed, not applied in place. Node-pin defaults ignore Page; there is no paged form. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: builderName, defaults

## widget

Wraps 139 official tools.

- `widget(epic_activate_widget)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Activate a live activatable widget, as if the game had activated it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetName, localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_add_uicomponent)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Adds a UI component of the given class to the named widget. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetName, componentClass. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.AddUIComponent (Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetName, componentClass).
- `widget(epic_add_view_condition)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Adds a condition to the WidgetBlueprint's View: when the source value changes and compares true against Value (Value to MaxValue for a Between operation), the destination is called or set. The widget blueprint is then compiled; compile errors do not fail the call and are reported in the condition's CompileErrors. Not supported during PIE. Values are compared as 32-bit floats. A destination function is called with default parameter values, and a destination property is set to its type's default value, not to the source value. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, sourceContext, sourcePropertyPath, destinationContext, destinationPropertyPath, operation, value, maxValue?
- `widget(epic_add_view_model_property)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Add a property to an existing ViewModel. The ViewModel is compiled automatically when it is next added to a widget or used in a binding. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewModel, propertyName, propertyType, defaultValue
- `widget(epic_add_view_model_to_widget)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Adds a ViewModel to the WidgetBlueprint Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, viewModelClass
- `widget(epic_add_widget)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Adds a widget to the tree at the specified position. Returns full widget info including Slot pointer. When ParentWidget is null and no root exists, the new widget becomes the root of the tree. Use ObjectTools.list_properties on the returned Widget and Slot to get property names before calling set_properties. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetClass, widgetDisplayName, parentWidget?, childIndex?
- `widget(epic_add_widget_to_animation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Add a UWidget (or a UPanelSlot with non-null content) as a possessable binding on a widget animation. Returns a FMovieSceneBindingProxy usable with every SequencerScripting method that takes a binding (MovieSceneBindingExtensions.AddTrack, etc.). ObjectToBind must be either: - A UWidget inside the Widget Blueprint's tree (typical case: animate a child widget's properties). - A UPanelSlot whose Content is non-null (animate slot properties like padding/anchors). The Widget Blueprint must have a compiled GeneratedClass. The implementation compiles the blueprint if needed before binding. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, animation, objectToBind
- `widget(epic_analog_input)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Send an analog axis value along the target user's focus path; 1D axes only, send 0 afterwards to release. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: key, value, userIndex?
- `widget(epic_bind_to_event_property)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Adds a Blueprint event handler graph node bound to a widget's multicast delegate event, Typical events: UButton::OnClicked / OnPressed / OnReleased / OnHovered / OnUnhovered, UCheckBox::OnCheckStateChanged, USlider::OnValueChanged. The matching delegate UPROPERTY must exist on PropertyClass (or a parent of it). Preconditions: - PropertyName must exist in the blueprint. - PropertyClass must be the widget's class (or a parent class) that declares the delegate. Params: widgetBlueprint, eventName, propertyName, propertyClass. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.BindToEventProperty (Params: widgetBlueprint, eventName, propertyName, propertyClass, bShouldJumpToNode?).
- `widget(epic_clear_conversion_function_argument_path)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Removes the property path bound to an input pin of a binding's conversion function, resetting the pin to its default literal, then recompiles the WidgetBlueprint. The compiler does not warn if the cleared pin carried the binding's source value. If the recompile fails the edit is kept; use CompileWidgetBlueprint to read the errors. Not supported during PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, bindingId, argumentName, bSourceToDestination?
- `widget(epic_clear_focus_log)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] Clears all recorded events. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_clear_highlight)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Remove any highlight overlay drawn by Highlight. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_clear_widget_lifetime_log)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.UMGDebuggerSubsystem] Discards all captured events. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_click)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Click a Slate widget identified by its ref. Returns false without clicking when the target point would not receive the event -- something is on top of it, the widget has no arranged size, or MouseDown still holds the button. The log and LastRefusal() name the reason. True means the event was delivered to the widget's branch, not that the widget handled it. Params: ref, button?, doubleClick?, modifiers?, params?
- `widget(epic_compile_widget_blueprint)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Compiles a widget blueprint. Returns false with error details if compilation fails. Errors include missing BindWidget bindings, type mismatches, and graph errors. Call after all widgets and properties are set. Save separately via AssetTools.save_asset. Params: widgetBlueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.CompileWidgetBlueprint (Params: widgetBlueprint).
- `widget(epic_create_and_add_widget)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem] Adds a widget to the tree at the specified position. When ParentWidget is null and no root exists, the new widget becomes the root of the tree. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetClass, widgetDisplayName, parentWidget?, childIndex?
- `widget(epic_create_view_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Creates a View Binding from the SourceProperty to the Destination property. If ConversionName is given, that conversion function is used, even if the property types already match (e.g. to format a FText). Otherwise, if there is a type mismatch, an existing conversion function will try to be used. Property paths resolve against each segment's declared type, so a field that only exists on a subclass of an intermediate object's declared type won't resolve. Bind to the intermediate object and cast inside a conversion function instead. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, sourceContext, sourcePropertyPath, destinationContext, destinationPropertyPath, conversionName
- `widget(epic_create_view_event_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Creates a View Event Binding from the EventProperty to the Destination property. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, eventContext, eventPropertyPath, destinationContext, destinationPropertyPath
- `widget(epic_create_view_model)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Creates a new ViewModel asset Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewModelName, packagePath, parentClass
- `widget(epic_create_widget_animation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Create a new UWidgetAnimation on a Widget Blueprint and return its summary. The empty FWidgetAnimationInfo return signals failure (Animation == nullptr). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, animationName, lengthSeconds?
- `widget(epic_create_widget_blueprint)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Creates a new Widget Blueprint asset. Returns the blueprint or nullptr on failure. Params: folderPath, assetName, parentClass. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.CreateWidgetBlueprint (Params: folderPath, assetName, parentClass).
- `widget(epic_deactivate_widget)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Deactivate a live activatable widget. Same addressing rules as ActivateWidget, but matches only currently-active widgets. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetName, localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_describe_widget)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset.WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset] Full structured description of every widget in the tree (or the subtree rooted at StartWidget). Values are the designer-time state stored in the asset. Anything set at runtime (for example a Blueprint graph changing a slot through SlotAsCanvasSlot, or style state that is not a UPROPERTY) does not appear; use TraceWidgetPropertyOverrides for graph writes. ComposedColors reports the composed pre-display **linear** tint only -- never a display-space value, and never for URichTextBlock/CommonRichTextBlock (a fundamentally different per-inline-tag-run model, excluded from v1 coverage).
