It does not model the contrast setting, DisplayGamma, retainer boxes, Button's DisabledEffect, or widget animations. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, startWidget?, maxDepth?, propertyNameFilter?, bSkipDefaultValues?, bIncludeFullDescription?, bIncludeComposedColors?
- `widget(epic_drag)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Drag from one Slate widget to another (mouse down, stepped move, release). Both ends are hit-tested first; returns false without dragging if either is covered or unarranged. The pointer is stepped along the path, so drag detection fires partway and the target receives OnDragOver, including once at the drop point. Waypoints bend that path, for gestures a straight line would not produce. Start and end positions are normalized within each widget and default to the center. Use them to aim at edge-band drop zones, e.g. EndPosition.Y = 0.1 to drop against the target's top edge. Leave EndRef empty and pass MoveBy or Waypoints to drag StartRef by a distance instead of onto another widget, e.g. splitters, sliders, scrollbars and divider grips. EndPosition is then ignored, and a path that never leaves the press point is refused since the drag would not move. Params: startRef, endRef, modifiers?, params?
- `widget(epic_fill_form)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Fill multiple Slate form fields at once. Params: fields
- `widget(epic_find_widget_animation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Find an animation by internal name. Returns empty info (Animation == nullptr) when not found. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, animationName
- `widget(epic_fixup_mvvmdata)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Attempts to fixup MVVM data. This function can be called if the MVVM Generated Blueprint graphs like conversion functions report compilation errors. It regenerates the graphs to ensure parity with view binding data. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_focus)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Set Slate keyboard/navigation focus to a widget, e.g. before gamepad navigation in PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: ref, userIndex?
- `widget(epic_get_action_bindings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] All UI action bindings currently active (reachable by input) for a player. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_get_activatable_tree)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Snapshot of the CommonUI activatable tree: root nodes, action-domain roots with children and bindings, active root, leafmost active widget, and optionally the full-depth indented tree dump text. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: localPlayerIndex?, bIncludeFullDump?
- `widget(epic_get_container_widget_navigation_doc)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem] Returns navigation/focus documentation for a container widget class - both layout panels (UScrollBox, UVerticalBox, ...) and list views (UListView, UTileView, ...). Reads from the class's Navigation* UCLASS meta and its ancestors', so it needs only a class, not a widget instance. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetClass
- `widget(epic_get_current_focus)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] What has focus RIGHT NOW for a user, queried live from Slate, with its full widget path. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: userIndex?
- `widget(epic_get_event_logging_status)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] The current status of the logger: on/off, event count, dropped count, time span, subscribed players. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_get_event_timeline)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Chronological digest of logged events, oldest first. Never carries callstacks; use GetLoggedEvents when those are wanted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: maxCount?, eventTypeFilter?, localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_get_focus_event_detail)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] The full causal chain for ONE event, located precisely by its sequence number (from any GetFocusEvents/GetFocusTimeline row). FocusReceived -> full path + symbolicated callstack + context + linked input/nav; other event types -> that record bare. bFound=false if evicted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: seq
- `widget(epic_get_focus_events)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] Recent events (newest first), filtered. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetNameFilter, eventTypeFilter, focusCauseFilter?, userIndex?, maxCount?, bIncludePath?
- `widget(epic_get_focus_log_status)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] Recording status: on/off, record count, dropped count, time span. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_get_focus_state)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Where Slate focus actually is for a player and where CommonUI intends it to be: the focused Slate widget, its UMG and owning-activatable identity, the leafmost widget's desired focus target, and whether focus sits inside the active root and the leafmost hierarchy. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_get_focus_timeline)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] Lightweight chronological digest of recorded events (oldest->newest). No paths/callstack. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: maxCount?, eventTypeFilter?, focusCauseFilter?, userIndex?
- `widget(epic_get_input_routing_state)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Everything governing whether input reaches game vs UI for a player: active input mode and config (mouse capture/lock, move/look ignore), the viewport and player controller flags that can block input regardless of CommonUI state, current input device type, input type locks, held keys, leafmost widget, and current Slate focus. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_get_logged_events)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Logged events, filtered. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetNameFilter?, eventTypeFilter?, localPlayerIndex?, maxCount?, bIncludeCallstacks?
- `widget(epic_get_named_slots)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Returns named slot bindings (separate from tree hierarchy). Params: widgetBlueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.GetNamedSlots (Params: widgetBlueprint).
- `widget(epic_get_query_syntax)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Retrieve the complete query grammar, so a Query can be built without guessing. Works with the service stopped. Call this first when unsure of the syntax. The response's Description carries the signature-token legend. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_get_service_status)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Read the service state without changing it, to check it is up or that a start stored something. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_get_umginfo)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Resolve a Slate widget ref back to the UMG UWidget that created it (for UMG and PIE UIs). Returns the UWidget name, its UClass, the owning WidgetBlueprint asset path, the enclosing UUserWidget chain, and a live Widget handle. "resolvedVia" is "self" when the widget itself carries UMG metadata, or "parent" when an ancestor did. On failure Error is set and Widget is null: either the widget is pure Slate with no UMG owner, or its authoring UWidget has since been destroyed. The remaining metadata fields are still filled in for the latter case. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: ref
- `widget(epic_get_view_model_connection_map)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset.WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset] Reverse-searches every Widget Blueprint whose MVVM View references ViewModelClass, and returns each one's own connection map grouped per widget. Each widget's rows are narrowed to just the VMFieldBinding bindings to that exact ViewModelClass; call GetWidgetConnectionMap directly for a widget's whole map. Native (non-Blueprint) ViewModel classes never surface here, since they have no on-disk package identity for the reverse search to find. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewModelClass, scopeFilter?
- `widget(epic_get_widget_animation_bindings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Get all bindings on a widget animation as FMovieSceneBindingProxy entries. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: animation
- `widget(epic_get_widget_class_info)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Returns the Category, Description and if it's a Panel for a single widget class. Same per-entry data as ListWidgetClasses, but lets callers query a class they already have without scanning every UClass. Returns an empty entry if WidgetClass is null. Can be used to get more information on the class from the Description and Category. Params: widgetClass. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.GetWidgetClassInfo (Params: widgetClass).
- `widget(epic_get_widget_connection_map)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset.WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset] Maps every connection on WidgetBlueprint (MVVM field bindings, event bindings, delegates, and legacy property bindings) into one pre-sorted checklist, Unconnected/Misconfigured rows first. Misconfigured only applies to VMFieldBinding rows: Fixable if a conversion function can bridge the types, Unbridgeable if none is allowlisted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, scopeFilter?
- `widget(epic_get_widget_description)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Full property dump of every widget in the tree. Each line: [N] Type Name Prop:Value ... slot:(SlotProp:Value ...) N is the 0-based index into result.Widgets -- use result.Widgets[N] to get the widget ref without text parsing. Same indentation format as GetTaggedWidgetDescription; richer per-widget detail. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: widgetBlueprint, startWidget?, maxDepth?
- `widget(epic_get_widget_lifetime_events)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.UMGDebuggerSubsystem] Returns captured widget lifetime events, newest first. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: nameFilter?, classFilter?, eventTypeFilter?, maxCount?, playerFilter?
- `widget(epic_get_widget_lifetime_log_status)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.UMGDebuggerSubsystem] Returns whether recording is running plus how many events are held and were dropped. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_get_widget_preview_active)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Reads the View Bindings design-time preview toggle state for a WidgetBlueprint. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_get_widget_tree_depth)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Returns the maximum depth of the widget tree. Depth: root with no children = 0; root + children = 1; etc. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: widgetBlueprint, startWidget?
- `widget(epic_get_widget_tree_depth__widget_static_analysis_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset.WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset] Returns the maximum depth of the widget tree. Depth: root with no children = 0; root + children = 1; etc. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, startWidget?
- `widget(epic_get_widget_view_config)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Returns the MVVM View configuration. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_get_widgets)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Returns blueprint info and all widgets in depth-first order. Children within each parent are in their panel slot order - this is the hierarchy order shown in the designer. Info contains ParentClass (pass to CreateWidgetBlueprint) and RootWidgetClass. Use ObjectTools.list_properties on each returned Widget and Slot to get property names before calling set_properties. Params: widgetBlueprint. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.GetWidgets (Params: widgetBlueprint).
- `widget(epic_highlight)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Outline every match in the live UI with the Widget Reflector's debug border, to see what a query selects. Needs a running service. Replaces any current highlight and persists until ClearHighlight or an empty query. Detached matches count in TotalMatchCount but only draw once their tab or window is surfaced. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: request
- `widget(epic_hit_test)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Report whether a widget is reachable by pointer input, without changing anything. Returns JSON {reached, samples, reachedSamples, x, y, reason}, using the same hit test Click, Hover, Drag and Scroll refuse on; `reason` names the obstruction. Grid > 1 samples a Grid x Grid lattice inset inside the widget instead of a single point. `reachedSamples` counts the samples that hit, and x/y locate the first sample that missed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: ref, params?
- `widget(epic_hover)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Hover over a Slate widget, triggering any hover state or tooltip. Returns false without hovering when the target point is covered or the widget is unarranged. Unlike the other action tools, Hover moves the OS cursor, since hover state only lasts while the pointer stays over the widget. On Mac, Click, Drag and Scroll move it too and leave it on the target, because Mac only routes pointer input to the window under the real cursor. A button held by MouseDown stays held. Params: ref, params?
- `widget(epic_key_down)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Press a key and leave it held; pair with KeyUp. Same key specs and gamepad routing as PressKey. A held modifier key (LeftShift, RightControl, LeftAlt, LeftCommand, ...) is down for every later key event of the same Slate user until released, by KeyUp or by a PressKey of the same key. It does not apply to pointer tools, which take their own Modifiers, nor to the platform modifier state FSlateApplication::GetModifierKeys reports. KeyUp or ReleaseHeldInput releases it, as does the hold watchdog after SlateInspectorToolset.HeldInputTimeoutSeconds without a pointer, touch, KeyDown or KeyUp call. Returns true once the press is sent, whether or not a widget handled it, as a held modifier is normally unhandled. False means nothing was sent because the key or user is invalid; LastRefusal() names the reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: key, userIndex?
- `widget(epic_key_up)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Release a key held by an earlier KeyDown for the same Slate user. Same key specs and gamepad routing as PressKey. Returns true once the release is sent, whether or not a widget handled it, as most widgets act on the press. False means nothing was sent because the key or user is invalid; LastRefusal() names the reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: key, userIndex?
- `widget(epic_last_refusal)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Why the last action tool refused, as JSON {seq, tool, target, reason}; seq is 0 when nothing has refused this session. seq increments on each refusal, so sample it before an action and compare afterwards to tell a new refusal from one already read. Click, Hover, Scroll, Drag, Type, Focus, SelectOption, PressKey, KeyDown and KeyUp record refusals, and they record one every time they return false unless Slate is not running. For PressKey that includes a key that was sent but that nothing handled. Read-only tools such as Snapshot and HitTest never change it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_list_bindable_widget_properties)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Lists the bindable property paths available on a widget (binding source/destination candidates). Names aren't filtered by direction -- a read-only property can't be used as a destination and a write-only one can't be used as a source; CreateViewBinding reports that mismatch if it's tried. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, widget
- `widget(epic_list_binding_conversion_options)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Reports whether SourcePropertyPath and DestinationPropertyPath bind directly, and/or which conversion functions bridge them. Parameters mirror CreateViewBinding's Source-/Destination-prefixed parameters. A function-backed leaf field on either side resolves as unbridgeable. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, sourceContext, sourcePropertyPath, destinationContext, destinationPropertyPath
- `widget(epic_list_class_bindable_fields)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Lists every field on TargetClass valid as an MVVM binding source or destination. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: targetClass, accessorClass
- `widget(epic_list_conversion_functions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] List Available Conversion functions for a WidgetBlueprint that bind a Source Property to a Destination Property Includes Pure and Const functions on the WidgetBlueprint itself once it has been compiled. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_list_observers)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] List all active observers as a JSON array for debugging. Each entry includes the observer identifier, whether it is the root observer, the root widget ref (if any), max depth, and cached snapshot size. Params: none
- `widget(epic_list_property_path_bindable_fields)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Resolves PropertyPath against Context and describes that single field. Unlike ListClassBindableFields, this doesn't gate on private/protected -- CreateViewBinding can bind those fields too, so we don't hide them. bIsPrivate/bIsProtected on the result say which case it is. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, context, propertyPath
- `widget(epic_list_view_conditions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] List all conditions on the WidgetBlueprint's View. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_list_view_models)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] List all ViewModel under the search path Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: searchPath
- `widget(epic_list_widget_animations)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] List all animations on a Widget Blueprint. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_list_widget_blueprints)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Lists widget blueprints in a content folder. Params: folderPath. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.ListWidgetBlueprints (Params: folderPath).
- `widget(epic_list_widget_classes)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Lists available widget classes, optionally filtered by name substring. Params: filter. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.ListWidgetClasses (Params: filter?).
- `widget(epic_list_widget_view_bindings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] List all view bindings on the WidgetBlueprint (including possible conversion functions). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_list_widget_view_event_bindings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] List all event bindings currently configured on the WidgetBlueprint's View, along with their context information. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_list_widget_view_events)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] List Available Event Properties for a WidgetBlueprint (i.e. a Multicast Delegate) Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_list_widget_view_models)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] List all view models used by a widget Blueprint Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint
- `widget(epic_mouse_down)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Press a mouse button at a widget's center and leave it held; idempotent for a button this widget already holds. MouseUp or ReleaseHeldInput releases it, as does the hold watchdog after SlateInspectorToolset.HeldInputTimeoutSeconds without a pointer, touch, KeyDown or KeyUp call. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: ref, button?, modifiers?
- `widget(epic_mouse_move)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Move the pointer to an absolute screen position; a button held by MouseDown stays held, so MouseDown/MouseMove/MouseUp drags. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: x, y, modifiers?
- `widget(epic_mouse_move_to)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Move the pointer to a widget's center plus an offset; a held button stays held. Use MouseMove for an absolute position. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: ref, offsetX?, offsetY?, modifiers?
- `widget(epic_mouse_up)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Release a mouse button at the pointer, to its captor or whatever lies under it; safe without a prior MouseDown. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: button?, modifiers?
- `widget(epic_move_uicomponent)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Moves a UI component before or after another component on the same widget. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetName, componentClassToMove, relativeToComponentClass, bMoveAfter. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.MoveUIComponent (Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetName, componentClassToMove, relativeToComponentClass, bMoveAfter).
- `widget(epic_move_widget)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Moves a widget to a new parent panel at the specified position. Returns updated widget info with new Slot. Params: widgetBlueprint, widget, newParent, childIndex?. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.MoveWidget (Params: widgetBlueprint, widget, newParent, childIndex?).
- `widget(epic_observe)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Register an observer on a widget subtree so its refs are continuously kept up to date (~100ms tick). Call this on the window or panel you are about to work with. It ensures new widgets appearing in that subtree are assigned refs automatically. Unobserve when you are done. A shallow root observer (depth 0) already covers top-level windows. Params: ref, maxDepth?
- `widget(epic_press_key)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Press and release a key on the focused Slate widget; gamepad keys route as controller input for the target user. Modifier keys held by KeyDown are down for the press, as with "Ctrl+" prefixes. True means some handler consumed the press, an auto-repeat, its char event or the release, not that the UI changed as intended. Any focusable widget handles Tab, arrow and D-pad keys as a navigation request, so true for them does not mean focus moved; compare Snapshot or HitTest before and after. False means nothing handled the key, or that nothing was sent because the key, user or RepeatCount is invalid; LastRefusal() names the reason, including which widget had focus. In PIE, false can also mean the game viewport passed the key to the game and the game did not consume it. Only BackSpace, Tab, Enter and Escape send a character; use Type to enter text. Params: key, userIndex?, repeatCount?
- `widget(epic_query)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Locate live Slate widgets with a query, whose grammar GetQuerySyntax returns. Needs a running service. Check bSuccess and ErrorMessage first, since a syntax error also returns no matches. TotalMatchCount and bResultTruncated tell an under-constrained query from an exact one. Pass each result's `Ref` to the SlateInspectorToolset tools to act on the widget. Prefer Query and WaitForQuery over the SlateInspectorToolset Snapshot/Observe/Screenshot tools. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: request
- `widget(epic_read_preview_widget_property)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Exports a property value from the live design-time preview widget of an open WidgetBlueprint designer. Use after SetWidgetPreviewActive to assert what the binding preview wrote to the canvas. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetName, propertyName
- `widget(epic_reconstruct_focus_path)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] Reconstructs the causal chain (input -> navigation -> focus + context + callstack) that gave a widget focus. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetName, occurrenceFromEnd?
- `widget(epic_refresh_active_root_focus)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Ask the router to re-apply focus to the active root's desired focus target. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_release_held_input)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Release every button MouseDown still holds, lift every finger TouchStart still holds and release every key KeyDown still holds, returning how many; buttons the user holds are left alone. Also run by the hold watchdog after SlateInspectorToolset.HeldInputTimeoutSeconds (default 100) without a pointer, touch, KeyDown or KeyUp call, at PIE end and at shutdown. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_remove_input_type_lock)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Remove an input type lock previously added with SetInputTypeLock. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: reason?, localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_remove_uicomponent)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Removes a UI component of the given class from the named widget. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetName, componentClass. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.RemoveUIComponent (Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetName, componentClass).
- `widget(epic_remove_view_condition)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Removes a condition from the WidgetBlueprint's View. Not supported during PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, condition
- `widget(epic_remove_widget)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Removes a widget and its children from the tree. Params: widgetBlueprint, widget. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.RemoveWidget (Params: widgetBlueprint, widget).
- `widget(epic_remove_widget_animation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Remove an animation from a Widget Blueprint. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, animation
- `widget(epic_remove_widget_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Remove a binding (by GUID) from a widget animation. Removes both the MovieScene possessable and the UMG binding entry. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: animation, bindingGuid
- `widget(epic_remove_widget_view_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Removes View Binding on the WidgetBlueprint Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, bindingId
- `widget(epic_rename_widget)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Renames a widget. Returns updated widget info or empty on failure. Params: widgetBlueprint, widget, newDisplayName. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.RenameWidget (Params: widgetBlueprint, widget, newDisplayName).
- `widget(epic_rename_widget_animation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Rename an animation. NewName must not collide with an existing animation on the blueprint. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, animation, newName
- `widget(epic_replace_widget_with_child)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Replaces a panel widget with its first child, removing the panel from the tree. The widget to replace must be a UPanelWidget with only one child. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetToReplace. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.ReplaceWidgetWithChild (Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetToReplace).
- `widget(epic_replace_widget_with_named_slot)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Replaces a host widget with the content of one of its named slots. The host must implement INamedSlotInterface (e.g., a UUserWidget exposing named slots). The slot's content widget is moved up to take the host's place in the tree. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetToReplace, namedSlot. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.ReplaceWidgetWithNamedSlot (Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetToReplace, namedSlot, bWarnAndAskUser?).
- `widget(epic_replace_widget_with_template)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Replaces a widget instance in the blueprint's widget tree with a new instance created from a different template widget class. Preserves references for members that exist on both classes with a compatible type/signature: bindings, BP graph variable references, animation bindings, and delegate bindings. Members without a compatible counterpart on the new class are listed in the returned report; references to those members in the outer blueprint will become orphaned graph nodes / dangling bindings. Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetToReplace, templateClass. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.ReplaceWidgetWithTemplate (Params: widgetBlueprint, widgetToReplace, templateClass).
- `widget(epic_screenshot)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Screenshot a Slate widget or an editor window. Prefer this over SceneTools.take_screenshot for Editor UI; use SceneTools only for 3D viewport. Pass a window ref from Windows("list") to capture a specific window; with no ref it captures the active window, falling back to the main editor window when nothing is active. A widget partly outside its window is cropped to the part inside it. Fails with an error naming the reason when there is nothing to capture: an unknown ref, a widget in no visible window, or one entirely outside it. Params: ref
- `widget(epic_scroll)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Scroll the mouse wheel over a Slate widget. Hit-tested like Click; returns false without scrolling if the point is covered or unarranged. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: ref, params?
- `widget(epic_select_option)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Select an option in a Slate combobox by its text label. Opens the dropdown, finds the matching text, and clicks it. Params: ref, value
- `widget(epic_set_binding_mode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Sets the binding mode (OneTimeToDestination/OneWayToDestination/TwoWay/OneWayToSource) for an existing view binding. Not supported during PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, bindingId, mode
- `widget(epic_set_conversion_function_argument_literal)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Sets a literal value on an input pin of a binding's conversion function, then recompiles the WidgetBlueprint. A pin bound to a property path must be cleared first with ClearConversionFunctionArgumentPath. If the recompile fails the value is kept; use CompileWidgetBlueprint to read the errors. Not supported during PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, bindingId, argumentName, value, bSourceToDestination?
- `widget(epic_set_event_binding_argument_literal)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Sets a literal value on an input pin of an event binding, then recompiles the WidgetBlueprint if the edit changed it. A pin bound to a property path is refused. If the recompile fails the value is kept; use CompileWidgetBlueprint to read the errors. Not supported during PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, event, argumentName, value
- `widget(epic_set_input_type_lock)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Lock the effective input type for a player until the lock is removed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: inputType, reason?, localPlayerIndex?
- `widget(epic_set_named_slot_content)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Sets content for a named slot. Returns full widget info including Slot pointer. Params: widgetBlueprint, hostWidget, slotName, widgetClass, widgetName. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.SetNamedSlotContent (Params: widgetBlueprint, slotName, widgetClass, widgetName, hostWidget?).
- `widget(epic_set_tree_visualization)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Toggle an in-viewport overlay that outlines activatable widgets, color-coded by activation/focus state: green bold with a black halo = leafmost active node, green dashed = activated in the active-root chain, yellow dashed = activated outside the active root, dim grey = deactivated (only with bIncludeInactive), cyan = the leafmost widget's desired focus target, orange = the widget actually holding Slate focus, white dashed = active root boundary. Outlines are stacked by Slate paint layer, so the widget CommonUI treats as topmost is drawn on top. Each activatable's label ends in [ActivationFocus] or [NoActivationFocus]; labels of widgets that opt out of activation focus are drawn in magenta. A legend with the active input mode and type is drawn top-left. The overlay is hit-test-invisible, so it does not perturb the input being debugged; it is PIE-only and auto-disables on PIE end. A multi-client session gets one overlay per client window, each showing only the players that window owns. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bEnabled, bIncludeInactive?, bShowLabels?, bLeafmostOnly?, bShowLegend?
- `widget(epic_set_view_condition_operation)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Sets the comparison of an existing condition, then compiles the widget blueprint; compile errors do not fail the call and are reported in the condition's CompileErrors. Not supported during PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, condition, operation, value, maxValue?
- `widget(epic_set_widget_animation_display_label)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Set the user-facing display label shown in the Animations panel. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: animation, displayLabel
- `widget(epic_set_widget_animation_length)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetAnimationToolset.WidgetAnimationToolset] Set the animation playback length in seconds. Updates the MovieScene playback range to [0, LengthSeconds] in tick resolution. LengthSeconds must be > 0. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: animation, lengthSeconds
- `widget(epic_set_widget_preview_active)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Turns the View Bindings design-time preview on or off for a WidgetBlueprint, exactly like the eye toggle on the View Bindings panel toolbar. The WidgetBlueprint's designer must be open for the canvas to update; the state is stored either way. No-ops while the per-user Enable Design-Time View Model Preview editor preference is off. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, bActive
- `widget(epic_set_widget_view_binding_enabled)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Changes the enabled and compile flag for an existing widget view binding. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, bindingId, bEnabled?, bCompile?
- `widget(epic_set_widget_view_config)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MVVMToolset.MVVMToolset] Writes the MVVM View configuration on the WidgetBlueprint's View. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, configuration
- `widget(epic_show_widget_lifetime_log_window)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.UMGDebuggerSubsystem] Opens or focuses the Widget Lifetime Log editor tab, which shows the same recording. Returns false without a Slate UI. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_snapshot)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Capture a Slate UI accessibility snapshot. Use this to read the current widget tree and discover refs for action tools (Click, Type, Hover, etc.). A shallow root observer (depth 0) covers top-level windows automatically. Before interacting with a specific window or panel, call Observe() on it to get deep coverage, then Snapshot that subtree to see its contents. Refs discovered by a previous Snapshot remain usable. You do NOT need to call Snapshot again before every action. Each [pos=X,Y size=W,H] is the widget's on-screen bounds in desktop pixels, whatever the DPI scale or rotation, the same space as the pointer and touch tools' screen positions and offsets. Params: ref, maxDepth?, bIncludeSourceLocations?
- `widget(epic_start_event_logging)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Begin logging CommonUI events (widget activation/deactivation/rebuild, input mode/config changes, input method changes, container displayed-widget/transition changes) into an in-memory ring buffer. Restarting clears the buffer. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bCaptureCallstacks?, bufferSize?
- `widget(epic_start_focus_recording)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] Starts recording Slate focus and navigation events into an in-memory buffer, discarding events already captured. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bCaptureCallstack?, bCaptureContext?, bufferSize?
- `widget(epic_start_service)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Begin mirroring the live Slate widget tree into the store, so Query/Highlight/WaitForQuery can work. The service is off by default. Call this once before locating widgets and leave it running for the session. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_start_widget_lifetime_logging)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.UMGDebuggerSubsystem] Starts recording Constructed, Destructed and Rebuilt events for every user widget, discarding events already captured. Query them with GetWidgetLifetimeEvents. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bufferSize?, bCaptureInvalidations?
- `widget(epic_stop_event_logging)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CommonUIToolset.CommonUIToolset] Stop logging; the buffer is retained for querying. Does nothing when not logging. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_stop_focus_recording)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateDebugger.SlateDebuggerSubsystem] Stops recording. The buffer is retained for querying. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_stop_service)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Stop mirroring and empty the store, ending the scanning cost. Pending WaitForQuery calls fail at once, and the query tools fail until StartService. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_stop_widget_lifetime_logging)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic UMGEditor.UMGDebuggerSubsystem] Stops recording. Captured events stay queryable until ClearWidgetLifetimeLog or the next start. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `widget(epic_toggle_widget_as_variable)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Sets the bIsVariable flag. Params: widgetBlueprint, widget, bIsVariable. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.ToggleWidgetAsVariable (Params: widgetBlueprint, widget, bIsVariable, bMarkBlueprintModified?).
- `widget(epic_touch_end)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Lift a finger held by an earlier TouchStart at an absolute screen position. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: x, y, touchIndex?
- `widget(epic_touch_move)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Move a finger held by an earlier TouchStart to an absolute screen position. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: x, y, touchIndex?
- `widget(epic_touch_start)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Put a finger down and leave it there; it shares the left mouse button with the cursor, so do not mix it with a held MouseDown. TouchEnd or ReleaseHeldInput lifts it, as does the hold watchdog after SlateInspectorToolset.HeldInputTimeoutSeconds without a pointer, touch, KeyDown or KeyUp call. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: ref, x?, y?, touchIndex?
- `widget(epic_trace_widget_property_overrides)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset.WidgetStaticAnalysisToolset] Traces every statically-discoverable write to a widget property across the whole tree, merging Blueprint-graph writes with static origins (class defaults, archetype overrides, bindings). Results are grouped per (widget, property) pair. The highest-Order Applied entry is the Winner; earlier entries are Redundant, later ones Conflict. With no Applied entry, the highest-Order entry is an uncertain best guess and all are Conflict. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: widgetBlueprint, propertyNameFilter?, widgetNameFilter?
- `widget(epic_type)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Type text into a Slate text input widget, focusing it first; use PressKey for keys that are not characters. Params: ref, text, submit?
- `widget(epic_unobserve)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Remove an observer by its identifier. Params: identifier
- `widget(epic_wait_for)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] Check once whether text is present or absent in the Slate widget tree (substring match); poll to wait. Params: text?, textGone?
- `widget(epic_wait_for_query)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic WidgetFinder.WidgetFinderSubsystem] Wait, without polling, until a query's raw match count meets Comparison/Threshold, then return the matches. Prefer it over repeated Query calls and over the SlateInspectorToolset Snapshot/Observe/Screenshot tools. Completes once, with bConditionMet, or with bTimedOut after TimeoutSeconds, which still counts as success. A bad query, a stopped service, or stopping the service mid-wait fails the call. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: request
- `widget(epic_windows)` - [Epic SlateInspectorToolset.SlateInspectorToolset] List, select, close or resize top-level Slate editor windows. "list" returns a JSON array of {index, ref, title, active, regular, x, y, width, height}. Pass the ref to Screenshot or Snapshot to target that window; the index is positional and changes as windows open and close, the ref does not. "resize" and "maximize" also work under -RenderOffScreen. Params: index?, width?, height?, ref?, input? (carries action, which cannot be sent at the top level)
- `widget(epic_wrap_widgets)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic UMGToolSet.UMGToolSet] Wraps one or more widgets in a new panel widget of the specified class. Only the root-most widgets in the selection are wrapped - children of other selected widgets are skipped because their parent will be wrapped. Returns info for each newly created wrapper. Use ObjectTools.list_properties on each returned Widget and Slot to discover property names before calling set_properties (padding, alignment, anchors, etc. vary per panel class). Params: widgetBlueprint, widgets, wrapperClass. On UE 5.9 it calls UMGEditor.WidgetBlueprintEditorSubsystem.WrapWidgets (Params: widgetBlueprint, widgets, wrapperClass).

## editor

Wraps 101 official tools.

- `editor(epic_add_assets_to_collection)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Adds assets to the specified collection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: collection, assetPaths
- `editor(epic_call_cheat)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CheatsToolset.CheatsToolset] Runs a cheat on the local player, exactly as if it were typed into the console. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: cheatName, args
- `editor(epic_capture)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Renders offscreen from the given world and camera using the specified mode. Use ListModes for names available in this build and session; DescribeMode supplies interpretation and sub-modes. Unknown or unavailable modes raise a script error. Mode names are case-sensitive. Availability does not validate scene content or every pass prerequisite. Uses world post-process volumes, with no temporal history or camera-specific post-process overrides. This is not a live viewport screenshot. PNG output is an opaque, display-encoded 8-bit visualization, not a raw or HDR buffer export. Capture timings are not representative game-frame timings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world, camera, mode, options
- `editor(epic_capture_active_editor_view)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Captures the active editor camera offscreen using the requested mode, without changing the viewport. Repeated calls follow viewport movement; use GetActiveEditorView plus Capture for a fixed camera. Mode, Options, output format and rendering limits are the same as Capture. Does not reproduce temporal history, editor overlays or camera-specific post-processing. This is not a viewport screenshot. Use DescribeMode for diagnostic guidance. Raises a script error if no active editor level viewport is available. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mode, options
- `editor(epic_capture_asset_image)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Renders a thumbnail for the specified asset (e.g. static meshes, skeletal meshes, skeletons, animations, montages, materials, textures). Params: assetPath
- `editor(epic_capture_editor_image)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Captures an image of the entire editor application as the user sees it. Params: none
- `editor(epic_capture_editor_viewport)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Redraws and captures an editor viewport with its view and post-process state: a level viewport or an asset editor viewport such as the Static Mesh or Skeletal Mesh Editor's. Restores camera and show flags on return; temporal history may advance or be affected by camera overrides. Asymmetric projections return the image with a warning and no camera snapshot or image FOV. Raises a script error for an unavailable/unreadable viewport or unsupported HDR encoding. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: captureTransform?, annotations?, bShowUI?, viewportId?
- `editor(epic_capture_game_viewport)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Captures the current Game or PIE viewport as Slate draws it, with UMG and any other UI over the viewport, such as the recording border. In PIE it redraws the window that holds the viewport; if that fails, the image shows the scene only, with a warning. Metadata records the world and request time, not the originating GPU frame, camera, mode or internal resolution. Output is an opaque 8-bit PNG, not a raw or HDR buffer export. PQ/scRGB HDR uses the engine screenshot decoder and sRGB8 image conversion. Conversion clips highlights and out-of-gamut channels without an additional tone curve. A warning identifies this conversion. Other HDR output encodings are rejected. Raises a script error if the host cannot render, or if no initialized, readable Game/PIE framebuffer is available. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world, maxResolution
- `editor(epic_capture_viewport)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Captures the level viewport with optional annotations. Annotations rendering overlays a projected 3D world-space grid plus name + position labels on visible actors. The grid is drawn at a configurable ground-plane Z and projected through the camera, with coordinate numbers at intersections (shown in meters). Each labeled actor gets a crosshair at its projected screen position with a leader-line callout placed to avoid overlap. This gives a vision-capable agent spatial awareness: it can reference grid coordinates to direct placement and identify scene contents by label. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: captureTransform?, annotations?, bShowUI?
- `editor(epic_center_view)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Re-centres the Rewind Debugger visible view range on Time, preserving the current view width. Independent of the scrub cursor: pair with SetScrubTime when you want to both move the cursor and bring it into the centre of the visible timeline. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: time
- `editor(epic_close_editor_for_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Closes every asset editor open for the specified asset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, unsavedChanges
- `editor(epic_create_collection)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Creates a new static collection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: collectionName, shareType
- `editor(epic_describe_mode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Describes a mode's purpose, interpretation, limits, registered sub-modes and default. Accepts the same names and aliases as Capture. Unknown names raise a script error. Availability covers capture-path and session prerequisites, not per-world content or pass activation. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: mode
- `editor(epic_destroy_collection)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Deletes the specified collection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: collection
- `editor(epic_execute_python)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.python_scripting.PythonScriptingTools] Runs a Python script in the editor's interpreter on the game thread. IMPORTANT: Call `read_me` once per conversation before using this tool, for full usage instructions -- including how to compose other tools and yield control during long-running work. Look up each `unreal` API with search_symbols or get_symbol before calling it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: script
- `editor(epic_exit_editor)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Requests a shutdown of the editor application. The request is queued for the next editor tick so this call can return first; the process then exits and no further tool calls are possible. Errors if any level or content package has unsaved changes and bDiscardUnsavedChanges is false. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bDiscardUnsavedChanges?
- `editor(epic_focus_on_actors)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Repositions the level editor camera to focus on the specified actors. Cannot be called while PIE is active. Params: actors
- `editor(epic_get_active_editor_modes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Returns the IDs of the currently active editor modes. More than one mode can be active at once; the default placement mode ("EM_Default") is usually present. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_active_editor_view)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Snapshots a level editor viewport's world, camera and clipping planes: ViewportId's, or the active one. Asset editor viewports are refused, since Capture cannot render their preview worlds. Pass World and Camera to Capture. Match SourceDimensions' aspect ratio to retain framing; letterboxing is excluded. The snapshot does not follow viewport movement. Requires an interactive editor session and an active level viewport. Raises a script error if the viewport is unavailable, has zero size or uses an unsupported projection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewportId?
- `editor(epic_get_asset_editor_selection)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Gets what is selected inside the asset editor for the given asset (e.g. selected graph nodes in a Blueprint or Material editor, selected widgets in a UMG editor, and objects shown in the editor's details panels). This is distinct from level-actor selection (GetSelectedActors) and content-browser selection (GetSelectedAssets). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset
- `editor(epic_get_asset_thumbnails)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Returns the assets' existing cached thumbnails (the images the Content Browser shows) without loading or rendering the assets. On success the result's value holds one image per requested path, in request order; an entry is an empty image when the asset has no saved thumbnail or its thumbnail could not be decoded. The call completes with an error instead if any requested path cannot be found. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPaths
- `editor(epic_get_camera_transform)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Returns the position and rotation of the level viewport camera. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_collection_assets)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Returns the assets in the specified collection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: collection
- `editor(epic_get_content_browser_path)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Gets the current path of the active content browser. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_csv_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.CsvProfilerToolset] Surveys a capture without returning per-frame values. StatGroups (the group histogram) is always populated; individual stat names only when StatNameFilter is set. GroupDepth groups the histogram by the first N segments of the '/'-delimited stat tree: depth 1 buckets under "Exclusive", depth 2 under "Exclusive/GameThread". Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, statNameFilter?, bIncludeAllMetadata?, groupDepth?
- `editor(epic_get_cvar_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConsoleToolset.ConsoleToolset] Reads the current value of a console variable as its canonical string representation (e.g. "true"/"false" for bools, "1.5" for floats). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: name
- `editor(epic_get_frame_timings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.StatsToolset] Reads the engine's per-frame timing globals once (no smoothing - usually the previous frame). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_frame_tree)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.StatsToolset] Captures the latest stat frame as a nested tree. Siblings sorted most-expensive first. Top-level entries are typically thread roots (GameThread, RenderThread). On leaves, ExclusiveMs equals InclusiveMs. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: minInclusiveMs, maxDepth, pathFilter
- `editor(epic_get_log_categories)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LogsToolset] Returns a sorted list of registered log categories. Params: filter. On UE 5.9 it calls ConsoleToolset.LogsToolset.GetLogCategories (Params: filter?, logFilePath?).
- `editor(epic_get_log_entries)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LogsToolset] Returns log entries from the current session's log file. Params: category?, pattern, maxEntries?. On UE 5.9 it calls ConsoleToolset.LogsToolset.GetLogEntries (Params: logCategory?, pattern?, maxEntries?, logFilePath?, maxCharsPerEntry?).
- `editor(epic_get_object_events)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Returns all events that fired on a recorded object within a time range. Events represent discrete occurrences such as Linguine prompt executions. Returns {"Events": []} when no session is active or no events exist in the range. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: objectId, startTime, endTime
- `editor(epic_get_object_properties)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Returns the recorded property state of an object at or before a given time. Uses closest-before semantics: returns the most recent snapshot at or before Time. Returns {} when no session is active or no snapshot exists before Time. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: objectId, time
- `editor(epic_get_open_assets)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Gets the list of assets currently open in asset editors. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_pivot_location)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Gets the current location of the editor's transform-gizmo pivot. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_recent_transactions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UndoHistoryToolset] Returns transactions from the undo history, newest first. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: count?, offset?
- `editor(epic_get_selected_actors)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Gets the currently selected actors in the level editor. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_selected_assets)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Gets the list of assets selected in the content browser. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_selected_object)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Returns the object currently selected in the Rewind Debugger outliner, if any. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_selected_outliner_folders)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Gets the folder paths selected across all open scene outliners. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_session_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Returns the current Rewind Debugger session state. Call this first to confirm a session is active before querying object data. Always returns all fields; IsActive is false when no session is loaded. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_get_show_flag)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Reads whether a named engine show flag is currently enabled on the active level editor viewport. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: flagName
- `editor(epic_get_stat_counters)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.StatsToolset] Samples every non-cycle counter in a stat group for a single frame: integer/float counters and memory pool sizes (draw calls, primitive counts, LLM tags, etc). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: groupName, bIncludeZero
- `editor(epic_get_symbol)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.python_scripting.PythonScriptingTools] Returns the documentation of a registered tool, or of a type, function, method, or property in the `unreal` Python API. Pass a toolset or full tool name, a top-level name ('EditorActorSubsystem', 'log'), or a qualified 'Class.member'. A toolset returns its description and the signature of each of its tools. A tool returns its signature, description, and parameters. A function or member returns its signature and full documentation. A type returns its description, then one line for each editor property (inherited ones included), enum value, and method it declares, and how many methods it inherits from each base; get_symbol on 'Type.member' or on a base gives the details. A struct only tools use returns its fields and the tools that use it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: name
- `editor(epic_get_trace_event_details)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Returns the full decoded JSON for a single trace event from a named source. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: source, eventId
- `editor(epic_get_transaction_details)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UndoHistoryToolset] Returns detailed information about a single transaction Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: transactionId, maxObjects?
- `editor(epic_get_transaction_recency_index)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UndoHistoryToolset] Returns how far back a transaction is in the undo history, counted newest-first: 0 is the most recent transaction, 1 the next, and so on. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: transactionId
- `editor(epic_get_verbosity)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LogsToolset] Returns the current verbosity level for a log category. Params: category?. On UE 5.9 it calls ConsoleToolset.LogsToolset.GetVerbosity (Params: logCategory).
- `editor(epic_get_video_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MediaToolset.MediaToolset] Reads container metadata. Windows Media Foundation is required. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path
- `editor(epic_get_visible_actors)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Returns all actors in the current level whose bounds intersect the viewport frustum. Params: none
- `editor(epic_is_pierunning)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] True for active PIE or simulation, including online login before world creation. A queued start alone returns false. Params: none
- `editor(epic_list_cheats)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic CheatsToolset.CheatsToolset] Lists the cheat commands available on the local player. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: nameFilter?
- `editor(epic_list_collections)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Lists all collections in the content browser. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_list_editor_modes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Lists the IDs of all registered editor modes (e.g. "EM_Default", "EM_Landscape", "EM_Foliage"), ordered by mode priority. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_list_editor_viewports)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Lists the open level and asset editor viewports, with ids to pass as Options.ViewportId or to CaptureEditorViewport. Only a viewport with bIsVisible set presents frames, so ask the user to bring any other to the front first. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_list_events)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.CsvProfilerToolset] Returns the capture's events with their frame indices. Repeats of the same event name on one frame collapse into a single entry carrying a Count. FrameEnd -1 runs to the end; the defaults return the whole run. At most Count events (the first in frame order) are returned. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, count?, frameStart?, frameEnd?
- `editor(epic_list_modes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Lists canonical names of modes that can capture in this build and session. Aliases are not listed separately. Availability reflects capture-path, build, session and project prerequisites, not scene content. Buffer-visualization targets incompatible with the capture output format are omitted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bIncludeUnavailable
- `editor(epic_list_object_trace_events)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Lists trace events attributed to ObjectId within [StartTime, EndTime], aggregated across every registered IRewindDebuggerToolsetEventDecoder. Returns lightweight rows; call GetTraceEventDetails to decode a specific event in full. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: objectId, startTime, endTime, sourceFilter, includedNameFilter, excludedNameFilter, limit, offset
- `editor(epic_list_objects)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Lists all objects recorded in the current session, optionally filtered by class name. Each entry includes an ObjectId for use with GetObjectEvents and GetObjectProperties. Returns {"Objects": []} when no session is active or no objects match the filter. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: className?
- `editor(epic_list_show_flags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Lists every engine show flag with its display name and editor group. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_list_stat_groups)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.StatsToolset] Enumerates registered stat groups. Names are STATGROUP_-prefixed and match the argument accepted by GetStatCounters. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_list_viewport_recordings)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Lists this session's viewport recordings, including the active one. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_list_views)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Snapshots actors with active camera components and player camera managers in the given world. Preserves custom perspective near planes and explicitly configured orthographic clip planes. A player view whose transform, active lens and clipping match a listed view marks that entry as a player view instead of adding one. Lists player views first, then the remaining actor cameras. Returns empty when no views exist; an invalid world raises a script error. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world
- `editor(epic_list_worlds)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Lists Editor, PIE and Game worlds in the engine's world contexts, including non-renderable worlds. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_open_editor_for_asset)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Opens an asset editor for the specified asset. Params: assetPath
- `editor(epic_read_image_file)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MediaToolset.MediaToolset] Reads up to 64 MiB of encoded PNG, JPEG, GIF, BMP, or WebP data without re-encoding. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path
- `editor(epic_read_me)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.python_scripting.PythonScriptingTools] Usage instructions for execute_python. Call this once per conversation before using execute_python. Explains the execution environment (namespace, async/await, composing other tools) and, only when relevant, the spatial coordinate-conversion helpers available in the current editor session. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `editor(epic_read_stat_values)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.CsvProfilerToolset] Returns up to Count per-frame values of one stat, starting at FrameStart. An over-large Count clamps to the stat's own length rather than erroring; Count = -1 reads to the end of the column. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, statName, frameStart?, count?
- `editor(epic_record_viewport)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Records a viewport for DurationSeconds, then completes with the finalized file. Canceling the call stops the recording early and keeps the file. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: options, durationSeconds
- `editor(epic_reduce_stat)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.CsvProfilerToolset] Reduces each requested stat to average, min, max, total, and the P50/P90/P95/P99 percentiles over a frame-index range. FrameEnd -1 runs to the end; the defaults reduce over the whole run. Stats not present in the capture are omitted from the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, statNames, frameStart?, frameEnd?
- `editor(epic_remove_assets_from_collection)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Removes assets from the specified collection. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: collection, assetPaths
- `editor(epic_run_console_command)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConsoleToolset.ConsoleToolset] Executes an arbitrary UE exec command. Output is written to the session log. Python is refused: any command containing "py" as a whole word (the py command, including when wrapped by defer, redirecttofile, etc.) is rejected. Run Python with the Python scripting tool instead. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: command
- `editor(epic_sample_frames)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MediaToolset.MediaToolset] Samples midpoint-spaced frames in a time range. Windows only. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, startSeconds, endSeconds, maxFrames?, maxEdge?
- `editor(epic_screen_coords_to_world)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Finds the world position of the nearest solid object at a given set of normalized view space coords. Params: coords, traceDistance?
- `editor(epic_search_console_commands)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConsoleToolset.ConsoleToolset] Returns exec commands whose name contains the search string (case-insensitive). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: name
- `editor(epic_search_cvars)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Finds all console variables that contain a given name. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: name
- `editor(epic_search_cvars__console_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConsoleToolset.ConsoleToolset] Finds all console variables that contain a given name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: name
- `editor(epic_search_symbols)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.python_scripting.PythonScriptingTools] Searches the registered tools and the `unreal` Python API for types, functions, methods, and properties. Searches what this editor session provides, so every result exists here. Feed a result's qualified_name to get_symbol to read it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pattern?, max_results?, match?, kinds?, accepts?, returns?
- `editor(epic_search_transactions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.UndoHistoryToolset] Searches the undo history by transaction label using a fuzzy (edit-distance) match, closest match first. When two transactions match a query equally well the more recent one is returned first. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: query, count?, offset?
- `editor(epic_select_actors)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Selects the specified actors in the current scene. Params: actors
- `editor(epic_select_asset_editor_objects)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Sets what is selected inside the asset editor for the given asset, replacing the current selection. Supported for Blueprint editors (graph nodes) and Widget editors (widgets and graph nodes); raises for material editors and other asset types whose selection cannot be set. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset, objects
- `editor(epic_select_assets)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Selects the specified assets in the content browser. Completes once the content browser has applied the selection. Params: assetPaths
- `editor(epic_select_object)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Selects a recorded object in the Rewind Debugger panel and opens the details panel so its state and timelines are visible to the user. Use after ListObjects to surface a particular object when reporting findings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: objectId
- `editor(epic_select_outliner_folders)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Selects the specified folders in every open scene outliner. Replaces the existing folder selection; non-folder selections (e.g. selected actors) are preserved. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPaths
- `editor(epic_set_camera_transform)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Sets the position and/or rotation of the level viewport camera. Anything not provided keeps its current value. Params: transform?, translation?, rotation?
- `editor(epic_set_content_browser_path)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Navigates the active content browser to the specified folder path. Params: path
- `editor(epic_set_cvar_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic ConsoleToolset.ConsoleToolset] Sets the value of a console variable. The value is parsed according to the variable's declared type. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: name, value
- `editor(epic_set_editor_mode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Activates the specified editor mode. Pass "EM_Default" (or an empty string) to return to the default placement/select mode. Cannot be used during PIE. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: modeId
- `editor(epic_set_pivot_location)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Sets the location of the editor's transform-gizmo pivot. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: location, bSnapToGrid?
- `editor(epic_set_scrub_time)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic RewindDebuggerToolset.RewindDebuggerToolset] Moves the Rewind Debugger scrub cursor to a specific recording time. The engine clamps to the recording's range. Disables auto-scroll-with-recording so the timeline stays put. Does not re-centre the visible view range; pair with CenterView to do that. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: time
- `editor(epic_set_show_flag)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Toggles a named engine show flag on the active level editor viewport (e.g. "Navigation", "Collision", "Grid", "Bounds", "Fog", "Landscape", "Volumes"). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: flagName, bEnabled
- `editor(epic_set_verbosity)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic EditorToolset.LogsToolset] Sets the verbosity level for a log category. Params: category?, verbosity. On UE 5.9 it calls ConsoleToolset.LogsToolset.SetVerbosity (Params: logCategory, verbosity).
- `editor(epic_set_viewport_view_mode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Sets the view mode of the active level editor viewport (e.g. Lit, Unlit, Wireframe, Lighting-only, complexity visualizations). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: viewMode
- `editor(epic_show_notification)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Shows a transient editor notification (Slate toast, bottom-right). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: message, state?, expireDuration?
- `editor(epic_skim_video)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic MediaToolset.MediaToolset] Samples midpoint-spaced frames over the video's known duration. Windows only. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, maxFrames?, maxEdge?
- `editor(epic_start_pie)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Starts PIE or simulation in the current level, then waits for warmup. Local single-player worlds usually exist at PostPIEStarted; deferred online login delays warmup until a world exists. The whole request has a 30-second timeout. Errors if a session is active or queued. Params: options?
- `editor(epic_start_viewport_recording)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Starts recording a viewport to an MP4 as the user sees it and returns at once; StopViewportRecording finishes it. One recording runs at a time, and it also ends on its own at MaxDurationSeconds or when the viewport goes away. The red border at the frame edges is the recording indicator, not scene content. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: options
- `editor(epic_stop_pie)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Stops PIE or simulation and waits for shutdown. Normally handled on an editor tick; pending online login defers shutdown until a world exists. Errors if no session is active. Params: none
- `editor(epic_stop_viewport_recording)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SceneCaptureToolset.SceneCaptureToolset] Stops a recording and completes once its file is finalized. A recording that already ended returns its result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: recordingId
- `editor(epic_top_stats)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.CsvProfilerToolset] Ranks the capture's stats by a reduced aggregate (average, max, total, or a P50/P90/P95/P99 percentile) over a frame range and returns the top Count, each already reduced. A StatNameFilter wildcard restricts the ranking to a group (e.g. "GPU*"); empty ranks all stats. FrameEnd -1 runs to the end; the defaults rank the whole run. Ranking is by raw aggregate value with no unit normalisation, so an unfiltered ranking mixes unit domains (byte counters rank alongside millisecond timings). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: path, count?, rankBy?, statNameFilter?, frameStart?, frameEnd?
- `editor(epic_trigger_gpuprofile)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PerfToolset.GpuProfilerToolset] Captures the next rendered frame's GPU work as a nested tree (2-3 frame latency). Top-level entries are GPU queues (Graphics, Async Compute); each contains its passes as a recursive subtree. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: minInclusiveMs
- `editor(epic_world_pos_to_screen_coords)` - [Epic EditorToolset.EditorAppToolset] Converts a world-space position into normalized screen space based on the editor viewport camera. Params: position

## reflection

Wraps 7 official tools.

- `reflection(epic_get_class)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.object.ObjectTools] Returns the class of an Unreal object. Params: instance. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ObjectToolset.GetClass (Params: instance).
- `reflection(epic_get_properties)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.object.ObjectTools] Returns the values of one or more properties on an object. Params: instance, properties. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ObjectToolset.GetProperties (Params: instance, properties).
- `reflection(epic_get_property_schema)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic EditorToolset.ObjectToolset] Returns a JSON Schema of the properties on an object, with each property's type and description. Much larger than ListProperties, so find the property there and come here only when its type is needed. A schema describes types rather than an instance, so Root cannot carry a subscript. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: instance, root?, maxDepth?
- `reflection(epic_list_properties)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.object.ObjectTools] Returns a list of properties that are on the specified object. Params: instance. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ObjectToolset.ListProperties (Params: instance, root?, nameFilter?, maxDepth?).
- `reflection(epic_reset_properties)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.object.ObjectTools] Resets one or more properties on an object to their default values, removing any per-instance overrides. Params: instance, properties. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ObjectToolset.ResetProperties (Params: instance, properties).
- `reflection(epic_search_subclasses)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.object.ObjectTools] Finds all subclasses of a given class. Params: base_class, class_name. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ObjectToolset.SearchSubclasses (Params: baseClass, className?).
- `reflection(epic_set_properties)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic editor_toolset.toolsets.object.ObjectTools] Sets the values of properties on an object. Params: instance, values. On UE 5.9 it calls EditorToolset.ObjectToolset.SetProperties (Params: instance, values).

## gameplay

Wraps 99 official tools.

- `gameplay(epic_add_blackboard_key)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Adds a key to the blackboard. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blackboard, keyName, keyType, bInstanceSynced?
- `gameplay(epic_add_body)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Adds a new empty body for the given bone. Params: physicsAsset, boneName
- `gameplay(epic_add_composite)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Adds a composite as a child of a composite (or root) parent. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: parent, compositeClass, index?
