- `gameplay(epic_add_constraint)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Adds a new constraint between two bodies. Both bodies must already exist. Params: physicsAsset, bone1Name, bone2Name
- `gameplay(epic_add_decorator)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Adds a decorator subnode to an owner. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: owner, decoratorClass, index?
- `gameplay(epic_add_option)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Adds an option to the query. An option wraps one generator that produces the items to score. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: query, generatorClass, index?
- `gameplay(epic_add_root_composite)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Adds the tree's single root composite. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: behaviorTree, compositeClass
- `gameplay(epic_add_root_decorator)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Adds a root-level decorator, used when the tree is referenced as a subtree. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: behaviorTree, decoratorClass, index?
- `gameplay(epic_add_service)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Adds a service subnode to a composite owner. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: owner, serviceClass, index?
- `gameplay(epic_add_tag)` - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Adds a new gameplay tag to the project. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Params: tagName, comment?, tagSource, devComment?
- `gameplay(epic_add_tag_source)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Creates a new tag list source that tags can then be added to. Its file is written when the first tag is added. No-ops if a tag list source with that name already exists in the same directory. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: sourceName, tagRoot
- `gameplay(epic_add_task)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Adds a task as a child of a composite parent. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: parent, taskClass, index?
- `gameplay(epic_add_test)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Adds a test to an option. Tests filter and score the option's generated items. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: option, testClass, index?
- `gameplay(epic_apply_input)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic InputToolset.InputToolset] Presses one or more actions/keys together, holds them for DurationSeconds, then releases them, completing once the release is done. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: entries, durationSeconds
- `gameplay(epic_create_behavior_tree)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Creates a new BehaviorTree asset, ready to edit. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName
- `gameplay(epic_create_blackboard_data)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Creates a new BlackboardData asset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName
- `gameplay(epic_create_environment_query)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Creates a new Environment Query asset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName
- `gameplay(epic_create_from_mesh)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Creates a physics asset from a skeletal mesh, auto-generating collision bodies for each bone. The asset is placed in the same folder as the mesh with the suffix "_PhysicsAsset". Params: meshPath, bAssignToMesh
- `gameplay(epic_describe_nav_mesh)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Dumps the full generation and query configuration of one navmesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: bIncludeNonDefaultProperties, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_describe_poly_at_location)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Describes the navmesh polygon nearest a location. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: point, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_find_distance_to_wall)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Measures the distance from a point to the nearest navmesh wall (walkable-space boundary). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: point, maxDistance, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_find_path)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Finds a path between two points. Area cost overrides and exclusions apply to this query only; no state is touched. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: start, end, areaCostOverrides, excludedAreaClasses, bAllowPartialPath, costLimit, maxPathPoints, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_find_referencers_by_tag)` - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Returns assets that reference a gameplay tag. Params: tagName
- `gameplay(epic_get_actor_nav_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Deep navigation report for one actor: everything navigation-relevant it carries and, when it follows paths, where it is going. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: actor, bIncludeNonDefaultProperties
- `gameplay(epic_get_allowed_blackboard_keys)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the blackboard keys a node's key selector accepts, matching the editor's key dropdown: the tree blackboard's own keys (and its parents') filtered by the selector's allowed types (an empty filter accepts any key). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, selectorPropertyName
- `gameplay(epic_get_blackboard)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic aimodule_toolset.toolsets.behavior_tree.BehaviorTreeTools] Returns the blackboard asset for this behavior tree. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: behavior_tree
- `gameplay(epic_get_blackboard__behavior_tree_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the tree's blackboard asset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: behaviorTree
- `gameplay(epic_get_blackboard_key_names)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the blackboard's own key names, not including parent-chain keys. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blackboard
- `gameplay(epic_get_body_mass_scale)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Returns the mass-scale multiplier for the given body. Params: physicsAsset, boneName
- `gameplay(epic_get_body_names)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Returns the bone name for each rigid body in a physics asset. Params: physicsAsset
- `gameplay(epic_get_body_physics_mode)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Returns the physics simulation mode for the given body. Params: physicsAsset, boneName
- `gameplay(epic_get_body_shapes)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Returns all collision shapes assigned to a body. Params: physicsAsset, boneName
- `gameplay(epic_get_child_graph_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the child graph nodes of a composite (or root) parent, in sibling order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: parent
- `gameplay(epic_get_children)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic aimodule_toolset.toolsets.behavior_tree.BehaviorTreeTools] Returns direct child nodes of a composite node. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: composite
- `gameplay(epic_get_condition_description)` - [Epic WorldConditionsToolset.WorldConditionTools] Returns a human-readable description of a single world condition. The condition must be passed as an FInstancedStruct containing an FWorldConditionBase-derived struct. Params: condition
- `gameplay(epic_get_constraints)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Returns all constraints in the physics asset with their current angular limits. Params: physicsAsset
- `gameplay(epic_get_decorator_graph_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the decorator subnode graph nodes attached to a graph node. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: owner
- `gameplay(epic_get_generator)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Returns the generator instance wrapped by an option. This is the object that holds the generator's settings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: option
- `gameplay(epic_get_nav_mesh_build_status)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Reports whether navmesh building is up to date and what rebuild work is pending or suspended. Addressing a navmesh narrows the report to it and adds tile detail (Recast only); null NavData with empty AgentName sweeps every navdata without tile detail. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_get_navigation_system_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Reports the state of a world's navigation system. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world, bIncludeNonDefaultProperties
- `gameplay(epic_get_node_depth)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic aimodule_toolset.toolsets.behavior_tree.BehaviorTreeTools] Returns the tree depth of a node by its list_nodes index. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: behavior_tree, node_index
- `gameplay(epic_get_node_depths)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic aimodule_toolset.toolsets.behavior_tree.BehaviorTreeTools] Returns tree depths for all nodes, matching list_nodes order. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: behavior_tree
- `gameplay(epic_get_node_instance)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the behavior node instance that a graph node wraps. This is the object that holds the node's settings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node
- `gameplay(epic_get_option_graph_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Returns the query's option graph nodes, in order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: query
- `gameplay(epic_get_option_instance)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Returns the option instance a graph node wraps. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: option
- `gameplay(epic_get_query_description)` - [Epic WorldConditionsToolset.WorldConditionTools] Returns a human-readable description of a world condition query. Params: queryDefinition
- `gameplay(epic_get_reachable_area)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Computes the navmesh region reachable from an origin within a path-cost budget, connectivity respected. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: origin, maxCost, maxPolyCenters, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_get_root_decorators)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic aimodule_toolset.toolsets.behavior_tree.BehaviorTreeTools] Returns root-level decorators on this tree. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: behavior_tree
- `gameplay(epic_get_root_graph_node)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the tree's root graph node. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: behaviorTree
- `gameplay(epic_get_root_graph_node__environment_query_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Returns the query's root graph node. Options connect to it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: query
- `gameplay(epic_get_service_graph_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the service subnode graph nodes attached to a graph node. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: owner
- `gameplay(epic_get_subtree)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic aimodule_toolset.toolsets.behavior_tree.BehaviorTreeTools] Returns the sub-BT asset referenced by a RunBehavior task. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: node
- `gameplay(epic_get_subtree__behavior_tree_toolset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Returns the BehaviorTree run by a RunBehavior task node instance. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node
- `gameplay(epic_get_tag_info)` - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Returns detailed information about a specific gameplay tag. Params: tagName
- `gameplay(epic_get_test_graph_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Returns the test subnode graph nodes attached to an option, in order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: option
- `gameplay(epic_get_test_instance)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Returns the test instance a graph node wraps. This is the object that holds the test's settings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: test
- `gameplay(epic_get_tile_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Describes the navmesh tile containing a location. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: location, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_is_input_applied)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic InputToolset.InputToolset] Reports whether each entry is currently active. Only Key reads player input. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: entries
- `gameplay(epic_list_nav_agent_actors)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists pawns as navigation agents, each with the navmesh it resolves to. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: classFilter, center, radius, maxResults, world
- `gameplay(epic_list_nav_areas)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists the concrete nav area classes in the process (engine and game); abstract, deprecated, and superseded classes are skipped. Meta areas (per-agent area pickers) are flagged. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world
- `gameplay(epic_list_nav_bounds_volumes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists every registered navigation bounds region and its supported agents. Navmesh only generates inside these bounds; a missing volume is the most common "no navmesh here". Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world
- `gameplay(epic_list_nav_data)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists the navigation data actors registered with the world's navigation system. Non-Recast navdata are included and flagged. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world
- `gameplay(epic_list_nav_invokers)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Reports the navigation invokers driving tile generation. On invoker-driven maps navmesh only exists near a registered invoker. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world
- `gameplay(epic_list_nav_links)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists nav links from three sources: nav link proxy actors, custom link components, and links baked into the navmesh itself, including whether an in-mesh link is broken. COST NOTE: the in-mesh source pays a full debug-geometry gather per visited tile, bounded by a tile budget; pass a Radius or Source=InMeshLinks to spend that budget where it matters. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: center, radius, source, maxResults, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_list_nav_modifiers)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists the nav modifier volumes and components (area carvers) shaping the navmesh. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: center, radius, maxResults, bIncludeNonDefaultProperties, world
- `gameplay(epic_list_nodes)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic aimodule_toolset.toolsets.behavior_tree.BehaviorTreeTools] Returns a flat list of all node UObjects in tree order. Order: root decorators, then DFS (composite, services, per-child decorators, child node). Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: behavior_tree
- `gameplay(epic_list_supported_agents)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists the agent configs a world supports and the live navigation data each one resolves to. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: world
- `gameplay(epic_list_tag_source_roots)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Returns the directories a new tag source can be created in: the project's Config/Tags directory and each registered plugin's. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `gameplay(epic_list_tag_sources)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Returns every gameplay tag source, sorted by name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `gameplay(epic_list_tags)` - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Returns gameplay tags registered in the project. Params: parentTag
- `gameplay(epic_list_tags_in_source)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Returns all gameplay tags defined in a specific tag source. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: tagSource
- `gameplay(epic_list_worlds)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Lists the worlds a navmesh tool call can target (editor, PIE server, PIE clients, game). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `gameplay(epic_move_graph_node)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Reorders a child among its siblings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: parent, child, newIndex
- `gameplay(epic_move_option)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Reorders an option among its siblings. Option order is the tie-break order the query runs them in. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: query, option, newIndex
- `gameplay(epic_move_subnode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Reorders an existing decorator or service among its siblings without recreating it, so its configured settings are kept. Decorator order affects evaluation and abort priority. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: owner, subnode, index
- `gameplay(epic_move_tags)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Moves a set of gameplay tags from their current INI source to a different one. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: tagsToMove, targetTagSource
- `gameplay(epic_move_test)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Reorders a test among its siblings without recreating it, so its settings are kept. Test order is the order they run in (and, with auto-sort off, affects performance). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: option, test, index
- `gameplay(epic_project_point_to_nav_mesh)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Projects a point onto navigation data: does walkable space exist here, and where exactly. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: point, horizontalExtent, verticalExtent, bAllAgents, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_query_nav_octree)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Queries the navigation octree for the elements contributing geometry or modifiers around a location: the authoritative answer to "what carved or built the navmesh here". Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: center, radius, maxResults, world
- `gameplay(epic_raycast_nav_mesh)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic NavMeshToolset.NavMeshToolset] Casts a ray across the navmesh surface and reports where walkable space ends. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: start, end, navData, agentName, world
- `gameplay(epic_remove_blackboard_key)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Removes a key from the blackboard. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: blackboard, keyName
- `gameplay(epic_remove_body)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Removes the body for the given bone along with any constraints that reference it. Raises a script error if PhysicsAsset is null or no body exists for BoneName. Params: physicsAsset, boneName
- `gameplay(epic_remove_constraint)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Removes the constraint between two bodies. Params: physicsAsset, bone1Name, bone2Name
- `gameplay(epic_remove_graph_node)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Removes a composite/task graph node, with its children and subnodes. The root cannot be removed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node
- `gameplay(epic_remove_option)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Removes an option and its tests. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: option
- `gameplay(epic_remove_shape)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Removes a collision primitive from a body by name. Params: physicsAsset, boneName, shapeName
- `gameplay(epic_remove_subnode)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Removes a decorator or service subnode from its owner. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: owner, subnode
- `gameplay(epic_remove_tag)` - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Removes a gameplay tag from the project. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Params: tagName
- `gameplay(epic_remove_test)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Removes a test from its option. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: option, test
- `gameplay(epic_rename_tag)` - [Epic GameplayTagsToolset.GameplayTagsToolset] Renames a gameplay tag, updating all references in the project. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Params: oldTagName, newTagName
- `gameplay(epic_set_blackboard)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Sets the tree's blackboard asset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: behaviorTree, blackboard
- `gameplay(epic_set_body_mass_scale)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Sets the mass-scale multiplier for the given body. Params: physicsAsset, boneName, massScale
- `gameplay(epic_set_body_physics_mode)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Sets the physics simulation mode for the given body. Params: physicsAsset, boneName, mode
- `gameplay(epic_set_box)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Adds or replaces a box collision primitive on a body. If any shape with the given name already exists on the body it is removed first. Params: physicsAsset, boneName, shapeName, center, rotation, extentX, extentY, extentZ
- `gameplay(epic_set_capsule)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Adds or replaces a capsule collision primitive on a body. If any shape with the given name already exists on the body it is removed first. The capsule's long axis is its local Z after applying Rotation. Params: physicsAsset, boneName, shapeName, center, rotation, radius, length
- `gameplay(epic_set_constraint_limits)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Updates the angular limits for an existing constraint. Params: physicsAsset, info
- `gameplay(epic_set_node_blackboard_key)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.BehaviorTreeToolset] Sets which blackboard key a node's key selector points at, by name, resolving the binding against the tree's blackboard. Prefer this over writing the selector's fields directly, since the resolved key id is a transient cache derived from the name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, selectorPropertyName, keyName
- `gameplay(epic_set_sphere)` - [Epic PhysicsToolsets.PhysicsAssetToolset] Adds or replaces a sphere collision primitive on a body. If any shape with the given name already exists on the body it is removed first. Params: physicsAsset, boneName, shapeName, center, radius
- `gameplay(epic_set_test_enabled)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AIModuleToolset.EnvironmentQueryToolset] Enables or disables a test. A disabled test stays on the option but is excluded from the built query. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: test, bEnabled

## gas

Wraps 15 official tools.

- `gas(epic_add_cue_tag)` - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Adds a new gameplay cue tag to the project. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Params: cueTag, comment?
- `gas(epic_create_cue_notify_asset)` - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Creates a new GameplayCueNotify Blueprint asset at the specified content browser location. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Params: cueTag, packagePath, assetName, bIsActor
- `gas(epic_create_gameplay_ability)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayAbilityToolset] Creates a new Gameplay Ability Blueprint asset, the same as the Content Browser's Gameplay Ability Blueprint factory. Unlike a plain Blueprint with a GameplayAbility parent, its root ability has a "Gameplay Ability Graph" in place of the default "EventGraph". This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: folderPath, assetName, parentClass
- `gas(epic_execute_cue_on_selected_actor)` - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Executes a gameplay cue non-replicated on the currently selected actor in the editor. Useful for previewing cue effects without network replication. Requires a PIE session or a configured GameplayCueManager to produce visible results. Params: cueTag, normalizedMagnitude, location, normal
- `gas(epic_find_attribute_set_classes)` - [Epic GASToolsets.AttributeSetToolset] Returns all AttributeSet subclasses found in the project, including their attributes. Covers both native C++ subclasses and Blueprint subclasses discovered via the asset registry. Params: none
- `gas(epic_find_cue_notify_assets)` *(UE 5.8)* - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Returns all GameplayCueNotify assets found in the project via the asset registry. Available on UE 5.8; absent from UE 5.9. Params: parentTag
- `gas(epic_find_cue_tags_without_notifies)` - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Returns gameplay cue tags that have no GameplayCueNotify of their own. Params: none
- `gas(epic_get_active_effects)` - [Epic GASToolsets.AbilitySystemInspectorToolset] Returns all gameplay effects currently active on the actor's AbilitySystemComponent. Params: actor
- `gas(epic_get_active_tags)` - [Epic GASToolsets.AbilitySystemInspectorToolset] Returns the gameplay tags currently owned by the actor's AbilitySystemComponent (includes loose tags, effect-granted tags, etc.). Params: actor
- `gas(epic_get_attribute_values)` - [Epic GASToolsets.AbilitySystemInspectorToolset] Returns the current base and modified values of all gameplay attributes on the actor's AbilitySystemComponent. Params: actor
- `gas(epic_get_cue_info)` - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Returns information about a specific gameplay cue, including its notify asset. Only notifies in the project's gameplay cue notify paths are known. Params: cueTag
- `gas(epic_get_granted_abilities)` - [Epic GASToolsets.AbilitySystemInspectorToolset] Returns all abilities granted to the actor's AbilitySystemComponent. Params: actor
- `gas(epic_list_attributes)` - [Epic GASToolsets.AttributeSetToolset] Returns the gameplay attributes defined on a specific AttributeSet class. Params: className
- `gas(epic_list_cues)` - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Returns gameplay cue tags registered in the project. Params: parentTag
- `gas(epic_remove_cue_tag)` - [Epic GASToolsets.GameplayCueToolset] Removes a gameplay cue tag from the project. This should ONLY be called after getting explicit direction or permission from the user. Params: cueTag

- `statetree(epic_get_all_states)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic state_tree_toolset.state_tree.StateTreeToolset] Returns every state in the tree (root + descendants), in hierarchy order. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: state_tree

- `statetree(epic_get_node_instance_object)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic state_tree_toolset.state_tree.StateTreeToolset] Returns the node's Instance UObject (per-state-tree-instance data, UObject-typed). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: state_tree, node_id
- `statetree(epic_get_node_template_data)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic state_tree_toolset.state_tree.StateTreeToolset] Returns the node's Template struct (the type-defining, shared data). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: state_tree, node_id

## plugins

Wraps 40 official tools.

- `plugins(epic_add_additional_plugin_directory)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Adds a directory the project searches for plugins, saves the .uproject file, and rescans so plugins in the directory are discovered without a restart. No-ops if the directory is already listed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: directory
- `plugins(epic_add_disallowed_plugin)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Adds an entry to a plugin's DisallowedPlugins array in its .uplugin file, or updates its comment if the entry already exists. The disallowed plugin does not need to be currently discovered. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName, disallowedPluginName, comment
- `plugins(epic_add_plugin_dependency)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Adds a dependency entry to a plugin's Plugins array in its .uplugin file. No-ops if a dependency with that name already exists with matching settings. The dependency plugin does not need to be currently discovered. Params: pluginName, dependencyName, bOptional, bEnabled, bActivate?
- `plugins(epic_create_plugin)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Creates a new plugin from a template and loads it into the editor. Use GetPluginTemplateDescriptions to obtain a valid TemplateInfo. Params: pluginName, relativePluginLocation, bPlaceInEngine, templateInfo, description, pluginVersePath?, pythonScriptAdditionalArgs?
- `plugins(epic_get_additional_plugin_directories)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Lists the extra directories the project searches for plugins (the .uproject's AdditionalPluginDirectories). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_get_capabilities)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AgentPluginToolset.AgentPluginToolset] Returns details for specific capabilities. Discovery fails after 60 seconds. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: capabilityIds
- `plugins(epic_get_configured_additional_metadata_keys)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Lists the additional metadata keys the GameFeatures subsystem is configured to read from each .uplugin (UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings::AdditionalPluginMetadataKeys), sorted. These are the only keys SetGameFeaturePluginMetadataValue and RemoveGameFeaturePluginMetadataValue will accept. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_get_disallowed_plugins)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Returns the entries from a plugin's DisallowedPlugins array in its .uplugin file. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_get_game_feature_plugin_details)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Reads the editable metadata on a Game Feature Plugin's .uplugin descriptor. Returns the BuiltInInitialFeatureState and the configured additional metadata keys (UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings::AdditionalPluginMetadataKeys) with their values rendered as JSON literals. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_get_game_feature_state)` - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Gets the current state of a Game Feature Plugin. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_get_plugin_dependencies)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Returns the dependency entries from a plugin's Plugins array in its .uplugin file. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_get_plugin_dependents)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Returns the names of all discovered plugins that declare a dependency on the given plugin. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_get_plugin_descriptor)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Gets the editable descriptor fields for a discovered plugin. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_get_plugin_for_asset)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Returns the name of the enabled plugin whose content mount point contains the given asset path. Accepts full asset paths or mount point prefixes (e.g. /PluginName/ or /Game/Path/To/Asset). Params: assetPath
- `plugins(epic_get_plugin_info)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Gets metadata for a discovered plugin, including description, version, base directory, content directory, descriptor path, and mounted asset path. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_get_plugin_template_descriptions)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Returns the list of available plugin templates. Pass one of the results to CreatePlugin to create a new plugin from that template. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_is_enabled)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Checks whether a discovered plugin is currently enabled. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_is_game_feature_active)` - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Checks whether a Game Feature Plugin is active. Raises an error if the subsystem is unavailable or the plugin is not found. Use GetGameFeatureState if you need the current state when the plugin is not active. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_is_game_feature_plugin)` - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Return whether or not a plugin is a Game Feature Plugin. Will error if no plugin of this name can be found by the Plugin Manager. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_is_plugin_creation_allowed)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Checks whether the editor settings permit plugin creation from the plugin browser. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_is_plugin_modification_allowed)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Checks whether the editor settings permit modifying plugins from the plugin browser. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_list_capabilities)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic AgentPluginToolset.AgentPluginToolset] Lists available capabilities from the current agent plugin directories and working directory. Discovery fails after 60 seconds. Warnings report rejected sources and discovery limits. Treat capability IDs as opaque values and pass them unchanged to GetCapabilities. Use an agent's Reference, not its ID, when a skill asks for a 'subagent_type'. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_list_discovered_game_feature_plugins)` - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Lists all discovered Game Feature Plugins sorted by name. This includes disabled plugins and plugins the Game Features system has not registered, which the other tools cannot resolve. Use the Plugins toolset to do general plugin enable/disable tasks. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_list_discovered_plugins)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Lists the names of all discovered plugins (enabled and disabled), sorted alphabetically. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_list_enabled_game_feature_plugins)` - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Lists enabled Game Feature Plugins that the Game Features system has registered, sorted by name. The project's policy can keep an enabled plugin from registering; ListDiscoveredGameFeaturePlugins still includes those. Use the Plugins toolset to do general plugin enable/disable tasks. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_list_enabled_plugins)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Lists the names of all enabled plugins, sorted alphabetically. Params: none
- `plugins(epic_remove_additional_plugin_directory)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Removes a directory the project searches for plugins, saves the .uproject file, and rescans. Plugins from the directory that are enabled stay loaded until the editor restarts. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: directory
- `plugins(epic_remove_disallowed_plugin)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Removes an entry from a plugin's DisallowedPlugins array in its .uplugin file. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName, disallowedPluginName
- `plugins(epic_remove_game_feature_plugin_metadata_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Removes one of the configured additional metadata keys from the GFP's .uplugin. After removal the key round-trips through AdditionalMetadata as an empty string -- that is the engine parser's default for missing keys, surfaced honestly. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName, key
- `plugins(epic_remove_plugin_dependency)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Removes a dependency entry from a plugin's Plugins array in its .uplugin file. Params: pluginName, dependencyName
- `plugins(epic_request_activate_game_feature)` - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Requests activation of a Game Feature Plugin, loading it first if it is not already loaded. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_request_deactivate_game_feature)` - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Requests deactivation of a Game Feature Plugin, which leaves it loaded. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_request_load_game_feature)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Requests load of a Game Feature Plugin without activating it. Use RequestActivateGameFeature instead to make the plugin's features live. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName
- `plugins(epic_request_unload_game_feature)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Requests unload of a Game Feature Plugin, taking it out of the Loaded state. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName, bKeepRegistered?
- `plugins(epic_set_game_feature_plugin_built_in_auto_state)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Sets the BuiltInInitialFeatureState on the GFP's .uplugin descriptor and writes the descriptor back to disk. EPluginToolsetGFPAutoState::Invalid is not accepted. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName, newState
- `plugins(epic_set_game_feature_plugin_metadata_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic GameFeaturesToolset.GameFeaturesToolset] Writes a value for one of the configured additional metadata keys to the GFP's .uplugin. JsonValueLiteral must be a valid JSON literal (e.g. true, 42, "hello", {"Major":1}); booleans and numbers are stored as native JSON types rather than strings. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName, key, jsonValueLiteral
- `plugins(epic_set_plugin_enabled)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Enables or disables a plugin in the project and saves the .uproject file, checking it out via source control if enabled. Params: pluginName, bEnabled
- `plugins(epic_update_plugin_dependency)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Changes the settings of an existing dependency entry in a plugin's Plugins array in its .uplugin file. Anything not provided keeps its current value, as do fields the plugin editor doesn't show, such as platform lists. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: pluginName, dependencyName, bOptional?, bEnabled?, bActivate?
- `plugins(epic_update_plugin_descriptor)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Updates a plugin's descriptor fields and writes them to its .uplugin file. Checks out the file via source control if source control is enabled. No-ops if the serialized descriptor is unchanged (file is not touched). Params: pluginName, newDescriptor
- `plugins(epic_validate_new_plugin_name_and_location)` - [Epic PluginToolset.PluginToolset] Validates that PluginName and RelativePluginLocation are acceptable for a new plugin. Params: pluginName, relativePluginLocation, bPlaceInEngine, templateInfo

## dataflow

Wraps 70 official tools.

- `dataflow(epic_add_comment)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] AddComment Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, comment, position, size, color
- `dataflow(epic_add_comment_box)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Adds a comment box around the given nodes. Params: graph, nodes, comment?, color?
- `dataflow(epic_add_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Adds a sub-graph. Add its "FDataflowSubGraphInputNode" and "FDataflowSubGraphOutputNode" nodes with AddNode, its signature with AddGraphBinding, and run it with a "SubGraph/<Name>" node. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphName, bIsForEach
- `dataflow(epic_add_graph_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Adds a binding to the signature of a sub-graph: an output pin of its input node (Direction "Input") or an input pin of its output node (Direction "Output"). The nodes running the sub-graph get the matching pin. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, direction, bindingName, dataType
- `dataflow(epic_add_link)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Links an output pin to an input pin, replacing the input pin's existing link if any, as the graph editor does: a pin of any type takes the type of the pin it is linked to, and a conversion node is added when the types need one. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, outputPinPath, inputPinPath
- `dataflow(epic_add_link_with_new_pin)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Adds a pin to a node that supports it (e.g. the input or output node of a sub-graph) for the type of SourcePinPath, and links the two, as dropping a link on the node in the graph editor does. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, sourcePinPath, targetNodePath
- `dataflow(epic_add_node)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Adds a node of the given type to the Dataflow graph. Params: graph, typeName, nodeName, jsonParams?, x?, y?
- `dataflow(epic_add_node__dataflow_scripting_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Adds a node. Use AddComment for comments. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, nodeType, nodeName, position
- `dataflow(epic_add_node_pins)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Adds pins to a node whose FDataflowNodeInfo::bCanAddPins is true, as its "Add Pin" menu does. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, count
- `dataflow(epic_add_variable)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Adds a new variable to the Dataflow graph. Supported type strings: Primitives : "Bool", "Int32", "Int64", "Float", "Double", "Name", "String" Structs : UScriptStruct name with or without the "F" prefix e.g. "Vector", "FVector", "Transform", "FTransform", "Rotator", "LinearColor" Objects : "Object:<ClassName>" where ClassName is with or without the "U"/"A" prefix e.g. "Object:StaticMesh", "Object:USkeletalMesh" Dataflow types, as pins show them: e.g. "float", "FVector", "TObjectPtr<UStaticMesh>", "TArray<int32>" Params: graph, name, type
- `dataflow(epic_add_variable__dataflow_scripting_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] AddVariable Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, variableName, dataType, defaultValue
- `dataflow(epic_assign_dataflow_template)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Assigns a Dataflow template to an existing Dataflow-compatible asset by duplicating the template graph and embedding it. Replaces any existing embedded graph. Params: asset, templateId
- `dataflow(epic_clear_preview_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Makes the asset use the preview Blueprint's default value of a variable again. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset, variableName
- `dataflow(epic_clear_variable_override)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Makes the asset use the graph's value of a variable again. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset, variableName
- `dataflow(epic_connect_node_pins)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Connects an output pin of one node to an input pin of another. Params: fromNode, fromPin, toNode, toPin
- `dataflow(epic_create_asset_with_dataflow)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Creates an asset generated by an embedded Dataflow graph (e.g. a cloth asset or a geometry collection). Get the graph with GetDataflowFromAsset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetClass, assetPath, templatePath
- `dataflow(epic_create_dataflow)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Creates a Dataflow asset with an empty main graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `dataflow(epic_create_dataflow_compatible_asset)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Creates a new Dataflow-compatible asset (e.g. ChaosClothAsset, GeometryCollection, FleshAsset, GroomAsset) with an empty embedded Dataflow graph. Params: className, name, path?
- `dataflow(epic_create_dataflow_compatible_asset_from_template)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Creates a new Dataflow-compatible asset and initialises its embedded Dataflow graph from a registered template in one step. Params: className, name, path, templateId
- `dataflow(epic_create_graph)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Creates a new saved Dataflow graph asset. Params: name, path
- `dataflow(epic_disconnect_node_pins)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Removes the connection between two node pins. Params: fromNode, fromPin, toNode, toPin
- `dataflow(epic_execute_node_function)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Runs a node function, as if its details panel button was pressed. Undoable. On a standalone graph, the function cannot see the per-instance overrides of the assets using it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: node, functionName
- `dataflow(epic_execute_node_function__dataflow_scripting_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Runs a function of a node, as pressing its button in the details panel does (e.g. "Import"). On a standalone graph, the function cannot see the per-asset overrides of the assets using it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, functionName
- `dataflow(epic_get_dataflow_from_asset)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Returns the Dataflow graph an asset is generated from, embedded or referenced (e.g. the graph of a cloth asset). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset
- `dataflow(epic_get_dataflow_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Returns the graphs and variables of a Dataflow asset. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow
- `dataflow(epic_get_dataflow_instance_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Returns what an asset generated by a Dataflow graph keeps besides the graph: its terminal node and the values it overrides. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset
- `dataflow(epic_get_graph_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Graphs Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath
- `dataflow(epic_get_graph_structure)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Returns the complete structure of a Dataflow graph including all nodes and connections. Params: graph
- `dataflow(epic_get_node_info)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Returns information about a node as a JSON object (name, type, position, pins). Params: node
- `dataflow(epic_get_node_info__dataflow_scripting_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Nodes Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath
- `dataflow(epic_get_node_type_info)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Returns a registered node type with the pins and editable properties of a new node of that type. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: nodeType
- `dataflow(epic_get_node_type_schema)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Returns the schema for a Dataflow node type including its input/output pins and editable UPROPERTY parameters. Params: typeName
- `dataflow(epic_list_dataflow_asset_classes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Lists the asset classes whose assets can have a Dataflow graph: those generated by one (e.g. a cloth asset), and those it can be attached to (e.g. a skeletal mesh). Whichever it is, the other functions treat their assets alike. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `dataflow(epic_list_dataflow_compatible_asset_types)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Returns a JSON list of every UClass that can host an embedded Dataflow graph (i.e. implements IDataflowInstanceInterface). Each entry has "className", "displayName", and "modulePath" fields. Use the "className" value as input to CreateDataflowCompatibleAsset or ListDataflowTemplatesForAssetClass. Params: none
- `dataflow(epic_list_dataflow_templates)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Lists the templates registered for an asset class, including those registered for its parent classes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetClass
- `dataflow(epic_list_dataflow_templates_for_asset_class)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Returns a JSON list of Dataflow templates registered for the given asset class. Templates registered for parent classes are included (class hierarchy walk). Params: className, bIncludeBlank?
- `dataflow(epic_list_node_types)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Returns a JSON list of all registered Dataflow node types. Params: bCommonOnly?
- `dataflow(epic_list_node_types__dataflow_scripting_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Lists the node types that can be added, including the nodes running the sub-graphs and reading the variables of Dataflow. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, filter
- `dataflow(epic_list_variables)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Returns all variables defined on the Dataflow graph as a JSON array. Each entry contains "name", "type", and "value" fields. Params: graph
- `dataflow(epic_remove_all_links)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] RemoveAllLinks Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, pinPath
- `dataflow(epic_remove_comment_box)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Removes a comment box node from the graph. Params: graph, commentId
- `dataflow(epic_remove_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Removes a sub-graph and its nodes. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath
- `dataflow(epic_remove_graph_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Removes a binding from the signature of a sub-graph, with its pins and links. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, bindingName
- `dataflow(epic_remove_link)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Remove Link Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, outputPinPath, inputPinPath
- `dataflow(epic_remove_node)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Removes a node and all its connections from the Dataflow graph. Params: graph, node
- `dataflow(epic_remove_node_pins)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Removes the last added pins of a node whose FDataflowNodeInfo::bCanRemovePins is true, with their links. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, count
- `dataflow(epic_remove_nodes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Removes nodes and comments, and their links, as one undo step. Nothing is removed if any path is invalid. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePaths
- `dataflow(epic_remove_variable)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Removes a variable from the Dataflow graph. Params: graph, name
- `dataflow(epic_remove_variable__dataflow_scripting_subsystem)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Remove Variable Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, variableName
- `dataflow(epic_rename_graph)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Renames a sub-graph, and the nodes running it. The main graph has no name. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, newName
- `dataflow(epic_rename_graph_binding)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Renames a binding of the signature of a sub-graph, as its details panel does: its pins keep their links, also on the nodes running the sub-graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, bindingName, newName
- `dataflow(epic_rename_node)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Renames a node, as editing its title in the graph editor does. What refers to the node by name keeps the old name (e.g. the terminal of an asset, see SetDataflowTerminal). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, newName
- `dataflow(epic_rename_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Renames a variable, and updates the nodes reading it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, variableName, newName
- `dataflow(epic_reposition_node)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Moves a node to a new position in the graph editor. Params: node, x, y
- `dataflow(epic_set_comment)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Set Comment Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, comment
- `dataflow(epic_set_comment_style)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Sets how a comment shows, as its details panel does. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, color, fontSize, bShowBubble, bColorBubble, bMovesNodes
- `dataflow(epic_set_dataflow_from_template)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Gives an asset a new embedded Dataflow graph, copied from a template, replacing the graph it had. An asset of a class the graph is attached to (e.g. a skeletal mesh) gets the attachment holding it. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset, templatePath
- `dataflow(epic_set_dataflow_terminal)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] SetDataflowTerminal Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset, nodeName
- `dataflow(epic_set_graph_binding_data_type)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Changes the type of a binding of the signature of a sub-graph, as its details panel does: the links of its pins that the new type cannot keep are removed. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, bindingName, dataType
- `dataflow(epic_set_graph_for_each)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] SetGraphForEach Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, graphPath, bIsForEach
- `dataflow(epic_set_node_position)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] SetNodePosition Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, position
- `dataflow(epic_set_node_properties)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Sets properties of a node, as listed in FDataflowNodeInfo::Properties, as one undo step. The node updates as when the details panel edits them, which can change its pins. Nothing is set if any property cannot be. An instanced object of another asset (e.g. read from another graph) is duplicated into Dataflow. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, nodePath, properties
- `dataflow(epic_set_pin_type)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Sets the type of an unlinked output pin of any type, as its "Convert to" menu does (e.g. the Result of a math node, float by default). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, pinPath, dataType
- `dataflow(epic_set_preview_blueprint)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Sets the Blueprint actor class the Dataflow editor previews the graph with (e.g. a character wearing a cloth asset). The assets using the graph set the values of its variables with SetPreviewVariable. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, blueprintClass
- `dataflow(epic_set_preview_variable)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Sets, for an asset, a variable of the Blueprint its graph is previewed with (e.g. "SkeletalMesh", the mesh a cloth asset is previewed on). Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset, variableName, value
- `dataflow(epic_set_variable)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Sets the value of an existing variable using its serialized string representation. The format depends on the variable's type (e.g., "3.14" for float, "true" for bool, "42" for int, "MyName" for FName). Params: graph, name, value
- `dataflow(epic_set_variable_data_type)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Set Variable Data Type Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, variableName, dataType
- `dataflow(epic_set_variable_default_value)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Set Variable Default Value Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: dataflow, variableName, defaultValue
- `dataflow(epic_set_variable_override)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic DataflowEditor.DataflowScriptingSubsystem] Gives a graph variable a value of its own for an asset generated by the graph. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: asset, variableName, value
- `dataflow(epic_update_node)` - [Epic DataflowAgent.DataflowAgentToolset] Updates an existing node's editable properties via JSON. Params: node, jsonParams

## conversation

Wraps 7 official tools.

- `conversation(epic_get_all_nodes)` - [Epic conversation_toolset.toolsets.conversation.ConversationTools] Returns all reachable nodes in the conversation. Use ObjectTools.get_class and get_properties on each node to inspect type and properties. Each node's GUID is available via its compiled_node_guid attribute. Params: conversation
- `conversation(epic_get_node_by_guid)` - [Epic conversation_toolset.toolsets.conversation.ConversationTools] Returns a conversation node by its GUID. Params: conversation, guid
- `conversation(epic_get_node_connections)` - [Epic conversation_toolset.toolsets.conversation.ConversationTools] Returns output connection GUIDs for a conversation node. Params: node
- `conversation(epic_get_node_guids)` - [Epic conversation_toolset.toolsets.conversation.ConversationTools] Returns GUIDs of all reachable nodes, in map iteration order. Use with get_node_by_guid to look up specific nodes. Params: conversation
- `conversation(epic_get_sub_nodes)` - [Epic conversation_toolset.toolsets.conversation.ConversationTools] Returns sub-nodes (requirements, choices) attached to a task node. Params: node
- `conversation(epic_list_entry_points)` - [Epic conversation_toolset.toolsets.conversation.ConversationTools] Returns entry points (FConversationEntryList structs). Params: conversation
- `conversation(epic_list_speakers)` - [Epic conversation_toolset.toolsets.conversation.ConversationTools] Returns speaker/participant information. Params: conversation

## source_control

Wraps 38 official tools.

- `source_control(epic_add)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Adds files to source control; they are added on the next submit. Async: await the result. The provider decides which files it accepts; a file it refuses, for example one that is already tracked, fails the call with its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_check_out)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Checks files out, so they can be edited and submitted. Async: await the result. The provider decides which files it accepts; a file it refuses, for example one that is not tracked or is checked out exclusively by another user, fails the call with its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_create_branch)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Creates a branch off the current branch. Async: await the result. Does not change local files. Providers may refuse while there are pending changes, and may push the new branch to the server. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: branchName, repository?
- `source_control(epic_create_changelist)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Creates a new pending changelist and moves the specified files into it. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPaths, description
- `source_control(epic_delete)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Marks files for delete and removes them from disk, discarding any unsaved changes in their loaded packages. Unloads the assets of the deleted files and reloads the open level when its map or external packages change, so a deleted actor of a level that stores its actors in separate files disappears from that level. Blocks until the delete and the reload complete. Game thread only. Raises when no files are given. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when a file cannot be resolved. Raises when the provider refuses a file, for example one that is not tracked; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_delete_branch)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Deletes a branch for every user. Providers that keep history may archive it instead. Async: await the result. Does not change local files. The provider decides which branches it accepts, for example it may refuse the current branch. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: branch, repository?
- `source_control(epic_download)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Downloads a revision of a file to a local path, without changing the workspace. Async: await the result. Refreshes the history of the file first. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: file, revision?, destination?
- `source_control(epic_edit_changelist_description)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Replaces the description of a pending changelist. Async: await the result. The default changelist's description cannot be edited; to give its files a description, create a changelist with CreateChangelist and move the files into it with MoveFilesToChangelist. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: changelistId, description
- `source_control(epic_get_asset_source_control_state)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Returns the source-control state for an asset. Blocks until the provider answers. Does not write to the message log. Raises when the asset does not exist or when the state cannot be read, for example when source control is disabled or unavailable. A file that is not tracked by source control is not an error: bIsSourceControlled is false. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `source_control(epic_get_changelist_files)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Lists the files in a pending changelist, including files that are not content. Raises when source control is unavailable or the changelist does not exist. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: changelistId
- `source_control(epic_get_checked_out_assets)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Returns the content paths of all assets you currently have open in source control (checked out, marked for add, or marked for delete). Non-content files (source, config, etc.) and anything that doesn't map to a mounted content root are omitted. Files marked for delete are still listed. Raises when source control is not enabled or the status refresh fails. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `source_control(epic_list_asset_revisions)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Returns the source-control revision history for an asset, newest first. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath
- `source_control(epic_list_branches)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Lists the branches of a repository. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: repository?
- `source_control(epic_list_changelists)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Lists the pending changelists, including the default changelist. Raises when source control is unavailable, the provider does not use changelists or the refresh fails. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
- `source_control(epic_list_submitted_changelists)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Lists submitted changelists, one page at a time, in the order the provider reports them, typically newest first. Async: await the result. To get the next page, pass the last identifier of a page as Revision. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: revision?, length?, repository?
- `source_control(epic_load_asset_revision)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Loads a historical revision of an asset alongside the currently loaded one. Async: await the result. Treat the loaded package as read-only. It stays in memory until garbage collection runs. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: assetPath, revision?
- `source_control(epic_merge_branch)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Merges the head of another branch into the current branch and reloads the loaded packages the merge changes, including the open level when its map or external packages change. Blocks until the merge and the reload complete. Game thread only. Previews the merge first to find the affected files. Files changed on both branches may become conflicted: resolve them before submitting. Providers may push the merge result to the server. Raises when the branch is empty. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when the repository does not exist. Raises, without merging, when a loaded package the merge changes has unsaved changes: save or revert it first. Raises when the provider fails an operation; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: branch, repository?
- `source_control(epic_move_files_to_changelist)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Moves files into an existing pending changelist. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: changelistId, files
- `source_control(epic_push_branch)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Pushes a branch to the server. Async: await the result. Does not change local files. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: branch, repository?
- `source_control(epic_query_branch)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Gets the details of a branch. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: branch?, repository?
- `source_control(epic_query_changelist_details)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Gets the details of a submitted changelist, such as its description, author and date. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: revision, repository?
- `source_control(epic_query_history)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Gets the revision history of any file, such as a Verse file, a project file or an asset, newest first. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: file
- `source_control(epic_query_provider)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Gets the status the provider reports for a repository, for example the current branch, user and workspace. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: repository?
- `source_control(epic_query_states)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Gets the current source control states of files, refreshing them from the provider first. Async: await the result. A file that is not tracked is not an error: its state reports bIsSourceControlled as false. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_resolve)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Marks conflicted mergeable files, such as text or Verse files, resolved once their conflict markers have been removed from the files on disk. Edit and save the files first. Async: await the result. Does not change the files. For files that are not mergeable, use ResolveMine or ResolveTheirs instead. The provider decides which files it accepts; a file it refuses, for example one that still has conflict markers, fails the call with its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_resolve_complete)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Completes the resolution of conflicts, once every conflicted file has been resolved with Resolve, ResolveMine or ResolveTheirs. Async: await the result. Providers may record the resolved files locally, for example by committing them to a personal branch. The provider decides when it accepts this; for example it fails while conflicts remain or when the repository is not resolving conflicts. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_resolve_mine)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Resolves conflicted files by keeping the local version, and reloads their loaded packages, including the open level when its map or external packages change. Blocks until the resolve and the reload complete. Game thread only. Raises when no files are given. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when a file cannot be resolved. Raises, without resolving, when a loaded package of the files has unsaved changes: save or revert it first. Raises when the provider refuses a file, for example one that is not conflicted; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_resolve_theirs)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Resolves conflicted files by taking the incoming version, discarding their local changes, and reloads their loaded packages, including the open level when its map or external packages change. Blocks until the resolve and the reload complete. Game thread only. Raises when no files are given. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when a file cannot be resolved. Raises, without resolving, when a loaded package of the files has unsaved changes: save or revert it first. Raises when the provider refuses a file, for example one that is not conflicted; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_restore_as_latest)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Makes the synced revision the latest revision of the current branch again, by submitting a new revision with its content. Blocks until the provider completes. Game thread only. Raises when the description is empty. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when the repository does not exist. Raises when the branch moved on since the last sync. Raises when files the restore changes are checked out by other users; the error lists them. Raises when the provider fails the operation; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: description, repository?
- `source_control(epic_restore_branch)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Restores a branch removed by DeleteBranch. Async: await the result. Does not change local files. The provider decides which branches it can restore. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: branch, repository?
- `source_control(epic_revert)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Reverts files, discarding their local changes and any unsaved changes in their loaded packages, and reloads those packages, including the open level when its map or external packages change. Blocks until the revert and the reload complete. Game thread only. Raises when no files are given. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when a file cannot be resolved. Raises when the provider refuses a file, for example one that is not checked out; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_revert_unchanged)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Releases the checkout of the given files that are checked out but not modified, so other users can check them out. Refreshes the modified state of the files first. Does not change the files on disk, so their loaded packages are not reloaded. Files that are not checked out or are modified are left alone. Blocks until the revert completes. Game thread only. Raises when no files are given. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when a file cannot be resolved. Raises when the provider fails an operation; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: files
- `source_control(epic_submit_changelist)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Submits a pending changelist after validating it, and fails with the validation errors if it does not pass. Waits for the asset registry to finish scanning first. Invokes the Submit Tool when one is available, as the Submit action in the Changelists window does; the user completes the submit there, so the changelist is not yet submitted when the result completes. Otherwise submits it directly. Async: await the result. Use this rather than submitting by any other route. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: changelistId
- `source_control(epic_submit_changes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Submits every pending change under the source control locations, without a changelist. Blocks until the submit completes. Game thread only. Refreshes the status of the source control locations, then submits every file there that can be checked in. Invokes the Submit Tool when one is available, as SubmitChangelist does; the user completes the submit there, so the changes are not yet submitted when this returns. Otherwise checks the files in directly, after virtualizing payloads when virtualization is enabled, which may append tags to the description. Does not save, sync or validate first, and does not add files that are not tracked. Raises when the description is empty. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when there is nothing to submit. Raises when the Submit Tool fails to start. Raises when the provider fails an operation; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: description
- `source_control(epic_switch_branch)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Switches to the head of another branch and reloads the loaded packages the switch changes, including the open level when its map or external packages change. Blocks until the switch and the reload complete. Game thread only. Previews the switch first to find the affected files. Raises when the branch is empty. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when the repository does not exist. Raises, without switching, when a loaded package the switch changes has unsaved changes: save or revert it first. Raises when the provider fails an operation; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: branch, repository?
- `source_control(epic_sync)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Syncs to a revision and reloads the loaded packages the sync changes, including the open level when its map or external packages change. Blocks until the sync and the reload complete. Game thread only. Previews the sync first to find the affected files. Raises when source control is unavailable. Raises when the repository does not exist. Raises, without syncing, when a loaded package the sync changes has unsaved changes: save or revert it first. Raises when the provider fails an operation; the error has its reason. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: revision?, repository?
- `source_control(epic_validate_changelist)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Validates a pending changelist the way the submit check does: the registered validators run on the changelist and on its assets. Waits for the asset registry to finish scanning first. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: changelistId
- `source_control(epic_validate_changes)` *(UE 5.9)* - [Epic SourceControl.SourceControlHelpers] Validates every pending change under the source control locations, without a changelist, the way the check-in dialog does. Collects the same files SubmitChanges submits, and runs the registered files validator on them. Waits for the asset registry to finish scanning first. Async: await the result. Available on UE 5.9; absent from UE 5.8. Params: none
